+
+// committedAtHeightLocked must be called with the application read or write
+// lock held. Holding that lock through proposal simulation prevents a Commit
+// from changing the reference snapshot during height validation.
+func (a *App) committedAtHeightLocked(height int64) (b0state.State, error) {
+	if a.closed || a.store == nil {
+		return b0state.State{}, errors.New("B0 application is not initialized")
+	}
+	state, err := a.store.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		return b0state.State{}, err
+	}
+	if state.Height() == math.MaxInt64 || height != state.Height()+1 {
+		return b0state.State{}, errors.New("unexpected B0 height")
+	}
+	return state, nil
+}
+
+func blockOverLimit(raws [][]byte) bool {
+	if len(raws) > maxBlockTxs {
+		return true
+	}
+	var size uint64
+	for _, raw := range raws {
+		if uint64(len(raw)) > maxBlockBytes-size {
+			return true
+		}
+		size += uint64(len(raw))
+	}
+	return false
 }

+	candidate, err := a.committedAtHeightLocked(req.Height)
+	if err != nil {
 		return nil, err
 	}
-	return &abci.ResponsePrepareProposal{}, nil
+	resp := &abci.ResponsePrepareProposal{}
+	if req.MaxTxBytes <= 0 {
+		return resp, nil
+	}
+	budget := uint64(req.MaxTxBytes)
+	if budget > maxBlockBytes {
+		budget = maxBlockBytes
+	}
+	var used uint64
+	for _, raw := range req.Txs {
+		if len(resp.Txs) == maxBlockTxs {
+			break
+		}
+		if uint64(len(raw)) > budget-used {
+			continue
+		}
+		next, code, err := b0state.EvaluateTrusted(candidate, raw)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		if code != tx.CodeOK {
+			continue
+		}
+		resp.Txs = append(resp.Txs, raw)
+		candidate = next
+		used += uint64(len(raw))
+	}
+	return resp, nil
 }

+	}
+	genesis, _ := fixture(t)
+	reopened, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer reopened.Close()
+	info, err := reopened.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
+	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("validated reopen: %+v %v", info, err)
+	}
+}

+package b0app
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"context"
+	"encoding/binary"
+	"fmt"
+	"sort"
+	"testing"
+
+	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
+)
+
+func BenchmarkB0CheckTxCost(b *testing.B) {
+	sender, receiver := testAddresses(b)
+	valid := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, 25, 0)
+	self := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", sender, 25, 0)
+	wrong := signedB0(b, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0)
+	zero := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, 0, 0)
+	badScalar := bytes.Clone(valid)
+	clear(badScalar[len(badScalar)-64 : len(badScalar)-32])
+	badECDSA := bytes.Clone(valid)
+	badECDSA[len(badECDSA)-80] ^= 1
+	workloads := []struct {
+		name string
+		raw  []byte
+		code uint32
+	}{
+		{"Malformed", []byte{1}, 1}, {"WrongChain", wrong, 2}, {"ZeroAmount", zero, 5},
+		{"NoncanonicalScalar", badScalar, 4}, {"InvalidECDSA", badECDSA, 4},
+		{"SelfTransfer", self, 0}, {"DistinctTransfer", valid, 0},
+	}
+	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
+		accounts := []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 1_000_000}}
+		if k > 1 {
+			accounts = append(accounts, b0state.Account{Address: receiver, Balance: 1_000_000})
+		}
+		for i := 0; len(accounts) < k; i++ {
+			var addr b0state.Address
+			binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(addr[16:], uint32(i))
+			if addr == sender || addr == receiver {
+				continue
+			}
+			accounts = append(accounts, b0state.Account{Address: addr, Balance: 1_000_000})
+		}
+		sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return bytes.Compare(accounts[i].Address[:], accounts[j].Address[:]) < 0 })
+		state, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, uint64(k)*1_000_000, accounts)
+		if err != nil {
+			b.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		a := syntheticApp(b, uint64(k)*1_000_000, state)
+		for _, work := range workloads {
+			b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/%s", k, work.name), func(b *testing.B) {
+				req := &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: work.raw}
+				response, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), req)
+				if err != nil || response.Code != work.code {
+					b.Fatalf("fixture: %+v %v", response, err)
+				}
+				b.ReportAllocs()
+				b.ResetTimer()
+				for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
+					response, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), req)
+					if err != nil || response.Code != work.code {
+						b.Fatal(response, err)
+					}
+				}
+				b.StopTimer()
+				b.ReportMetric(float64(26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k), "state-logical-bytes/op")
+			})
+		}
+		if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+			b.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+}

+package b0app
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"context"
+	"crypto/sha256"
+	"encoding/binary"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+
+	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"
+	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4"
+	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4/ecdsa"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
+)
+
+// Test-only scalar 1 is public and never used as a node or production key.
+func signedB0(t testing.TB, chain string, receiver [20]byte, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
+	return signedB0Key(t, 1, chain, receiver, amount, nonce)
+}
+
+func signedB0Key(t testing.TB, keyByte byte, chain string, receiver [20]byte, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
+	t.Helper()
+	var secret [32]byte
+	secret[31] = keyByte
+	key := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secret[:])
+	record := tx.Transaction{ChainID: chain, Receiver: receiver, Amount: amount, Nonce: nonce}
+	copy(record.Sender[:], key.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
+	digest, err := tx.SigningDigest(record)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	signature := ecdsa.Sign(key, digest[:])
+	r, s := signature.R(), signature.S()
+	r.PutBytes(&record.R)
+	s.PutBytes(&record.S)
+	wire, err := tx.Encode(record)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return wire
+}
+
+func testAddresses(t testing.TB) (b0state.Address, b0state.Address) {
+	t.Helper()
+	var secretA, secretB [32]byte
+	secretA[31], secretB[31] = 1, 2
+	a, b := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secretA[:]), secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secretB[:])
+	var ka, kb [33]byte
+	copy(ka[:], a.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
+	copy(kb[:], b.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
+	addrA, err := tx.SenderAddress(ka)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	addrB, err := tx.SenderAddress(kb)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return addrA, addrB
+}
+
+func appWithBalance(t *testing.T, chain string, balance uint64) (*App, string, b0state.Address, b0state.Address) {
+	t.Helper()
+	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
+	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: balance}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	transport, err := b0genesis.Encode(genesis, chain)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
+	a, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: chain, AppStateBytes: transport}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return a, path, sender, receiver
+}
+
+func TestB0ABCITransactionLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
+	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
+	a, path, sender, receiver := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	first := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
+	second := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 10, 1)
+	bad := bytes.Clone(first)
+	bad[0] = 0
+	for _, test := range []struct {
+		raw  []byte
+		code uint32
+		log  string
+	}{
+		{first, 0, "ok"}, {second, 6, "invalid_nonce"}, {bad, 1, "malformed"},
+	} {
+		resp, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: test.raw})
+		if err != nil || resp.Code != test.code || resp.Log != test.log || resp.Codespace != resultCodespace || resp.GasWanted != 0 || resp.GasUsed != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", resp, err)
+		}
+	}
+	proposed, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 1000, Txs: [][]byte{bad, second, first, second}})
+	if err != nil || len(proposed.Txs) != 2 || !bytes.Equal(proposed.Txs[0], first) || !bytes.Equal(proposed.Txs[1], second) {
+		t.Fatalf("PrepareProposal: %+v %v", proposed, err)
+	}
+	for _, raws := range [][][]byte{{bad}, {second}, {first, first}} {
+		resp, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: raws})
+		if err != nil || resp.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
+			t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal invalid: %+v %v", resp, err)
+		}
+	}
+	accepted, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: proposed.Txs})
+	if err != nil || accepted.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT {
+		t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal valid: %+v %v", accepted, err)
+	}
+	final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{bad, first, first, second}})
+	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != 4 {
+		t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
+	}
+	for i, want := range []uint32{1, 0, 6, 0} {
+		if final.TxResults[i].Code != want || final.TxResults[i].Codespace != resultCodespace {
+			t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock tx %d: %+v", i, final.TxResults[i])
+		}
+	}
+	before, _, err := a.Committed()
+	if err != nil || before.Height() != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("pending became committed: %+v %v", before, err)
+	}
+	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("duplicate Commit")
+	}
+	for _, check := range []struct {
+		addr           b0state.Address
+		balance, nonce uint64
+	}{
+		{sender, 65, 2}, {receiver, 35, 0},
+	} {
+		q, err := a.Query(ctx, &abci.RequestQuery{Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: check.addr[:]})
+		if err != nil || q.Code != 0 || q.Height != 1 || len(q.Value) != 17 || q.Value[0] != 1 || binary.BigEndian.Uint64(q.Value[1:9]) != check.balance || binary.BigEndian.Uint64(q.Value[9:17]) != check.nonce {
+			t.Fatalf("Query: %+v %v", q, err)
+		}
+	}
+	info, err := a.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
+	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 1 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, final.AppHash) {
+		t.Fatalf("Info: %+v %v", info, err)
+	}
+	replay, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: first, Type: abci.CheckTxType_Recheck})
+	if err != nil || replay.Code != 6 {
+		t.Fatalf("replay CheckTx: %+v %v", replay, err)
+	}
+	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	restarted, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("post-transfer restart: %v", err)
+	}
+	defer restarted.Close()
+	state, exists, err := restarted.Committed()
+	recoveredHash := state.AppHash()
+	if err != nil || !exists || state.Height() != 1 || state.TotalSupply() != 100 || !bytes.Equal(recoveredHash[:], final.AppHash) {
+		t.Fatalf("recovered state: %+v %v", state, err)
+	}
+	third := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 5, 2)
+	response, err := restarted.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{first, third}})
+	if err != nil || response.TxResults[0].Code != 6 || response.TxResults[1].Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("post-restart execution: %+v %v", response, err)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestB0ProposalBudgetAndLimitFallback(t *testing.T) {
+	a, _, _, receiver := appWithBalance(t, "c", 6145)
+	defer a.Close()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	first := signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, 0)
+	second := signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, 1)
+	if len(first) != 146 {
+		t.Fatalf("fixture width %d", len(first))
+	}
+	resp, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 146, Txs: [][]byte{bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, 195), first, second}})
+	if err != nil || len(resp.Txs) != 1 || !bytes.Equal(resp.Txs[0], first) {
+		t.Fatalf("skip oversized candidate: %+v %v", resp, err)
+	}
+	empty, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 0, Txs: [][]byte{first}})
+	if err != nil || len(empty.Txs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("zero budget: %+v %v", empty, err)
+	}
+	// Every V20 record is signed, funded, and carries a sequential nonce.
+	countBatch := make([][]byte, 6145)
+	for i := range countBatch {
+		countBatch[i] = signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, uint64(i))
+	}
+	if len(countBatch)*len(first) != 897170 {
+		t.Fatal("count fixture byte total")
+	}
+	countInitial, _, err := a.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_, countCodes, err := b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(countInitial, countBatch)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	for i, code := range countCodes {
+		if code != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("count fixture tx %d invalid: %d", i, code)
+		}
+	}
+	countDigest := sha256.New()
+	for _, wire := range countBatch {
+		_, _ = countDigest.Write(wire)
+	}
+	t.Logf("V20 count chain=c K=1 genesis_supply=6145 tx_count=%d aggregate_bytes=%d sha256_concatenated=%x first_wire=%s last_wire=%s", len(countBatch), len(countBatch)*146, countDigest.Sum(nil), hex.EncodeToString(countBatch[0]), hex.EncodeToString(countBatch[len(countBatch)-1]))
+	decision, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: countBatch})
+	if err != nil || decision.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
+		t.Fatalf("count ProcessProposal: %+v %v", decision, err)
+	}
+	final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: countBatch})
+	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(countBatch) {
+		t.Fatalf("count FinalizeBlock: %v", err)
+	}
+	for _, result := range final.TxResults {
+		if result.Code != 10 || result.Log != "block_limit" {
+			t.Fatal("count fallback result")
+		}
+	}
+	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	state, _, err := a.Committed()
+	if err != nil || state.Height() != 1 || state.TotalSupply() != 6145 || len(state.Accounts()) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("count fallback state: %+v %v", state, err)
+	}
+	wantCount, err := countInitial.AdvanceBlock()
+	if err != nil || state.AppHash() != wantCount.AppHash() {
+		t.Fatalf("count fallback AppHash: %v", err)
+	}
+	longChain := strings.Repeat("c", 50)
+	b, _, _, longReceiver := appWithBalance(t, longChain, 5378)
+	defer b.Close()
+	byteBatch := make([][]byte, 5378)
+	for i := range byteBatch {
+		byteBatch[i] = signedB0(t, longChain, longReceiver, 1, uint64(i))
+	}
+	if len(byteBatch[0]) != 195 || len(byteBatch)*195 != 1048710 {
+		t.Fatal("byte fixture size")
+	}
+	byteInitial, _, err := b.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_, byteCodes, err := b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(byteInitial, byteBatch)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	for i, code := range byteCodes {
+		if code != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("byte fixture tx %d invalid: %d", i, code)
+		}
+	}
+	byteDigest := sha256.New()
+	for _, wire := range byteBatch {
+		_, _ = byteDigest.Write(wire)
+	}
+	t.Logf("V20 bytes chain_length=50 K=1 genesis_supply=5378 tx_count=%d aggregate_bytes=%d sha256_concatenated=%x first_wire=%s last_wire=%s", len(byteBatch), len(byteBatch)*195, byteDigest.Sum(nil), hex.EncodeToString(byteBatch[0]), hex.EncodeToString(byteBatch[len(byteBatch)-1]))
+	decision, err = b.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: byteBatch})
+	if err != nil || decision.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
+		t.Fatalf("byte ProcessProposal: %+v %v", decision, err)
+	}
+	final, err = b.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: byteBatch})
+	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(byteBatch) {
+		t.Fatalf("byte FinalizeBlock: %v", err)
+	}
+	for _, result := range final.TxResults {
+		if result.Code != 10 {
+			t.Fatal("byte fallback result")
+		}
+	}
+	wantBytes, err := byteInitial.AdvanceBlock()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	pending, exists, err := b.store.Pending()
+	if err != nil || !exists || pending.AppHash() != wantBytes.AppHash() {
+		t.Fatalf("byte fallback AppHash: %v", err)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestB0QueryAndNonceBoundary(t *testing.T) {
+	a, _, _, receiver := appWithBalance(t, "pluto-b0-v1", 100)
+	defer a.Close()
+	ctx := context.Background()
+	for _, req := range []*abci.RequestQuery{{}, {Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: []byte{1}}, {Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: make([]byte, 20), Height: 1}, {Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: make([]byte, 20), Prove: true}} {
+		q, err := a.Query(ctx, req)
+		if err != nil || q.Code != 1 || q.Log != "unsupported_query" || q.Codespace != "pluto.query.v1" || len(q.Value) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("unsupported Query: %+v %v", q, err)
+		}
+	}
+	q, err := a.Query(ctx, &abci.RequestQuery{Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: receiver[:]})
+	if err != nil || q.Code != 0 || !bytes.Equal(q.Value, []byte{0}) {
+		t.Fatalf("missing account: %+v %v", q, err)
+	}
+	// Nonce overflow is exercised by the accepted state-engine fixture.
+}

+func TestB0AcceptedSingleTransactionVectors(t *testing.T) {
+	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
+	_, receiver := testAddresses(t)
+	valid := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
+	clone := func() []byte { return bytes.Clone(valid) }
+	badR := clone()
+	clear(badR[len(badR)-64 : len(badR)-32])
+	highS := clone()
+	low := new(big.Int).SetBytes(highS[len(highS)-32:])
+	new(big.Int).Sub(secp256k1.Params().N, low).FillBytes(highS[len(highS)-32:])
+	modifiedAmount := clone()
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(modifiedAmount[len(modifiedAmount)-80:len(modifiedAmount)-72], 26)
+	badKey := clone()
+	badKey[12+len(chain)] = 4
+	badMagic := clone()
+	badMagic[0] = 0
+	badVersion := clone()
+	badVersion[9] = 2
+	short := clone()[:len(valid)-1]
+	long := append(clone(), 0)
+	badLength0 := clone()
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(badLength0[10:12], 0)
+	badLength51 := clone()
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(badLength51[10:12], 51)
+	badChainByte := clone()
+	badChainByte[12] = 0xff
+	uncompressedKey := clone()
+	keyEnd := 12 + len(chain) + 33
+	uncompressedKey = append(append([]byte(nil), uncompressedKey[:keyEnd]...), append(bytes.Repeat([]byte{0}, 32), uncompressedKey[keyEnd:]...)...)
+	zeroAndBadSignature := clone()
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(zeroAndBadSignature[len(zeroAndBadSignature)-80:len(zeroAndBadSignature)-72], 0)
+	zeroAndBadSignature[len(zeroAndBadSignature)-33] ^= 1
+	badBinding := clone()
+	var bSecret [32]byte
+	bSecret[31] = 2
+	copy(badBinding[12+len(chain):12+len(chain)+33], secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(bSecret[:]).PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
+	cases := []struct {
+		id       string
+		raw      []byte
+		code     tx.Code
+		eligible bool
+	}{
+		{"V01", valid, 0, true}, {"V02", badR, 4, false}, {"V03", highS, 4, false},
+		{"V04", modifiedAmount, 4, true}, {"V05", signedB0(t, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0), 2, true},
+		{"V06", badKey, 3, true},
+		{"V07-bad-magic", badMagic, 1, false}, {"V07-version", badVersion, 1, false},
+		{"V07-short", short, 1, false}, {"V07-trailing", long, 1, false},
+		{"V07-chain-length-zero", badLength0, 1, false}, {"V07-chain-length-51", badLength51, 1, false},
+		{"V07-chain-byte", badChainByte, 1, false},
+		{"V07-65-byte-key", uncompressedKey, 1, false},
+		{"V08", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 1), 6, true},
+		{"V10", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 101, 0), 7, true},
+		{"V11", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 0, 0), 5, true},
+		{"V12", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 100, 0), 0, true},
+		{"V16-binding", badBinding, 4, true},
+		{"V16-unfunded-B", signedB0Key(t, 2, chain, receiver, 1, 0), 7, true},
+		{"V24", zeroAndBadSignature, 5, true},
+	}
+	for _, vector := range cases {
+		t.Run(vector.id, func(t *testing.T) {
+			a, _, _, _ := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
+			defer a.Close()
+			ctx := context.Background()
+			id, idErr := tx.TxID(vector.raw)
+			if (idErr == nil) != vector.eligible {
+				t.Fatalf("TxID eligibility: %v", idErr)
+			}
+			check, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: vector.raw})
+			if err != nil || check.Code != uint32(vector.code) || check.Log != resultLog(vector.code) {
+				t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", check, err)
+			}
+			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{vector.raw}})
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			wanted := abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT
+			if vector.code == 0 {
+				wanted = abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT
+			}
+			if proposal.Status != wanted {
+				t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal: %+v", proposal)
+			}
+			before, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{vector.raw}})
+			if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != 1 || final.TxResults[0].Code != uint32(vector.code) || final.TxResults[0].Log != resultLog(vector.code) {
+				t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
+			}
+			pending, exists, err := a.store.Pending()
+			if err != nil || !exists {
+				t.Fatalf("pending: %v", err)
+			}
+			expected, code, err := b0state.Evaluate(before, vector.raw)
+			if err != nil || code != vector.code {
+				t.Fatalf("reference Evaluate code=%d err=%v", code, err)
+			}
+			expected, err = expected.AdvanceBlock()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			pendingHash := pending.AppHash()
+			if pendingHash != expected.AppHash() || !bytes.Equal(final.AppHash, pendingHash[:]) {
+				t.Fatalf("pending AppHash mismatch")
+			}
+			if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			committed, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil || committed.AppHash() != expected.AppHash() {
+				t.Fatalf("committed state: %v", err)
+			}
+			hash := committed.AppHash()
+			t.Logf("%s wire_hex=%s expected_code=%d txid_eligible=%t pluto_txid=%x comet_hash=%x committed_apphash_h1=%x", vector.id, hex.EncodeToString(vector.raw), vector.code, vector.eligible, id, tx.CometHash(vector.raw), hash)
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+// Inject only a complete, integrity-valid synthetic state into a disposable
+// D-024 store. This is an ABCI contract fixture, not a real consensus node.
+func syntheticApp(t testing.TB, genesisBalance uint64, state b0state.State) *App {
+	t.Helper()
+	sender, _ := testAddresses(t)
+	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis("pluto-b0-v1", []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: genesisBalance}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
+	store, err := b0store.Create(path, genesis, "pluto-b0-v1")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	logical, err := state.LogicalBytes()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	hash := state.AppHash()
+	record := append(append([]byte("PLUTOS2!"), logical...), hash[:]...)
+	db, err := pebble.Open(path, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := db.Set([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"), record, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	a, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return a
+}
+
+func TestB0AcceptedSyntheticBoundaryVectors(t *testing.T) {
+	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
+	maxNonceState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 100, Nonce: math.MaxUint64}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	zeroBalanceState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 1, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Nonce: math.MaxUint64}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 1}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	accounts := make([]b0state.Account, 65536)
+	accounts[0] = b0state.Account{Address: sender, Balance: 1}
+	for i := 1; i < len(accounts); i++ {
+		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(accounts[i].Address[16:], uint32(i-1))
+	}
+	sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return bytes.Compare(accounts[i].Address[:], accounts[j].Address[:]) < 0 })
+	capState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 1, accounts)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		id      string
+		state   b0state.State
+		balance uint64
+		nonce   uint64
+		amount  uint64
+		want    uint32
+	}{
+		{"V17", maxNonceState, 100, math.MaxUint64, 1, 6},
+		{"V19", capState, 1, 0, 1, 9},
+		{"V25", zeroBalanceState, 1, math.MaxUint64, 1, 6},
+	} {
+		t.Run(item.id, func(t *testing.T) {
+			a := syntheticApp(t, item.balance, item.state)
+			defer a.Close()
+			raw := signedB0(t, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, item.amount, item.nonce)
+			check, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: raw})
+			if err != nil || check.Code != item.want {
+				t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", check, err)
+			}
+			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(context.Background(), &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{raw}})
+			if err != nil || proposal.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
+				t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal: %+v %v", proposal, err)
+			}
+			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(context.Background(), &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{raw}})
+			if err != nil || final.TxResults[0].Code != item.want {
+				t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
+			}
+			pending, _, err := a.store.Pending()
+			beforeAccounts, afterAccounts := item.state.Accounts(), pending.Accounts()
+			if err != nil || !slices.Equal(beforeAccounts, afterAccounts) || pending.TotalSupply() != item.state.TotalSupply() || pending.Height() != 2 {
+				t.Fatalf("state changed on failed tx: %v", err)
+			}
+			t.Logf("%s synthetic_integrity_valid_h1=%x wire_hex=%s code=%d pending_apphash_h2=%x", item.id, item.state.AppHash(), hex.EncodeToString(raw), item.want, pending.AppHash())
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestB0AcceptedOrderedVectors(t *testing.T) {
+	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
+	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
+	first := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
+	second := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 30, 1)
+	insufficient := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 101, 0)
+	self := signedB0(t, chain, sender, 25, 0)
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		id                         string
+		raws                       [][]byte
+		codes                      []uint32
+		balanceA, nonceA, balanceB uint64
+		accept                     bool
+	}{
+		{"V13", [][]byte{first, first}, []uint32{0, 6}, 75, 1, 25, false},
+		{"V14", [][]byte{first, second}, []uint32{0, 0}, 45, 2, 55, true},
+		{"V15", [][]byte{{1}, first}, []uint32{1, 0}, 75, 1, 25, false},
+		{"V21", [][]byte{insufficient, first}, []uint32{7, 0}, 75, 1, 25, false},
+		{"V22", [][]byte{self}, []uint32{0}, 100, 1, 0, true},
+	} {
+		t.Run(item.id, func(t *testing.T) {
+			a, _, _, _ := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
+			defer a.Close()
+			ctx := context.Background()
+			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: item.raws})
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			want := abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT
+			if item.accept {
+				want = abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT
+			}
+			if proposal.Status != want {
+				t.Fatalf("proposal status: %+v", proposal)
+			}
+			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: item.raws})
+			if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(item.codes) {
+				t.Fatalf("final: %+v %v", final, err)
+			}
+			for i, code := range item.codes {
+				if final.TxResults[i].Code != code {
+					t.Fatalf("tx %d code %d want %d", i, final.TxResults[i].Code, code)
+				}
+			}
+			if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			state, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			aAccount, _ := state.Account(sender)
+			bAccount, bFound := state.Account(receiver)
+			if aAccount.Balance != item.balanceA || aAccount.Nonce != item.nonceA || bAccount.Balance != item.balanceB || state.TotalSupply() != 100 {
+				t.Fatalf("accounts: A=%+v B=%+v", aAccount, bAccount)
+			}
+			if item.id == "V22" && bFound {
+				t.Fatal("self transfer created receiver")
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestB0V18GenesisSupplyOverflowAndV23CorruptRecord(t *testing.T) {
+	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
+	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
+	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
+	a, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	// Construct a canonical-looking two-allocation binary whose checked
+	// total-supply sum overflows uint64. The accepted encoder must reject it.
+	bad := make([]byte, 8+4+2+len(chain)+4+2*(20+8))
+	copy(bad[:8], "PLUTOG2!")
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(bad[8:12], 2)
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(bad[12:14], uint16(len(chain)))
+	copy(bad[14:], chain)
+	p := 14 + len(chain)
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(bad[p:p+4], 2)
+	p += 4
+	copy(bad[p:p+20], sender[:])
+	p += 20
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(bad[p:p+8], math.MaxUint64)
+	p += 8
+	copy(bad[p:p+20], receiver[:])
+	p += 20
+	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(bad[p:p+8], 1)
+	encoded, err := json.Marshal(base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bad))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: chain, AppStateBytes: encoded}); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("V18 overflowing genesis accepted")
+	}
+	if _, exists, err := a.Committed(); err != nil || exists {
+		t.Fatalf("V18 created state: exists=%t err=%v", exists, err)
+	}
+	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	store, err := b0store.Create(path, genesis, chain)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	db, err := pebble.Open(path, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	value, closer, err := db.Get([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	corrupt := bytes.Clone(value)
+	if err := closer.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	corrupt[len(corrupt)-33] ^= 1 // logical nonce byte; retain old digest
+	if err := db.Set([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"), corrupt, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if reopened, err := Open(path, chain, genesis); err == nil {
+		_ = reopened.Close()
+		t.Fatal("V23 corrupt complete record accepted")
+	}
+	t.Log("V18 InitChain rejected overflowing supply without creating a store; V23 application Open rejected modified logical state with unchanged digest")
+}

+func TestTrustedEvaluatorMatchesPhase03CReference(t *testing.T) {
+	sender, receiver, valid := baseFixture(t)
+	state, err := NewState(chain, 0, 100, []Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	wrong := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	wrong[12] = 'x'
+	zero := signed(t, 1, receiver, 0, 0)
+	noncanonical := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	for i := len(noncanonical) - 64; i < len(noncanonical)-32; i++ {
+		noncanonical[i] = 0
+	}
+	invalidECDSA := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	invalidECDSA[len(invalidECDSA)-80] ^= 1
+	for _, raw := range [][]byte{{1}, wrong, zero, noncanonical, invalidECDSA, valid,
+		signed(t, 1, sender, 25, 0), signed(t, 1, receiver, 200, 0), signed(t, 1, receiver, 25, 1)} {
+		reference, referenceCode, referenceErr := Evaluate(state, raw)
+		integrated, integratedCode, integratedErr := EvaluateTrusted(state, raw)
+		if referenceErr != nil || integratedErr != nil || referenceCode != integratedCode || reference.AppHash() != integrated.AppHash() {
+			t.Fatalf("different result for %x: reference=%d/%v integrated=%d/%v", raw, referenceCode, referenceErr, integratedCode, integratedErr)
+		}
+		a, _ := reference.LogicalBytes()
+		b, _ := integrated.LogicalBytes()
+		if !bytes.Equal(a, b) {
+			t.Fatal("different logical state")
+		}
+	}
+	if _, _, err := EvaluateTrusted(State{}, []byte{1}); err != ErrState {
+		t.Fatalf("untrusted state accepted: %v", err)
+	}
+}

+// The Phase 03C Evaluate method remains the untouched reference path. Each
+// subbenchmark uses the same immutable snapshot and test-only signed bytes.
+func benchmarkState(b *testing.B, k int, sender, receiver Address) State {
+	b.Helper()
+	accounts := []Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 1_000_000}}
+	if k > 1 {
+		accounts = append(accounts, Account{Address: receiver, Balance: 1_000_000})
+	}
+	for i := 0; len(accounts) < k; i++ {
+		var addr Address
+		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(addr[16:], uint32(i))
+		if addr == sender || addr == receiver {
+			continue
+		}
+		accounts = append(accounts, Account{Address: addr, Balance: 1_000_000})
+	}
+	sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return string(accounts[i].Address[:]) < string(accounts[j].Address[:]) })
+	s, err := NewState(chain, 0, uint64(k)*1_000_000, accounts)
+	if err != nil {
+		b.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return s
+}

+func BenchmarkB0StateCost(b *testing.B) {
+	// baseFixture uses the approved Phase 03B V01 vector; signed uses only
+	// explicit public test scalars 1 and 2.
+	sender, receiver, valid := baseFixture(b)
+	self := signed(b, 1, sender, 25, 0)
+	zero := signed(b, 1, receiver, 0, 0)
+	wrong := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	wrong[12] = 'x'
+	noncanonical := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	for i := len(noncanonical) - 64; i < len(noncanonical)-32; i++ {
+		noncanonical[i] = 0
+	}
+	invalidECDSA := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
+	invalidECDSA[len(invalidECDSA)-64-16] ^= 1 // signed amount/nonce region
+	workloads := []struct {
+		name string
+		raw  []byte
+		code tx.Code
+	}{
+		{"Malformed", []byte{1}, 1}, {"WrongChain", wrong, 2},
+		{"ZeroAmount", zero, 5}, {"NoncanonicalScalar", noncanonical, 4},
+		{"InvalidECDSA", invalidECDSA, 4}, {"SelfTransfer", self, 0},
+		{"DistinctTransfer", valid, 0},
+	}
+	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
+		state := benchmarkState(b, k, sender, receiver)
+		logicalBytes := 26 + len(chain) + 36*k
+		for _, path := range []struct {
+			name     string
+			evaluate func(State, []byte) (State, tx.Code, error)
+		}{
+			{"Reference", Evaluate}, {"Trusted", EvaluateTrusted},
+		} {
+			for _, work := range workloads {
+				b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/%s/%s", k, path.name, work.name), func(b *testing.B) {
+					_, code, err := path.evaluate(state, work.raw)
+					if err != nil || code != work.code {
+						b.Fatalf("fixture code=%d want=%d err=%v", code, work.code, err)
+					}
+					b.ReportAllocs()
+					b.ResetTimer()
+					for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
+						_, code, err := path.evaluate(state, work.raw)
+						if err != nil || code != work.code {
+							b.Fatal(code, err)
+						}
+					}
+					b.StopTimer()
+					b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
+				})
+			}
+		}
+		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/EmptyBlock", k), func(b *testing.B) {
+			b.ReportAllocs()
+			b.ResetTimer()
+			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
+				if _, err := state.AdvanceBlock(); err != nil {
+					b.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			b.StopTimer()
+			b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
+		})
+		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/SerializeAndHash", k), func(b *testing.B) {
+			b.ReportAllocs()
+			b.ResetTimer()
+			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
+				if _, err := state.LogicalBytes(); err != nil {
+					b.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			b.StopTimer()
+			b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
+		})
+	}
+}

+// EvaluateTrusted is for immutable states produced by NewState, ParseGenesis,
+// DecodeLogical, or a prior successful transition. The store verifies its
+// complete snapshot on load; this path avoids an O(K) hash for cheap-invalid
+// wire input. A malformed in-memory State is an internal error, not a tx code.
+func EvaluateTrusted(s State, raw []byte) (State, tx.Code, error) {
+	if !s.trusted {
+		return State{}, 0, ErrState
+	}
 	if code := tx.Validate(raw, s.chainID); code != tx.CodeOK {
 		return s, code, nil
 	}

 }
 
+// SimulateOrderedTrusted starts from a verified immutable snapshot and uses
+// the same transition logic as Evaluate without repeated full-state checks.
+func SimulateOrderedTrusted(s State, raws [][]byte) (State, []tx.Code, error) {
+	if !s.trusted {
+		return State{}, nil, ErrState
+	}
+	candidate := s
+	codes := make([]tx.Code, 0, len(raws))
+	for _, raw := range raws {
+		next, code, err := EvaluateTrusted(candidate, raw)
+		if err != nil {
+			return State{}, nil, err
+		}
+		candidate = next
+		codes = append(codes, code)
+	}
+	return candidate, codes, nil
+}
+
 // SimulateOrdered evaluates every byte string in supplied order against all
 // prior successes; failures leave the candidate unchanged and do not stop it.
 // It does not enforce an ABCI block envelope or advance height.

+package b0store

+import (
+	"encoding/binary"

+func benchmarkGenesis(k int) []byte {
+	allocations := make([]b0state.Allocation, k)
+	for i := range allocations {
+		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(allocations[i].Address[16:], uint32(i))
+		allocations[i].Balance = 1_000_000
+	}
+	sort.Slice(allocations, func(i, j int) bool { return string(allocations[i].Address[:]) < string(allocations[j].Address[:]) })
+	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis("pluto-b0-v1", allocations)
+	if err != nil {
+		panic(err)
+	}
+	return genesis
+}
+
+func BenchmarkB0SyncSnapshot(b *testing.B) {
+	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
+		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d", k), func(b *testing.B) {
+			dir := filepath.Join(b.TempDir(), "store")
+			store, err := Create(dir, benchmarkGenesis(k), "pluto-b0-v1")
+			if err != nil {
+				b.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			defer store.Close()
+			b.ReportAllocs()
+			b.ResetTimer()
+			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
+				b.StopTimer()
+				if _, _, err := store.PrepareTrusted(nil, false); err != nil {
+					b.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				b.StartTimer()
+				if err := store.Commit(); err != nil {
+					b.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			b.StopTimer()
+			b.ReportMetric(float64(8+26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k+32), "record-bytes/op")
+		})
+	}
+}

+// Enable explicitly for raw, independent sync latency samples. The test
+// prints every observation; no percentile is inferred from a tiny sample.
+func TestB0SyncLatencySamples(t *testing.T) {
+	if os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_SYNC_SAMPLES") != "1" {
+		t.Skip("set PLUTO_B0_SYNC_SAMPLES=1 for diagnostic samples")
+	}
+	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
+		store, err := Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "store"), benchmarkGenesis(k), "pluto-b0-v1")
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		for i := 0; i < 30; i++ {
+			if _, _, err := store.PrepareTrusted(nil, false); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			start := time.Now()
+			if err := store.Commit(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Logf("sync_sample k=%d trial=%d elapsed_ns=%d record_bytes=%d", k, i, time.Since(start).Nanoseconds(), 8+26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k+32)
+		}
+		if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}

+package b0store
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"context"
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"strings"
+	"sync/atomic"
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
+	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs"
+	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs/errorfs"
+)
+
+// walFault reaches a specific Pebble WAL filesystem call. It rejects that
+// call before it executes. This models a returned I/O error, not a power loss.
+func walFault(target errorfs.Op) (vfs.FS, *atomic.Bool, *atomic.Bool) {
+	enabled, reached := new(atomic.Bool), new(atomic.Bool)
+	fs := errorfs.Wrap(vfs.Default, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(op errorfs.Op, path string) error {
+		if enabled.Load() && op == target && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") && reached.CompareAndSwap(false, true) {
+			return errorfs.ErrInjected
+		}
+		return nil
+	}))
+	return fs, enabled, reached
+}
+
+// Pebble v1.1.5 uses File.SyncTo for WAL durability. Its bundled errorfs
+// forwards SyncTo without injection, so this wrapper targets that method.
+type walSyncFS struct {
+	vfs.FS
+	enabled *atomic.Bool
+	mark    string
+	kill    bool
+}
+type walSyncFile struct {
+	vfs.File
+	fs   *walSyncFS
+	path string
+}
+
+func (fs *walSyncFS) wrap(path string, f vfs.File, err error) (vfs.File, error) {
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	return &walSyncFile{File: f, fs: fs, path: path}, nil
+}
+func (fs *walSyncFS) Create(path string) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, err := fs.FS.Create(path)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
+}
+func (fs *walSyncFS) Open(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, err := fs.FS.Open(path, opts...)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
+}
+func (fs *walSyncFS) OpenReadWrite(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, err := fs.FS.OpenReadWrite(path, opts...)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
+}
+func (fs *walSyncFS) ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath string) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, err := fs.FS.ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath)
+	return fs.wrap(newPath, f, err)
+}
+func (f *walSyncFile) SyncTo(length int64) (bool, error) {
+	if f.inject("SyncTo", length) {
+		return false, errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.SyncTo(length)
+}
+func (f *walSyncFile) Sync() error {
+	if f.inject("Sync", 0) {
+		return errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.Sync()
+}
+func (f *walSyncFile) SyncData() error {
+	if f.inject("SyncData", 0) {
+		return errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.SyncData()
+}
+func (f *walSyncFile) inject(method string, length int64) bool {
+	if !f.fs.enabled.Load() || !strings.HasSuffix(f.path, ".log") {
+		return false
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(f.fs.mark, []byte(fmt.Sprintf("%s %s length=%d", method, f.path, length)), 0600); err != nil {
+		panic(err)
+	}
+	if f.fs.kill {
+		select {}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+
+func recoveredTransfer(t *testing.T, dir string, oldHash, newHash [32]byte, sender, receiver b0state.Address) string {
+	t.Helper()
+	s, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
+	if err != nil {
+		return fmt.Sprintf("FAIL_CLOSED:%v", err)
+	}
+	defer s.Close()
+	state, err := s.Committed()
+	if err != nil || state.Validate() != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("reopened invalid committed state: %v", err)
+	}
+	a, _ := state.Account(sender)
+	b, found := state.Account(receiver)
+	switch state.AppHash() {
+	case oldHash:
+		if state.Height() != 0 || a.Balance != 100 || a.Nonce != 0 || found {
+			t.Fatalf("old hash with incorrect effects: A=%+v B=%+v", a, b)
+		}
+		return fmt.Sprintf("OLD A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent", oldHash)
+	case newHash:
+		if state.Height() != 1 || a.Balance != 75 || a.Nonce != 1 || !found || b.Balance != 25 || b.Nonce != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("new hash with incorrect effects: A=%+v B=%+v", a, b)
+		}
+		return fmt.Sprintf("NEW A=1 hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0", newHash)
+	default:
+		t.Fatalf("reopened unexpected AppHash=%x height=%d", state.AppHash(), state.Height())
+		return ""
+	}
+}
+
+func TestM4ReturnedWALWriteAndSyncErrors(t *testing.T) {
+	genesis, wire, sender, receiver := fixture(t)
+	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_CHILD"); mode != "" {
+		op := errorfs.OpFileWrite
+		if mode == "wal-sync" {
+			op = errorfs.OpFileSync
+		}
+		fs, enabled, reached := walFault(op)
+		// Pebble treats some WAL write failures as fatal and may exit this
+		// process before DB.Set returns. The marker records the reached call.
+		fs = errorfs.Wrap(fs, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(current errorfs.Op, path string) error {
+			if enabled.Load() && current == op && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") && !reached.Load() {
+				if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), []byte(mode+" "+path), 0600); err != nil {
+					panic(err)
+				}
+			}
+			return nil
+		}))
+		var faultFS vfs.FS = fs
+		if mode == "wal-sync" {
+			faultFS = &walSyncFS{FS: vfs.Default, enabled: enabled, mark: os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK")}
+		}
+		s, err := OpenExistingWithOptions(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_DIR"), chain, Options{FS: faultFS})
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		enabled.Store(true)
+		err = s.Commit()
+		enabled.Store(false)
+		fmt.Printf("child Commit returned: %v\n", err)
+		_ = s.Close()
+		return
+	}
+	for _, tc := range []struct {
+		name string
+		op   errorfs.Op
+	}{{"wal-write", errorfs.OpFileWrite}, {"wal-sync", errorfs.OpFileSync}} {
+		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
+			created, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			old, err := created.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := created.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			s, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_, newHash, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire})
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			mark := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "reached")
+			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Second)
+			defer cancel()
+			cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestM4ReturnedWALWriteAndSyncErrors$")
+			cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M4_CHILD="+tc.name, "PLUTO_FAULT_DIR="+dir, "PLUTO_FAULT_MARK="+mark)
+			output, childErr := cmd.CombinedOutput()
+			if ctx.Err() != nil {
+				t.Fatal(ctx.Err())
+			}
+			marker, err := os.ReadFile(mark)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("target WAL %s not reached: %v child=%v output=%s", tc.name, err, childErr, output)
+			}
+			outcome := recoveredTransfer(t, dir, old.AppHash(), newHash, sender, receiver)
+			t.Logf("M4 simulated I/O %s reached at %q; child_exit=%v output=%q; reopened %s", tc.name, marker, childErr, output, outcome)
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestM11GenesisCreationFaultBoundaries(t *testing.T) {
+	genesis, _, sender, receiver := fixture(t)
+	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M11_CHILD"); mode != "" {
+		op := errorfs.OpFileWrite
+		if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
+			op = errorfs.OpFileSync
+		}
+		killMode := strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill")
+		fs, enabled, _ := walFault(op)
+		fs = errorfs.Wrap(fs, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(current errorfs.Op, path string) error {
+			if enabled.Load() && current == op && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") {
+				if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), []byte(mode+" "+path), 0600); err != nil {
+					panic(err)
+				}
+				if killMode {
+					select {}
+				}
+			}
+			return nil
+		}))
+		var faultFS vfs.FS = fs
+		if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
+			faultFS = &walSyncFS{FS: vfs.Default, enabled: enabled, mark: os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), kill: killMode}
+		}
+		_, err := createWithOptions(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_DIR"), genesis, chain, Options{FS: faultFS, BeforeGenesisSync: func() error { enabled.Store(true); return nil }})
+		fmt.Printf("child genesis returned: %v\n", err)
+		return
+	}
+	for _, mode := range []string{"before-mkdir", "after-mkdir", "before-record", "wal-write", "wal-sync", "wal-write-kill", "wal-sync-kill", "after-sync"} {
+		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
+			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
+			var reached atomic.Bool
+			opts := Options{}
+			switch mode {
+			case "before-mkdir":
+				opts.BeforeGenesisMkdir = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
+			case "after-mkdir":
+				opts.AfterGenesisMkdir = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
+			case "before-record":
+				opts.BeforeGenesisSync = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
+			case "after-sync":
+				opts.AfterGenesisSync = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
+			}
+			var createErr error
+			if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-write") || strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
+				mark := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "reached")
+				ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Second)
+				defer cancel()
+				cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestM11GenesisCreationFaultBoundaries$")
+				cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M11_CHILD="+mode, "PLUTO_FAULT_DIR="+dir, "PLUTO_FAULT_MARK="+mark)
+				var output []byte
+				var err error
+				if strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill") {
+					var buf bytes.Buffer
+					cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &buf, &buf
+					if startErr := cmd.Start(); startErr != nil {
+						t.Fatal(startErr)
+					}
+					deadline := time.Now().Add(15 * time.Second)
+					for {
+						if _, statErr := os.Stat(mark); statErr == nil {
+							break
+						}
+						if time.Now().After(deadline) {
+							_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+							_ = cmd.Wait()
+							t.Fatal("genesis WAL kill marker not reached")
+						}
+						time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
+					}
+					if killErr := cmd.Process.Kill(); killErr != nil {
+						t.Fatal(killErr)
+					}
+					err = cmd.Wait()
+					output = buf.Bytes()
+				} else {
+					output, err = cmd.CombinedOutput()
+				}
+				if ctx.Err() != nil {
+					t.Fatal(ctx.Err())
+				}
+				if _, statErr := os.Stat(mark); statErr != nil {
+					t.Fatalf("genesis %s not reached: %v exit=%v output=%s", mode, statErr, err, output)
+				}
+				createErr = fmt.Errorf("child exit=%v output=%q", err, output)
+				reached.Store(true)
+			} else {
+				_, createErr = createWithOptions(dir, genesis, chain, opts)
+			}
+			if !reached.Load() || createErr == nil {
+				t.Fatalf("genesis %s target not reached: %v", mode, createErr)
+			}
+			if mode == "before-mkdir" {
+				if _, err := os.Stat(dir); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
+					t.Fatalf("directory unexpectedly created: %v", err)
+				}
+				if _, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); !errors.Is(err, ErrMissing) {
+					t.Fatalf("absent store opened: %v", err)
+				}
+				if _, err := os.Stat(dir); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
+					t.Fatalf("OpenExisting created missing path: %v", err)
+				}
+				retry, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatalf("fresh retry failed: %v", err)
+				}
+				state, err := retry.Committed()
+				if err != nil || state.Height() != 0 {
+					t.Fatalf("fresh retry state: %v", err)
+				}
+				t.Logf("M11 before-mkdir reached; no B0 directory; fresh retry A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0", state.AppHash())
+				_ = retry.Close()
+				return
+			}
+			if _, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain); !errors.Is(err, ErrExisting) {
+				t.Fatalf("incomplete path reset: %v", err)
+			}
+			opened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
+			if mode == "after-sync" && err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("post-sync genesis not recoverable: %v", err)
+			}
+			if err == nil {
+				state, stateErr := opened.Committed()
+				if stateErr != nil || state.Height() != 0 || state.Validate() != nil {
+					t.Fatalf("post-sync genesis invalid: %v", stateErr)
+				}
+				a, _ := state.Account(sender)
+				_, receiverFound := state.Account(receiver)
+				if a.Balance != 100 || a.Nonce != 0 || receiverFound {
+					t.Fatal("genesis effects incorrect")
+				}
+				t.Logf("M11 %s reached; A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent; identical record recoverable", mode, state.AppHash())
+				_ = opened.Close()
+				return
+			}
+			t.Logf("M11 simulated %s reached; create=%v; incomplete store fails closed: %v", mode, createErr, err)
+		})
+	}
+}

 	return codes, candidate.AppHash(), nil
 }
 
+// PrepareTrusted is the B0 ABCI finalization path. The committed value was
+// fully validated on creation/reload and cannot be mutated by callers. It
+// preserves the Phase 03C Prepare API as the reference evaluator path.
+// An over-limit decided block passes no transactions and advances once.
+func (s *Store) PrepareTrusted(raws [][]byte, overLimit bool) ([]tx.Code, [32]byte, error) {
+	s.mu.Lock()
+	defer s.mu.Unlock()
+	if s.closed {
+		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrClosed
+	}
+	if s.uncertain {
+		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrUncertain
+	}
+	if s.pending != nil {
+		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrPending
+	}
+	var candidate b0state.State
+	var codes []tx.Code
+	var err error
+	if overLimit {
+		candidate = s.committed
+	} else {
+		candidate, codes, err = b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(s.committed, raws)
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, [32]byte{}, err
+		}
+	}
+	candidate, err = candidate.AdvanceBlock()
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, [32]byte{}, err
+	}
+	s.pending = &candidate
+	return codes, candidate.AppHash(), nil
+}
+
 // Commit performs one synchronous complete-record Pebble write. A failed
 // pre-sync hook leaves pending for retry. Any Set or post-sync error leaves
 // in-memory committed unchanged and requires reopening to resolve ambiguity.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Nearest-rank diagnostic percentiles from logged Pebble sync samples."""
+import math
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+
+groups = defaultdict(list)
+for line in Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text().splitlines():
+    match = re.search(r"sync_sample k=(\d+) trial=(\d+) elapsed_ns=(\d+)", line)
+    if match:
+        groups[int(match.group(1))].append(int(match.group(3)))
+for k in (1, 64, 4096, 65536):
+    values = sorted(groups[k])
+    assert len(values) == 30, (k, len(values))
+    def rank(p):
+        return values[math.ceil(p * len(values)) - 1]
+    print(f"K={k} n={len(values)} median_ns={rank(.5)} p95_ns={rank(.95)} p99_ns={rank(.99)} min_ns={values[0]} max_ns={values[-1]}")

+func waitMarker(t *testing.T, path string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
+	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
+		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
+			return
+		}
+		time.Sleep(25 * time.Millisecond)
+	}
+	t.Fatalf("fault marker not reached: %s", path)
+}

+func TestB0M7AsymmetricApplicationCrash(t *testing.T) {
+	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M7_CHILD"); mode != "" {
+		home := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M7_HOME")
+		genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		a, err := b0app.Open(filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2"), "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		gate := &preExecutionGate{App: a, mode: mode, blocked: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
+		srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_M7_READY"), []byte("server-ready"), 0600); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		go func() {
+			<-gate.blocked
+			if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_M7_BLOCKED"), []byte(mode), 0600); err != nil {
+				panic(err)
+			}
+		}()
+		select {} // The parent SIGKILLs this disposable application process.
+	}
+	for _, mode := range []string{"before-finalize", "before-commit"} {
+		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
+			plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+			comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+			root := t.TempDir()
+			home := filepath.Join(root, "home")
+			genesisFile := filepath.Join(root, "genesis.bin")
+			genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+			if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			rpc, p2p, abci := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+			if rpc == p2p || rpc == abci || p2p == abci {
+				t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+			}
+			args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+				"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abci)}
+			if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("init: %v %s", err, out)
+			}
+			ready, blocked := filepath.Join(root, "app.ready"), filepath.Join(root, "app.blocked")
+			appLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "app.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			appChild := exec.Command(os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestB0M7AsymmetricApplicationCrash$")
+			appChild.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M7_CHILD="+mode, "PLUTO_M7_HOME="+home, "PLUTO_M7_READY="+ready, "PLUTO_M7_BLOCKED="+blocked)
+			appChild.Stdout, appChild.Stderr = appLog, appLog
+			if err := appChild.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if appChild.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = appChild.Process.Kill()
+					_ = appChild.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = appLog.Close()
+			})
+			waitMarker(t, ready)
+			firstLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-first.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+			cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
+			if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+					_ = cmd.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = firstLog.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+			var sender, receiver b0state.Address
+			aBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+			bBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+			copy(sender[:], aBytes)
+			copy(receiver[:], bBytes)
+			wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+			var admission struct {
+				Result struct {
+					Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+				} `json:"result"`
+			}
+			if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("admission=%d", admission.Result.Code)
+			}
+			waitMarker(t, blocked)
+			if err := appChild.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := appChild.Wait(); err == nil {
+				t.Fatal("application child was not killed")
+			}
+			// Comet remains the independent process at the instant of app kill.
+			// Observe its RPC again after the lost callback, then coordinate stop.
+			time.Sleep(1200 * time.Millisecond)
+			var status statusResponse
+			rpcErr := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/status", &status)
+			if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil && err != os.ErrProcessDone {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+			_ = firstLog.Close()
+			beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+			a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			before, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			account, _ := before.Account(sender)
+			if beforeB != beforeC+1 || before.Height() != beforeC || account.Balance != 100 || account.Nonce != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("pre-recovery B=%d C=%d A=%d sender=%+v", beforeB, beforeC, before.Height(), account)
+			}
+			config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", a)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			defer srv.Stop()
+			defer a.Close()
+			restartLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-restart.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+			restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
+			if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+					_ = restart.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = restartLog.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, beforeB+1)
+			if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			after, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			account, _ = after.Account(sender)
+			receiverAccount, found := after.Account(receiver)
+			if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 || !found || receiverAccount.Balance != 25 || receiverAccount.Nonce != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("duplicate/missing effect: A=%+v B=%+v", account, receiverAccount)
+			}
+			finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() {
+				t.Fatalf("post-recovery B=%d C=%d A=%d", finalB, finalC, after.Height())
+			}
+			if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
+				if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				for _, name := range []string{"app.log", "comet-first.log", "comet-restart.log"} {
+					raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(root, name))
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m7-"+mode+"-"+name), raw, 0600); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			t.Logf("M7 %s: SIGKILL app child at marked callback; Comet RPC after 1.2s=%v; before B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent; real replay; after B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0; logs=%s", mode, rpcErr, beforeB, beforeC, before.Height(), before.AppHash(), finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.AppHash(), root)
+		})
+	}
+}

+//go:build integration
+
+package integration
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"context"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"strconv"
+	"syscall"
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0app"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/node"
+	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/server"
+	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"
+)
+
+type genesisResponseGate struct {
+	*b0app.App
+	synced  chan struct{}
+	release chan struct{}
+}
+
+func (g *genesisResponseGate) InitChain(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestInitChain) (*abci.ResponseInitChain, error) {
+	resp, err := g.App.InitChain(ctx, req)
+	if err == nil {
+		close(g.synced)
+		<-g.release
+	}
+	return resp, err
+}
+
+func TestB0M11RealInitChainResponseLoss(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+	root := t.TempDir()
+	home := filepath.Join(root, "home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(root, "genesis.bin")
+	genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init: %v %s", err, out)
+	}
+	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	gate := &genesisResponseGate{App: a, synced: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
+	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	firstLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-first.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
+	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = srv.Stop()
+		_ = a.Close()
+		_ = firstLog.Close()
+	})
+	select {
+	case <-gate.synced:
+	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("real InitChain did not reach post-sync gate")
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("Comet was not killed")
+	}
+	close(gate.release)
+	if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_ = firstLog.Close()
+	beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	reopened, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	before, exists, err := reopened.Committed()
+	if err != nil || !exists || before.Height() != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("genesis reopened: %v exists=%v A=%d", err, exists, before.Height())
+	}
+	accounts := before.Accounts()
+	if len(accounts) != 1 || accounts[0].Balance != 100 || accounts[0].Nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("genesis effects=%+v", accounts)
+	}
+	if beforeB != 0 || beforeC != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("genesis response loss B=%d C=%d A=0", beforeB, beforeC)
+	}
+	expectedTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(genesis, "pluto-b0-v1")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	capture := &capturedB0App{App: reopened, observed: make(chan []byte, 1)}
+	second, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", capture)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := second.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer second.Stop()
+	defer reopened.Close()
+	restartLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-restart.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
+	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+			_ = restart.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = restartLog.Close()
+	})
+	var retried []byte
+	select {
+	case retried = <-capture.observed:
+	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("Comet did not retry InitChain")
+	}
+	if !bytes.Equal(retried, expectedTransport) {
+		t.Fatal("retry transport differs from initial genesis")
+	}
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	after, _, err := reopened.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() || after.Accounts()[0].Balance != 100 || after.Accounts()[0].Nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("post-retry B=%d C=%d A=%d accounts=%+v", finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.Accounts())
+	}
+	if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		for _, name := range []string{"comet-first.log", "comet-restart.log"} {
+			raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(root, name))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m11-"+name), raw, 0600); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	t.Logf("M11 real InitChain response withheld after synced A=0; SIGKILL Comet; before B=%d C=%d A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0; identical JSON/Base64 retry observed; after B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=100/0", beforeB, beforeC, before.AppHash(), finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.AppHash())
+}

+//go:build integration

+package integration

+import (
+	"context"

+	"fmt"

+	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs/errorfs"
+	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/server"
+	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"

+type m4SyncFS struct {
+	vfs.FS
+	enabled *atomic.Bool
+	mark    string
+	kill    bool
+}
+type m4SyncFile struct {
+	vfs.File
+	fs   *m4SyncFS
+	path string
+}
+
+func (fs *m4SyncFS) wrap(path string, f vfs.File, err error) (vfs.File, error) {
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	return &m4SyncFile{File: f, fs: fs, path: path}, nil
+}
+func (fs *m4SyncFS) Create(path string) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, e := fs.FS.Create(path)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, e)
+}
+func (fs *m4SyncFS) Open(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, e := fs.FS.Open(path, opts...)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, e)
+}
+func (fs *m4SyncFS) OpenReadWrite(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, e := fs.FS.OpenReadWrite(path, opts...)
+	return fs.wrap(path, f, e)
+}
+func (fs *m4SyncFS) ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath string) (vfs.File, error) {
+	f, e := fs.FS.ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath)
+	return fs.wrap(newPath, f, e)
+}
+func (f *m4SyncFile) fail(method string) bool {
+	if !f.fs.enabled.Load() || !strings.HasSuffix(f.path, ".log") {
+		return false
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(f.fs.mark, []byte(method+" "+f.path), 0600); err != nil {
+		panic(err)
+	}
+	if f.fs.kill {
+		select {}
+	}
+	return true
+}
+func (f *m4SyncFile) Sync() error {
+	if f.fail("Sync") {
+		return errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.Sync()
+}
+func (f *m4SyncFile) SyncData() error {
+	if f.fail("SyncData") {
+		return errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.SyncData()
+}
+func (f *m4SyncFile) SyncTo(n int64) (bool, error) {
+	if f.fail(fmt.Sprintf("SyncTo(%d)", n)) {
+		return false, errorfs.ErrInjected
+	}
+	return f.File.SyncTo(n)
+}
+
+type m4FaultApp struct {
+	*b0app.App
+	target  atomic.Bool
+	enabled *atomic.Bool
+}
+
+func (a *m4FaultApp) FinalizeBlock(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestFinalizeBlock) (*abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock, error) {
+	r, e := a.App.FinalizeBlock(ctx, req)
+	if e == nil && len(req.Txs) > 0 {
+		a.target.Store(true)
+	}
+	return r, e
+}
+func (a *m4FaultApp) Commit(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestCommit) (*abci.ResponseCommit, error) {
+	if a.target.Load() {
+		a.enabled.Store(true)
+	}
+	return a.App.Commit(ctx, req)
+}
+
+func TestB0M4RealCrossStoreWALFault(t *testing.T) {
+	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_REAL_CHILD"); mode != "" {
+		home := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_REAL_HOME")
+		genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+		config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		enabled := new(atomic.Bool)
+		mark := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_REAL_MARK")
+		killMode := strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill")
+		var fs vfs.FS
+		if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-write") {
+			fs = errorfs.Wrap(vfs.Default, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(op errorfs.Op, path string) error {
+				if enabled.Load() && op == errorfs.OpFileWrite && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") {
+					if err := os.WriteFile(mark, []byte("Write "+path), 0600); err != nil {
+						panic(err)
+					}
+					if killMode {
+						select {}
+					}
+					return errorfs.ErrInjected
+				}
+				return nil
+			}))
+		} else {
+			fs = &m4SyncFS{FS: vfs.Default, enabled: enabled, mark: mark, kill: killMode}
+		}
+		a, err := b0app.OpenWithStoreOptions(filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2"), "pluto-b0-v1", genesis, b0store.Options{FS: fs})
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		wrapper := &m4FaultApp{App: a, enabled: enabled}
+		srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", wrapper)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_REAL_READY"), []byte("ready"), 0600); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		select {} // Pebble fatal exits this disposable application process.
+	}
+	for _, mode := range []string{"wal-write", "wal-sync", "wal-write-kill", "wal-sync-kill"} {
+		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
+			plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+			comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+			root := t.TempDir()
+			home := filepath.Join(root, "home")
+			genesisFile := filepath.Join(root, "genesis.bin")
+			genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+			if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+			if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+				t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+			}
+			args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile, "--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+			if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("init: %v %s", err, out)
+			}
+			initialLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-initial.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			initial := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+			initial.Stdout, initial.Stderr = initialLog, initialLog
+			if err := initial.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+			if err := initial.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := initial.Wait(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = initialLog.Close()
+			ready, mark := filepath.Join(root, "app.ready"), filepath.Join(root, "fault.mark")
+			appLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "app.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			appChild := exec.Command(os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestB0M4RealCrossStoreWALFault$")
+			appChild.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M4_REAL_CHILD="+mode, "PLUTO_M4_REAL_HOME="+home, "PLUTO_M4_REAL_READY="+ready, "PLUTO_M4_REAL_MARK="+mark)
+			appChild.Stdout, appChild.Stderr = appLog, appLog
+			if err := appChild.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if appChild.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = appChild.Process.Kill()
+					_ = appChild.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = appLog.Close()
+			})
+			waitMarker(t, ready)
+			firstLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-first.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+			cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
+			if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+					_ = cmd.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = firstLog.Close()
+			})
+			deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
+			for {
+				var status statusResponse
+				if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/status", &status); err == nil {
+					break
+				}
+				if time.Now().After(deadline) {
+					t.Fatal("Comet RPC not ready")
+				}
+				time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
+			}
+			var sender, receiver b0state.Address
+			aBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+			bBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+			copy(sender[:], aBytes)
+			copy(receiver[:], bBytes)
+			wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+			var admission struct {
+				Result struct {
+					Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+				} `json:"result"`
+			}
+			if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("admission=%d", admission.Result.Code)
+			}
+			waitMarker(t, mark)
+			if strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill") {
+				if err := appChild.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			childDone := make(chan error, 1)
+			go func() { childDone <- appChild.Wait() }()
+			select {
+			case <-childDone:
+			case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+				t.Fatal("fault child did not terminate")
+			}
+			if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil && err != os.ErrProcessDone {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+			_ = firstLog.Close()
+			_ = appLog.Close()
+			beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+			a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			before, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			account, _ := before.Account(sender)
+			receiverAccount, found := before.Account(receiver)
+			if beforeB != beforeC+1 || (before.Height() != beforeC && before.Height() != beforeC+1) {
+				t.Fatalf("fault cross-store heights B=%d C=%d A=%d", beforeB, beforeC, before.Height())
+			}
+			if before.Height() == beforeC && (account.Balance != 100 || account.Nonce != 0 || found) {
+				t.Fatalf("old state effects=%+v %+v", account, receiverAccount)
+			}
+			if before.Height() == beforeC+1 && (account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 || !found || receiverAccount.Balance != 25) {
+				t.Fatalf("new state effects=%+v %+v", account, receiverAccount)
+			}
+			if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			restartLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-restart.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			restart := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+			restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
+			if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+					_ = restart.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = restartLog.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, beforeB+1)
+			if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = restartLog.Close()
+			finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			afterApp, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			defer afterApp.Close()
+			after, _, err := afterApp.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			account, _ = after.Account(sender)
+			receiverAccount, found = after.Account(receiver)
+			if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() || account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 || !found || receiverAccount.Balance != 25 || receiverAccount.Nonce != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("recovery B=%d C=%d A=%d sender=%+v receiver=%+v", finalB, finalC, after.Height(), account, receiverAccount)
+			}
+			marker, _ := os.ReadFile(mark)
+			if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
+				if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				for _, name := range []string{"app.log", "comet-initial.log", "comet-first.log", "comet-restart.log"} {
+					raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(root, name))
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m4-"+mode+"-"+name), raw, 0600); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			replay := "real"
+			if before.Height() == beforeC+1 {
+				replay = "mock"
+			}
+			method := "Pebble process-fatal I/O error"
+			if strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill") {
+				method = "SIGKILL application while WAL call blocked"
+			}
+			t.Logf("M4 %s at %q, %s; before B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=%d/%d; %s replay; after B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0", mode, marker, method, beforeB, beforeC, before.Height(), before.AppHash(), func() uint64 {
+				if before.Height() == beforeC {
+					return 100
+				}
+				return 75
+			}(), func() uint64 {
+				if before.Height() == beforeC {
+					return 0
+				}
+				return 1
+			}(), replay, finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.AppHash())
+		})
+	}
+}
