+func TestB0M6CometPostCommitAckFailpoint(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+	root := t.TempDir()
+	home := filepath.Join(root, "home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(root, "genesis.bin")
+	genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init: %v %s", err, out)
+	}
+	configFile := filepath.Join(home, "config", "config.toml")
+	configRaw, err := os.ReadFile(configFile)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(string(configRaw), "create_empty_blocks = true") {
+		t.Fatal("cannot pin first transaction block")
+	}
+	configRaw = []byte(strings.Replace(string(configRaw), "create_empty_blocks = true", "create_empty_blocks = false", 1))
+	if err := os.WriteFile(configFile, configRaw, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", a)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer srv.Stop()
+	defer a.Close()
+	firstLogPath := filepath.Join(root, "comet-first.log")
+	firstLog, err := os.Create(firstLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	index := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M6_FAIL_INDEX")
+	if index == "" {
+		index = "7"
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "FAIL_TEST_INDEX="+index)
+	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
+	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = firstLog.Close()
+	})
+	deadline := time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
+	for {
+		var status statusResponse
+		if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/status", &status); err == nil {
+			break
+		}
+		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
+			t.Fatal("Comet RPC not ready")
+		}
+		time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
+	}
+	var receiver, sender b0state.Address
+	aBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+	bBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+	copy(sender[:], aBytes)
+	copy(receiver[:], bBytes)
+	wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+	var admission struct {
+		Result struct {
+			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+		} `json:"result"`
+	}
+	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("admission=%d", admission.Result.Code)
+	}
+	done := make(chan error, 1)
+	go func() { done <- cmd.Wait() }()
+	select {
+	case <-done:
+	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+		_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+		<-done
+		t.Fatal("failpoint did not terminate Comet")
+	}
+	_ = firstLog.Close()
+	firstRaw, err := os.ReadFile(firstLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	before, exists, err := a.Committed()
+	if err != nil || !exists {
+		t.Fatalf("app state: %v", err)
+	}
+	response, responseErr := cometFinalizedResponse(t, home, beforeB)
+	t.Logf("M6 exploratory index=%s B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x finalized_response_err=%v log_tail=%s", index, beforeB, beforeC, before.Height(), before.AppHash(), responseErr, string(firstRaw[max(0, len(firstRaw)-1500):]))
+	beforeHash := before.AppHash()
+	if !strings.Contains(string(firstRaw), "*** fail-test "+index+" ***") || !strings.Contains(string(firstRaw), "committed state") || beforeB != 1 || beforeC != 0 || before.Height() != 1 || responseErr != nil || len(response.TxResults) != 1 || response.TxResults[0].Code != 0 || !bytes.Equal(response.AppHash, beforeHash[:]) {
+		t.Fatal("M6 post-ack/pre-state-save boundary was not reached")
+	}
+	account, _ := before.Account(sender)
+	receiverAccount, found := before.Account(receiver)
+	if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 || !found || receiverAccount.Balance != 25 {
+		t.Fatalf("pre-restart transfer=%+v receiver=%+v", account, receiverAccount)
+	}
+	restartLogPath := filepath.Join(root, "comet-restart.log")
+	restartLog, err := os.Create(restartLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
+	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+			_ = restart.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = restartLog.Close()
+	})
+	deadline = time.Now().Add(30 * time.Second)
+	for {
+		var status statusResponse
+		if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/status", &status); err == nil {
+			break
+		}
+		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
+			t.Fatal("restart RPC not ready")
+		}
+		time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	after, _, err := a.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	account, _ = after.Account(sender)
+	receiverAccount, found = after.Account(receiver)
+	if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() || account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 || !found || receiverAccount.Balance != 25 || receiverAccount.Nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("post-restart B=%d C=%d A=%d sender=%+v receiver=%+v", finalB, finalC, after.Height(), account, receiverAccount)
+	}
+	restartRaw, err := os.ReadFile(restartLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m6-comet-first.log"), firstRaw, 0600); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m6-comet-restart.log"), restartRaw, 0600); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	t.Logf("M6 FAIL_TEST_INDEX=%s after successful Commit response before Comet state Save; before B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0; restart mock replay; after B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0", index, beforeB, beforeC, before.Height(), before.AppHash(), finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.AppHash())
+}

+//go:build integration

+package integration
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"os"

+	blockstore "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/store"

+func TestB0PinnedHeightAndHashMismatchFailClosed(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, mode := range []string{"app-ahead", "same-height-different-hash", "consensus-ahead-blockstore", "consensus-hash-tamper", "app-behind-unavailable", "app-behind-pruned", "replay-hash-conflict"} {
+		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
+			plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+			home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "home")
+			genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis.bin")
+			genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+			if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+				t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+			}
+			args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+				"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+			if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("init: %v: %s", err, out)
+			}
+			logFile, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "first.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			cmd := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+			cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = logFile, logFile
+			if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+					_ = cmd.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = logFile.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+			if err := cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := cmd.Wait(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = logFile.Close()
+			dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+			a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			state, _, err := a.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			beforeHeight := state.Height()
+			if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			blockHeight, consensusHeight := cometHeights(t, home)
+			injectedA, injectedHash := beforeHeight, state.AppHash()
+			if blockHeight != beforeHeight || consensusHeight != beforeHeight {
+				t.Fatalf("initial mismatch B=%d C=%d A=%d", blockHeight, consensusHeight, beforeHeight)
+			}
+			if mode == "app-ahead" {
+				a, err = b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(context.Background(), &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: beforeHeight + 1}); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if _, err := a.Commit(context.Background(), &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				advanced, _, err := a.Committed()
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				injectedHash = advanced.AppHash()
+				if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				injectedA = beforeHeight + 1
+			} else if mode == "same-height-different-hash" || mode == "app-behind-unavailable" || mode == "app-behind-pruned" || mode == "replay-hash-conflict" {
+				var sender, receiver b0state.Address
+				ab, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+				bb, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+				copy(sender[:], ab)
+				copy(receiver[:], bb)
+				alteredHeight := beforeHeight
+				if mode == "app-behind-unavailable" || mode == "replay-hash-conflict" {
+					alteredHeight = beforeHeight - 1
+				}
+				altered, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", alteredHeight, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 99}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 1}})
+				if mode == "app-behind-pruned" {
+					altered, err = b0state.ParseGenesis(genesis, "pluto-b0-v1")
+					alteredHeight = 0
+				}
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				logical, err := altered.LogicalBytes()
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				hash := altered.AppHash()
+				injectedA, injectedHash = alteredHeight, hash
+				record := append(append([]byte("PLUTOS2!"), logical...), hash[:]...)
+				db, err := pebble.Open(dbDir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if err := db.Set([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"), record, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				// The alternate record is internally canonical and integrity-valid;
+				// only the external Comet state/history can detect the mismatch.
+				probe, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatalf("valid alternate record rejected before Comet handshake: %v", err)
+				}
+				if err := probe.Close(); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if mode == "app-behind-unavailable" {
+					blockDB, err := dbm.NewDB("blockstore", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, filepath.Join(home, "data"))
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					// Remove the latest block while retaining C. This gives A<C
+					// with missing history and must never silently reinitialize.
+					bs := blockstore.NewBlockStore(blockDB)
+					if err := bs.DeleteLatestBlock(); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					if err := blockDB.Close(); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+				} else if mode == "app-behind-pruned" {
+					stateDB, err := dbm.NewDB("state", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, filepath.Join(home, "data"))
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					consensus, err := sm.NewStore(stateDB, sm.StoreOptions{}).Load()
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					if err := stateDB.Close(); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					blockDB, err := dbm.NewDB("blockstore", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, filepath.Join(home, "data"))
+					if err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+					bs := blockstore.NewBlockStore(blockDB)
+					pruned, _, err := bs.PruneBlocks(beforeHeight, consensus)
+					if err != nil || pruned != 1 || bs.Base() != beforeHeight {
+						t.Fatalf("prune failed count=%d base=%d err=%v", pruned, bs.Base(), err)
+					}
+					if err := blockDB.Close(); err != nil {
+						t.Fatal(err)
+					}
+				}
+			} else {
+				stateDB, err := dbm.NewDB("state", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, filepath.Join(home, "data"))
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				store := sm.NewStore(stateDB, sm.StoreOptions{})
+				consensus, err := store.Load()
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if mode == "consensus-ahead-blockstore" {
+					consensus.LastBlockHeight++
+				} else {
+					consensus.AppHash = append([]byte(nil), consensus.AppHash...)
+					consensus.AppHash[0] ^= 1
+				}
+				if err := store.Save(consensus); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if err := stateDB.Close(); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 15*time.Second)
+			defer cancel()
+			out, probeErr := exec.CommandContext(ctx, plutod, "start", "--home", home).CombinedOutput()
+			if ctx.Err() != nil || probeErr == nil {
+				t.Fatalf("mismatch did not fail closed: mode=%s context=%v err=%v output=%s", mode, ctx.Err(), probeErr, out)
+			}
+			if mode == "app-ahead" && !strings.Contains(string(out), "higher than core") {
+				t.Fatalf("wrong app-ahead failure: %s", out)
+			}
+			if mode == "same-height-different-hash" {
+				if !strings.Contains(string(out), "state.AppHash does not match AppHash after replay") {
+					t.Fatalf("wrong hash mismatch failure: %s", out)
+				}
+			}
+			expected := map[string]string{
+				"consensus-ahead-blockstore": "StateBlockHeight (3) > StoreBlockHeight (2)",
+				"consensus-hash-tamper":      "state.AppHash does not match AppHash after replay",
+				"app-behind-unavailable":     "StateBlockHeight (2) > StoreBlockHeight (1)",
+				"app-behind-pruned":          "app block height (0) is too far below block store base (2)",
+				"replay-hash-conflict":       "state.AppHash does not match AppHash after replay",
+			}
+			if expected[mode] != "" && !strings.Contains(string(out), expected[mode]) {
+				t.Fatalf("wrong %s failure: %s", mode, out)
+			}
+			if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
+				if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "mismatch-"+mode+".log"), out, 0600); err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+			}
+			finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			t.Logf("%s: real Comet refused restart; original B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent; injected B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x; process exit=%v output=%q", mode, blockHeight, consensusHeight, beforeHeight, state.AppHash(), finalB, finalC, injectedA, injectedHash, probeErr, out)
+		})
+	}
+}

+//go:build integration
+
+package integration
+
+import (
+	"bytes"
+	"context"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"strconv"
+	"strings"
+	"sync"
+	"sync/atomic"
+	"syscall"
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0app"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/node"
+	dbm "github.com/cometbft/cometbft-db"
+	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/server"
+	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"
+	sm "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/state"
+	blockstore "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/store"
+)
+
+// The wrapper is confined to a disposable real-Comet integration test. It
+// loses the Commit acknowledgment after the application sync has completed.
+type postSyncCommitGate struct {
+	*b0app.App
+	target  atomic.Bool
+	once    sync.Once
+	synced  chan struct{}
+	release chan struct{}
+}
+
+type preExecutionGate struct {
+	*b0app.App
+	mode      string // "before-finalize" or "before-commit"
+	target    atomic.Bool
+	cancelled atomic.Bool
+	once      sync.Once
+	blocked   chan struct{}
+	release   chan struct{}
+}
+
+func (g *preExecutionGate) PrepareProposal(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestPrepareProposal) (*abci.ResponsePrepareProposal, error) {
+	if len(req.Txs) > 0 && g.mode == "before-proposal" {
+		g.once.Do(func() { close(g.blocked) })
+		<-g.release
+	}
+	return g.App.PrepareProposal(ctx, req)
+}
+
+func (g *preExecutionGate) FinalizeBlock(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestFinalizeBlock) (*abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock, error) {
+	if len(req.Txs) > 0 && g.mode == "before-finalize" {
+		g.once.Do(func() { close(g.blocked) })
+		<-g.release
+		if g.cancelled.Load() {
+			return nil, context.Canceled
+		}
+	}
+	resp, err := g.App.FinalizeBlock(ctx, req)
+	if err == nil && len(req.Txs) > 0 {
+		g.target.Store(true)
+	}
+	return resp, err
+}
+
+func (g *preExecutionGate) Commit(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestCommit) (*abci.ResponseCommit, error) {
+	if g.target.Load() && g.mode == "before-commit" {
+		g.once.Do(func() { close(g.blocked) })
+		<-g.release
+		if g.cancelled.Load() {
+			return nil, context.Canceled
+		}
+	}
+	return g.App.Commit(ctx, req)
+}
+
+func (g *postSyncCommitGate) FinalizeBlock(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestFinalizeBlock) (*abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock, error) {
+	resp, err := g.App.FinalizeBlock(ctx, req)
+	if err == nil && len(req.Txs) > 0 {
+		g.target.Store(true)
+	}
+	return resp, err
+}
+
+func (g *postSyncCommitGate) Commit(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestCommit) (*abci.ResponseCommit, error) {
+	resp, err := g.App.Commit(ctx, req)
+	if err == nil && g.target.Load() {
+		g.once.Do(func() { close(g.synced) })
+		<-g.release
+	}
+	return resp, err
+}
+
+func cometHeights(t *testing.T, home string) (int64, int64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	dir := filepath.Join(home, "data")
+	blockDB, err := dbm.NewDB("blockstore", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	b := blockstore.NewBlockStore(blockDB).Height()
+	if err := blockDB.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	stateDB, err := dbm.NewDB("state", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	state, err := sm.NewStore(stateDB, sm.StoreOptions{}).Load()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := stateDB.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return b, state.LastBlockHeight
+}
+
+func cometFinalizedResponse(t *testing.T, home string, height int64) (*abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock, error) {
+	t.Helper()
+	db, err := dbm.NewDB("state", dbm.GoLevelDBBackend, filepath.Join(home, "data"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	response, loadErr := sm.NewStore(db, sm.StoreOptions{}).LoadLastFinalizeBlockResponse(height)
+	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return response, loadErr
+}
+
+func TestB0PinnedMockReplayAfterDurableCommitBeforeAcknowledgment(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis.bin")
+	genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init: %v: %s", err, out)
+	}
+	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	gate := &postSyncCommitGate{App: a, synced: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
+	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	logPath := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "comet-first.log")
+	logFile, err := os.Create(logPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = logFile, logFile
+	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = srv.Stop()
+		_ = a.Close()
+		_ = logFile.Close()
+	})
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+	var receiver b0state.Address
+	rawReceiver, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+	copy(receiver[:], rawReceiver)
+	wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+	var admission struct {
+		Result struct {
+			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+		} `json:"result"`
+	}
+	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("CheckTx admission: %+v", admission)
+	}
+	select {
+	case <-gate.synced:
+	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("transaction Commit did not reach post-sync gate")
+	}
+	// The child is killed while its ABCI Commit response is withheld.
+	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("Comet child was not killed")
+	}
+	close(gate.release)
+	if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_ = logFile.Close()
+	blockHeight, stateHeight := cometHeights(t, home)
+	recovered, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	state, exists, err := recovered.Committed()
+	if err != nil || !exists {
+		t.Fatalf("reopen app: %v", err)
+	}
+	appHeight := state.Height()
+	var sender b0state.Address
+	rawSender, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+	copy(sender[:], rawSender)
+	account, _ := state.Account(sender)
+	if blockHeight != appHeight || stateHeight != appHeight-1 || account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("pre-replay B=%d C=%d A=%d sender=%+v", blockHeight, stateHeight, appHeight, account)
+	}
+	storedResponse, err := cometFinalizedResponse(t, home, blockHeight)
+	committedHash := state.AppHash()
+	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(storedResponse.AppHash, committedHash[:]) || len(storedResponse.TxResults) != 1 || storedResponse.TxResults[0].Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("M5 persisted Finalize response: %+v %v", storedResponse, err)
+	}
+	secondServer, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", recovered)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := secondServer.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer secondServer.Stop()
+	defer recovered.Close()
+	secondLogPath := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "comet-restart.log")
+	secondLog, err := os.Create(secondLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer secondLog.Close()
+	restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = secondLog, secondLog
+	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer func() {
+		if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+			_ = restart.Wait()
+		}
+	}()
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, appHeight+1)
+	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := secondServer.Stop(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := recovered.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	secondLogBytes, err := os.ReadFile(secondLogPath)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if !bytes.Contains(secondLogBytes, []byte("Replay last block using mock app")) {
+		t.Fatalf("pinned mock replay path not observed: %s", secondLogBytes)
+	}
+	after, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer after.Close()
+	afterState, _, err := after.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	account, _ = afterState.Account(sender)
+	if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("duplicate transfer after mock replay: %+v", account)
+	}
+	finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	if finalB != finalC || finalC != afterState.Height() {
+		t.Fatalf("replay heights B=%d C=%d A=%d", finalB, finalC, afterState.Height())
+	}
+	t.Logf("M5/M6: real Comet SIGKILL while Commit response withheld; before B=%d C=%d A=%d; mock replay observed; after B=%d C=%d A=%d; sender balance=%d nonce=%d; app hash=%x; log=%s", blockHeight, stateHeight, appHeight, finalB, finalC, afterState.Height(), account.Balance, account.Nonce, afterState.AppHash(), strings.TrimSpace(secondLogPath))
+}
+
+func TestB0PinnedRealReplayBeforeApplicationCommit(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, mode := range []string{"before-finalize", "before-commit"} {
+		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
+			plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+			comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+			home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "home")
+			genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis.bin")
+			genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+			if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+				t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+			}
+			args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+				"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+			if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatalf("init: %v: %s", err, out)
+			}
+			config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+			a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			gate := &preExecutionGate{App: a, mode: mode, blocked: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
+			srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			firstLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "first.log"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+			cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
+			if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+					_ = cmd.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = srv.Stop()
+				_ = a.Close()
+				_ = firstLog.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+			var receiver b0state.Address
+			rawReceiver, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+			copy(receiver[:], rawReceiver)
+			wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+			var admission struct {
+				Result struct {
+					Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+				} `json:"result"`
+			}
+			if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("admission: %+v", admission)
+			}
+			select {
+			case <-gate.blocked:
+			case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+				t.Fatal("target callback did not block")
+			}
+			if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
+				t.Fatal("Comet child was not killed")
+			}
+			gate.cancelled.Store(true)
+			close(gate.release)
+			if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = firstLog.Close()
+			blockHeight, stateHeight := cometHeights(t, home)
+			recovered, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			before, _, err := recovered.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if blockHeight != stateHeight+1 || before.Height() != stateHeight {
+				t.Fatalf("pre-replay B=%d C=%d A=%d", blockHeight, stateHeight, before.Height())
+			}
+			if mode == "before-commit" {
+				storedResponse, err := cometFinalizedResponse(t, home, blockHeight)
+				if err != nil || len(storedResponse.TxResults) != 1 || storedResponse.TxResults[0].Code != 0 {
+					t.Fatalf("M3 persisted Finalize response: %+v %v", storedResponse, err)
+				}
+				var sender, receiver b0state.Address
+				ab, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+				bb, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+				copy(sender[:], ab)
+				copy(receiver[:], bb)
+				expected, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", blockHeight, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 75, Nonce: 1}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 25}})
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				hash := expected.AppHash()
+				if !bytes.Equal(storedResponse.AppHash, hash[:]) {
+					t.Fatalf("M3 stored AppHash %x expected %x", storedResponse.AppHash, hash)
+				}
+			}
+			secondServer, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", recovered)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := secondServer.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			secondLogPath := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "restart.log")
+			secondLog, err := os.Create(secondLogPath)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+			restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = secondLog, secondLog
+			if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			t.Cleanup(func() {
+				if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+					_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+					_ = restart.Wait()
+				}
+				_ = secondServer.Stop()
+				_ = recovered.Close()
+				_ = secondLog.Close()
+			})
+			b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, blockHeight+1)
+			if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := secondServer.Stop(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if err := recovered.Close(); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			_ = secondLog.Close()
+			logBytes, err := os.ReadFile(secondLogPath)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if !bytes.Contains(logBytes, []byte("Replay last block using real app")) {
+				t.Fatalf("real replay path absent: %s", logBytes)
+			}
+			after, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			defer after.Close()
+			state, _, err := after.Committed()
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			var sender b0state.Address
+			rawSender, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+			copy(sender[:], rawSender)
+			account, _ := state.Account(sender)
+			if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("replayed transfer state: %+v", account)
+			}
+			finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+			if finalB != finalC || finalC != state.Height() {
+				t.Fatalf("heights after real replay B=%d C=%d A=%d", finalB, finalC, state.Height())
+			}
+			t.Logf("%s: Comet SIGKILL before application write; before B=%d C=%d A=%d; real replay observed; after B=%d C=%d A=%d; balance=%d nonce=%d hash=%x", mode, blockHeight, stateHeight, before.Height(), finalB, finalC, state.Height(), account.Balance, account.Nonce, state.AppHash())
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestB0PinnedCrashBeforeBlockPersistence(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
+	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis.bin")
+	genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init: %v: %s", err, out)
+	}
+	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	gate := &preExecutionGate{App: a, mode: "before-proposal", blocked: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
+	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	logFile, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "first.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = logFile, logFile
+	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = srv.Stop()
+		_ = a.Close()
+		_ = logFile.Close()
+	})
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+	var receiver b0state.Address
+	rawReceiver, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+	copy(receiver[:], rawReceiver)
+	wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+	var admission struct {
+		Result struct {
+			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+		} `json:"result"`
+	}
+	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_sync?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &admission); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if admission.Result.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("admission: %+v", admission)
+	}
+	select {
+	case <-gate.blocked:
+	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("proposal callback did not block")
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("Comet child was not killed")
+	}
+	gate.cancelled.Store(true)
+	close(gate.release)
+	if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_ = logFile.Close()
+	blockHeight, stateHeight := cometHeights(t, home)
+	recovered, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	state, _, err := recovered.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if blockHeight != stateHeight || stateHeight != state.Height() {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected block persistence B=%d C=%d A=%d", blockHeight, stateHeight, state.Height())
+	}
+	var sender b0state.Address
+	rawSender, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+	copy(sender[:], rawSender)
+	account, _ := state.Account(sender)
+	if account.Balance != 100 || account.Nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("pre-restart state: %+v", account)
+	}
+	secondServer, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", recovered)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := secondServer.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	secondLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "restart.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
+	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = secondLog, secondLog
+	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+			_ = restart.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = secondServer.Stop()
+		_ = recovered.Close()
+		_ = secondLog.Close()
+	})
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, blockHeight+1)
+	// The pre-persistence proposal was not available for replay. Rebroadcast
+	// it explicitly and confirm one transfer is committed.
+	var committed broadcastResult
+	if err := rpcGetLong(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_commit?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &committed); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if committed.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || committed.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("rebroadcast result: %+v", committed)
+	}
+	state, _, err = recovered.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	account, _ = state.Account(sender)
+	if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("rebroadcast state: %+v", account)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Logf("M1: killed Comet in PrepareProposal before block persistence; B=C=A=%d; after explicit rebroadcast A=%d balance=%d nonce=%d", blockHeight, state.Height(), account.Balance, account.Nonce)
+}
+
+func TestB0ApplicationProcessKilledWhileCometChildAlive(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis.bin")
+	genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init: %v: %s", err, out)
+	}
+	logFile, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "first.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}
+	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = logFile, logFile
+	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	group := cmd.Process.Pid
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		_ = syscall.Kill(-group, syscall.SIGKILL)
+		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = cmd.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = logFile.Close()
+	})
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+	var receiver b0state.Address
+	rawReceiver, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+	copy(receiver[:], rawReceiver)
+	wire := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+	var committed broadcastResult
+	if err := rpcGetLong(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_commit?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(wire), &committed); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if committed.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || committed.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("pre-crash transfer: %+v", committed)
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("application process was not killed")
+	}
+	// The Comet child inherited this isolated process group. Confirm the
+	// child remains after the application parent was terminated, then stop it.
+	childAlive := syscall.Kill(-group, 0) == nil
+	if !childAlive {
+		t.Fatal("Comet child did not remain alive after application kill")
+	}
+	_ = syscall.Kill(-group, syscall.SIGKILL)
+	_ = logFile.Close()
+	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
+	var reopened *b0app.App
+	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
+	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
+		reopened, err = b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+		if err == nil {
+			break
+		}
+		time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
+	}
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("reopen after app/Comet stop: %v", err)
+	}
+	state, _, err := reopened.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	var sender b0state.Address
+	rawSender, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+	copy(sender[:], rawSender)
+	account, _ := state.Account(sender)
+	if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("post-crash transfer state: %+v", account)
+	}
+	if err := reopened.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	restartLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "restart.log"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	restart := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
+	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(func() {
+		if restart.ProcessState == nil {
+			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
+			_ = restart.Wait()
+		}
+		_ = restartLog.Close()
+	})
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, state.Height()+1)
+	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	_ = restartLog.Close()
+	check, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer check.Close()
+	after, _, err := check.Committed()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	account, _ = after.Account(sender)
+	if account.Balance != 75 || account.Nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("duplicate execution after app kill: %+v", account)
+	}
+	finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
+	if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() {
+		t.Fatalf("post-restart B=%d C=%d A=%d", finalB, finalC, after.Height())
+	}
+	t.Logf("M7: SIGKILL application parent, Comet child remained alive; before coordinated restart B=%d C=%d A=%d; after B=%d C=%d A=%d; sender balance=%d nonce=%d", beforeB, beforeC, state.Height(), finalB, finalC, after.Height(), account.Balance, account.Nonce)
+}

+//go:build integration

+package integration

+import (
+	"encoding/base64"
+	"encoding/binary"
+	"encoding/hex"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"fmt"
+	"net/http"
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"

+	"time"
+
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
+	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
+	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4"
+	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4/ecdsa"

+
+func realNodeSigned(t *testing.T, receiver b0state.Address, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
+	return realNodeSignedChain(t, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, amount, nonce)
+}
+
+func realNodeSignedChain(t *testing.T, chain string, receiver b0state.Address, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
+	t.Helper()
+	var secret [32]byte
+	secret[31] = 1 // Public TEST ONLY scalar; never a validator key.
+	key := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secret[:])
+	record := tx.Transaction{ChainID: chain, Receiver: receiver, Amount: amount, Nonce: nonce}
+	copy(record.Sender[:], key.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
+	digest, err := tx.SigningDigest(record)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	sig := ecdsa.Sign(key, digest[:])
+	r, s := sig.R(), sig.S()
+	r.PutBytes(&record.R)
+	s.PutBytes(&record.S)
+	wire, err := tx.Encode(record)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return wire
+}
+
+type broadcastResult struct {
+	Result struct {
+		CheckTx struct {
+			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+			Log  string `json:"log"`
+		} `json:"check_tx"`
+		TxResult struct {
+			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
+			Log  string `json:"log"`
+		} `json:"tx_result"`
+		Hash   string `json:"hash"`
+		Height string `json:"height"`
+	} `json:"result"`
+	Error any `json:"error"`
+}
+
+func rpcGetLong(t *testing.T, port int, path string, target any) error {
+	t.Helper()
+	client := http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}
+	response, err := client.Get(fmt.Sprintf("http://127.0.0.1:%d%s", port, path))
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	defer response.Body.Close()
+	if response.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
+		return fmt.Errorf("RPC HTTP %d", response.StatusCode)
+	}
+	return json.NewDecoder(response.Body).Decode(target)
+}
+
+func TestB0RealTransactionCommitAndRestart(t *testing.T) {
+	plutod, err := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0-home")
+	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis-v2.bin")
+	genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
+	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
+		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
+	}
+	initArgs := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
+		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
+	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, initArgs...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("init B0: %v: %s", err, out)
+	}
+	var process *exec.Cmd
+	start := func(label string) {
+		file, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), label+".log"))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		process = exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
+		process.Stdout, process.Stderr = file, file
+		if err := process.Start(); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = file.Close() })
+	}
+	stop := func() {
+		if process == nil {
+			return
+		}
+		_ = process.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM)
+		done := make(chan error, 1)
+		go func() { done <- process.Wait() }()
+		select {
+		case err := <-done:
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Errorf("stop B0: %v", err)
+			}
+		case <-time.After(15 * time.Second):
+			_ = process.Process.Kill()
+			<-done
+			t.Error("B0 stop timeout")
+		}
+		process = nil
+	}
+	t.Cleanup(stop)
+	start("first")
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
+	var sender, receiver b0state.Address
+	addrA, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
+	addrB, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
+	copy(sender[:], addrA)
+	copy(receiver[:], addrB)
+	broadcast := func(raw []byte) broadcastResult {
+		t.Helper()
+		var r broadcastResult
+		if err := rpcGetLong(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_commit?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(raw), &r); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if r.Error != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("broadcast RPC error: %+v", r.Error)
+		}
+		return r
+	}
+	query := func(address b0state.Address) (uint64, uint64) {
+		t.Helper()
+		var response struct {
+			Result struct {
+				Response struct {
+					Code  uint32 `json:"code"`
+					Value string `json:"value"`
+				} `json:"response"`
+			} `json:"result"`
+		}
+		path := "/abci_query?path=%22b0%2Faccount%2Fv1%22&data=0x" + hex.EncodeToString(address[:])
+		if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, path, &response); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		value, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(response.Result.Response.Value)
+		if err != nil || response.Result.Response.Code != 0 || len(value) != 17 || value[0] != 1 {
+			t.Fatalf("account query: %+v %v", response, err)
+		}
+		return binary.BigEndian.Uint64(value[1:9]), binary.BigEndian.Uint64(value[9:17])
+	}
+	first := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
+	bad := []byte{1, 2, 3}
+	invalid := broadcast(bad)
+	if invalid.Result.CheckTx.Code != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("malformed CheckTx: %+v", invalid)
+	}
+	badSignature := append([]byte(nil), first...)
+	badSignature[len(badSignature)-80] ^= 1
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		raw  []byte
+		code uint32
+	}{
+		{realNodeSignedChain(t, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0), 2},
+		{badSignature, 4},
+		{realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 1), 6},
+		{realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 101, 0), 7},
+	} {
+		response := broadcast(item.raw)
+		if response.Result.CheckTx.Code != item.code {
+			t.Fatalf("negative CheckTx code=%d want=%d", response.Result.CheckTx.Code, item.code)
+		}
+	}
+	valid := broadcast(first)
+	cometHash := tx.CometHash(first)
+	if valid.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || valid.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 || !strings.EqualFold(valid.Result.Hash, hex.EncodeToString(cometHash[:])) {
+		t.Fatalf("valid broadcast: %+v", valid)
+	}
+	height, err := strconv.ParseInt(valid.Result.Height, 10, 64)
+	if err != nil || height < 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("broadcast height: %+v %v", valid, err)
+	}
+	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 75 || nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("sender after transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
+	}
+	if balance, nonce := query(receiver); balance != 25 || nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("receiver after transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
+	}
+	var block blockResponse
+	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, fmt.Sprintf("/block?height=%d", height), &block); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if len(block.Result.Block.Data.Txs) != 1 || block.Result.Block.Data.Txs[0] != base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(first) {
+		t.Fatalf("included transaction bytes: %+v", block)
+	}
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, height+1)
+	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, fmt.Sprintf("/block?height=%d", height+1), &block); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	expected, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", height, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 75, Nonce: 1}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 25}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	hash := expected.AppHash()
+	if !strings.EqualFold(block.Result.Block.Header.AppHash, hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])) {
+		t.Fatalf("header H+1 does not contain state hash after H: %s expected %X", block.Result.Block.Header.AppHash, hash)
+	}
+	t.Logf("transaction H=%d CometHash=%s; header H+1 AppHash=%X; Pluto TxID is separate", height, valid.Result.Hash, hash)
+	stop()
+	start("restart")
+	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, height+2)
+	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 75 || nonce != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("sender after restart: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
+	}
+	replay := broadcast(first)
+	if replay.Result.CheckTx.Code != 6 {
+		t.Fatalf("replay accepted: %+v", replay)
+	}
+	second := broadcast(realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 10, 1))
+	if second.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || second.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("post-restart tx: %+v", second)
+	}
+	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 65 || nonce != 2 {
+		t.Fatalf("sender after second transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
+	}
+	if balance, nonce := query(receiver); balance != 35 || nonce != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("receiver after second transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
+	}
+}

// Package b0app connects the accepted B0 state machine to CometBFT ABCI++.
package b0app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/binary"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"os"
	"slices"
	"sync"

	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0store"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/version"
)

const (
	maxBlockTxs     = 6144
	maxBlockBytes   = 1048576
	resultCodespace = "pluto.b0.v1"
)

var ErrGenesisConflict = errors.New("B0 genesis conflicts with configured or committed state")

// App owns the B0 store and serializes initialization with all ABCI calls.
// A missing store is allowed only before a fresh CometBFT chain initializes.
type App struct {
	mu              sync.RWMutex
	dbDir           string
	chainID         string
	expectedGenesis []byte
	expectedState   b0state.State
	store           *b0store.Store
	closed          bool
}

var _ abci.Application = (*App)(nil)

func Open(dbDir, chainID string, canonicalGenesis []byte) (*App, error) {
	return OpenWithStoreOptions(dbDir, chainID, canonicalGenesis, b0store.Options{})
}

// OpenWithStoreOptions exposes Pebble filesystem fault injection to recovery
// tests. The production node calls Open with zero options.
func OpenWithStoreOptions(dbDir, chainID string, canonicalGenesis []byte, options b0store.Options) (*App, error) {
	expected, err := b0state.ParseGenesis(canonicalGenesis, chainID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	a := &App{dbDir: dbDir, chainID: chainID, expectedGenesis: bytes.Clone(canonicalGenesis), expectedState: expected}
	if _, err := os.Stat(dbDir); err == nil {
		a.store, err = b0store.OpenExistingWithOptions(dbDir, chainID, options)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		state, err := a.store.Committed()
		if err != nil {
			_ = a.store.Close()
			return nil, err
		}
		// At height zero the whole logical snapshot must equal genesis. After
		// transfers, accounts can differ; supply remains conserved. CometBFT's
		// replay handshake checks the committed height/hash against its history.
		// Neither check alone proves complete historical genesis provenance.
		if (state.Height() == 0 && (state.AppHash() != expected.AppHash() ||
			!slices.Equal(state.Accounts(), expected.Accounts()))) ||
			state.TotalSupply() != expected.TotalSupply() {
			_ = a.store.Close()
			return nil, ErrGenesisConflict
		}
	} else if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, err
	}
	return a, nil
}

func (a *App) Close() error {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()
	if a.closed {
		return b0store.ErrClosed
	}
	a.closed = true
	if a.store != nil {
		return a.store.Close()
	}
	return nil
}

func (a *App) Committed() (b0state.State, bool, error) {
	a.mu.RLock()
	defer a.mu.RUnlock()
	if a.closed {
		return b0state.State{}, false, b0store.ErrClosed
	}
	if a.store == nil {
		return b0state.State{}, false, nil
	}
	state, err := a.store.Committed()
	return state, err == nil, err
}

func (a *App) Info(context.Context, *abci.RequestInfo) (*abci.ResponseInfo, error) {
	state, exists, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp := &abci.ResponseInfo{Data: "Pluto B0 V2", Version: version.Number, AppVersion: 2}
	if exists {
		resp.LastBlockHeight = state.Height()
		hash := state.AppHash()
		resp.LastBlockAppHash = append([]byte(nil), hash[:]...)
	}
	return resp, nil
}

func (a *App) InitChain(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestInitChain) (*abci.ResponseInitChain, error) {
	if req == nil || req.InitialHeight != 1 || req.ChainId != a.chainID {
		return nil, ErrGenesisConflict
	}
	binary, state, err := b0genesis.Decode(req.AppStateBytes, req.ChainId)
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(binary, a.expectedGenesis) || state.AppHash() != a.expectedState.AppHash() {
		return nil, ErrGenesisConflict
	}
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()
	if a.closed {
		return nil, b0store.ErrClosed
	}
	if a.store == nil {
		store, err := b0store.Create(a.dbDir, binary, a.chainID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("persist B0 genesis: %w", err)
		}
		a.store = store
	} else {
		committed, err := a.store.Committed()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if committed.Height() != 0 || committed.AppHash() != state.AppHash() {
			return nil, ErrGenesisConflict
		}
	}
	hash := state.AppHash()
	return &abci.ResponseInitChain{AppHash: append([]byte(nil), hash[:]...)}, nil
}

func resultLog(code tx.Code) string {
	switch code {
	case 0:
		return "ok"
	case 1:
		return "malformed"
	case 2:
		return "wrong_chain"
	case 3:
		return "invalid_key"
	case 4:
		return "invalid_signature"
	case 5:
		return "invalid_amount"
	case 6:
		return "invalid_nonce"
	case 7:
		return "insufficient_balance"
	case 8:
		return "balance_overflow"
	case 9:
		return "account_limit"
	case 10:
		return "block_limit"
	default:
		panic("unrecognized B0 result code")
	}
}

func (a *App) CheckTx(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestCheckTx) (*abci.ResponseCheckTx, error) {
	state, exists, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if !exists {
		return nil, errors.New("B0 application is not initialized")
	}
	var raw []byte
	if req != nil {
		raw = req.Tx
	}
	_, code, err := b0state.EvaluateTrusted(state, raw)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &abci.ResponseCheckTx{Code: uint32(code), Log: resultLog(code), Codespace: resultCodespace}, nil
}

// committedAtHeightLocked must be called with the application read or write
// lock held. Holding that lock through proposal simulation prevents a Commit
// from changing the reference snapshot during height validation.
func (a *App) committedAtHeightLocked(height int64) (b0state.State, error) {
	if a.closed || a.store == nil {
		return b0state.State{}, errors.New("B0 application is not initialized")
	}
	state, err := a.store.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		return b0state.State{}, err
	}
	if state.Height() == math.MaxInt64 || height != state.Height()+1 {
		return b0state.State{}, errors.New("unexpected B0 height")
	}
	return state, nil
}

func blockOverLimit(raws [][]byte) bool {
	if len(raws) > maxBlockTxs {
		return true
	}
	var size uint64
	for _, raw := range raws {
		if uint64(len(raw)) > maxBlockBytes-size {
			return true
		}
		size += uint64(len(raw))
	}
	return false
}

func (a *App) PrepareProposal(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestPrepareProposal) (*abci.ResponsePrepareProposal, error) {
	if req == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("unexpected B0 proposal height")
	}
	a.mu.RLock()
	defer a.mu.RUnlock()
	candidate, err := a.committedAtHeightLocked(req.Height)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp := &abci.ResponsePrepareProposal{}
	if req.MaxTxBytes <= 0 {
		return resp, nil
	}
	budget := uint64(req.MaxTxBytes)
	if budget > maxBlockBytes {
		budget = maxBlockBytes
	}
	var used uint64
	for _, raw := range req.Txs {
		if len(resp.Txs) == maxBlockTxs {
			break
		}
		if uint64(len(raw)) > budget-used {
			continue
		}
		next, code, err := b0state.EvaluateTrusted(candidate, raw)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if code != tx.CodeOK {
			continue
		}
		resp.Txs = append(resp.Txs, raw)
		candidate = next
		used += uint64(len(raw))
	}
	return resp, nil
}

func (a *App) ProcessProposal(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestProcessProposal) (*abci.ResponseProcessProposal, error) {
	if req == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("missing B0 proposal")
	}
	a.mu.RLock()
	defer a.mu.RUnlock()
	candidate, err := a.committedAtHeightLocked(req.Height)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if blockOverLimit(req.Txs) {
		return &abci.ResponseProcessProposal{Status: abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT}, nil
	}
	for _, raw := range req.Txs {
		next, code, err := b0state.EvaluateTrusted(candidate, raw)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if code != tx.CodeOK {
			return &abci.ResponseProcessProposal{Status: abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT}, nil
		}
		candidate = next
	}
	return &abci.ResponseProcessProposal{Status: abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT}, nil
}

func (a *App) FinalizeBlock(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestFinalizeBlock) (*abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock, error) {
	if req == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("missing B0 finalized block")
	}
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()
	if _, err := a.committedAtHeightLocked(req.Height); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	overLimit := blockOverLimit(req.Txs)
	codes, hash, err := a.store.PrepareTrusted(req.Txs, overLimit)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp := &abci.ResponseFinalizeBlock{AppHash: append([]byte(nil), hash[:]...), TxResults: make([]*abci.ExecTxResult, len(req.Txs))}
	for i := range req.Txs {
		code := tx.Code(10)
		if !overLimit {
			code = codes[i]
		}
		resp.TxResults[i] = &abci.ExecTxResult{Code: uint32(code), Log: resultLog(code), Codespace: resultCodespace}
	}
	return resp, nil
}

func (a *App) Commit(context.Context, *abci.RequestCommit) (*abci.ResponseCommit, error) {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()
	if a.closed || a.store == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("B0 application is not initialized")
	}
	if err := a.store.Commit(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &abci.ResponseCommit{}, nil
}

func (a *App) Query(_ context.Context, req *abci.RequestQuery) (*abci.ResponseQuery, error) {
	state, exists, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if !exists {
		return nil, errors.New("B0 application is not initialized")
	}
	if req == nil || req.Path != "b0/account/v1" || len(req.Data) != 20 || req.Height != 0 || req.Prove {
		return &abci.ResponseQuery{Code: 1, Codespace: "pluto.query.v1", Log: "unsupported_query"}, nil
	}
	var address b0state.Address
	copy(address[:], req.Data)
	account, found := state.Account(address)
	value := []byte{0}
	if found {
		value = make([]byte, 17)
		value[0] = 1
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(value[1:9], account.Balance)
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(value[9:17], account.Nonce)
	}
	return &abci.ResponseQuery{Code: 0, Height: state.Height(), Value: value}, nil
}

func (*App) ExtendVote(context.Context, *abci.RequestExtendVote) (*abci.ResponseExtendVote, error) {
	return nil, errors.New("vote extensions are disabled")
}
func (*App) VerifyVoteExtension(context.Context, *abci.RequestVerifyVoteExtension) (*abci.ResponseVerifyVoteExtension, error) {
	return &abci.ResponseVerifyVoteExtension{Status: abci.ResponseVerifyVoteExtension_REJECT}, nil
}
func (*App) ListSnapshots(context.Context, *abci.RequestListSnapshots) (*abci.ResponseListSnapshots, error) {
	return &abci.ResponseListSnapshots{}, nil
}
func (*App) OfferSnapshot(context.Context, *abci.RequestOfferSnapshot) (*abci.ResponseOfferSnapshot, error) {
	return &abci.ResponseOfferSnapshot{Result: abci.ResponseOfferSnapshot_REJECT}, nil
}
func (*App) LoadSnapshotChunk(context.Context, *abci.RequestLoadSnapshotChunk) (*abci.ResponseLoadSnapshotChunk, error) {
	return nil, errors.New("snapshots are disabled")
}
func (*App) ApplySnapshotChunk(context.Context, *abci.RequestApplySnapshotChunk) (*abci.ResponseApplySnapshotChunk, error) {
	return &abci.ResponseApplySnapshotChunk{Result: abci.ResponseApplySnapshotChunk_ABORT}, nil
}

const goldenGenesis = "504c55544f47322100000002000b706c75746f2d62302d763100000001b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a0000000000000064"

func fixture(t *testing.T) ([]byte, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	binary, err := hex.DecodeString(goldenGenesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	transport, err := b0genesis.Encode(binary, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return binary, transport
}

func initialize(t *testing.T, path string) (*App, [32]byte) {
	t.Helper()
	binary, transport := fixture(t)
	a, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	resp, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{
		InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: transport,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var hash [32]byte
	copy(hash[:], resp.AppHash)
	return a, hash
}

func TestInitializeDurableIdempotentAndReproducible(t *testing.T) {
	path1 := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	path2 := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, hash := initialize(t, path1)
	b, secondHash := initialize(t, path2)
	defer b.Close()
	if hash != secondHash || hex.EncodeToString(hash[:]) != "e480c8b300c5ac1d662628f90334aae185ad38ed791c59ab14e3aa42abf083da" {
		t.Fatalf("cross-instance AppHash mismatch %x / %x", hash, secondHash)
	}
	state, exists, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil || !exists || state.Height() != 0 || state.TotalSupply() != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("committed state: exists=%v err=%v", exists, err)
	}
	accounts := state.Accounts()
	if len(accounts) != 1 || accounts[0].Balance != 100 || accounts[0].Nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("accounts: %+v", accounts)
	}
	binary, transport := fixture(t)
	retry, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: transport})
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(retry.AppHash, hash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("identical retry: %v", err)
	}
	conflicting := bytes.Clone(binary)
	conflicting[len(conflicting)-1]++
	conflictingTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(conflicting, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{
		InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: conflictingTransport,
	}); !errors.Is(err, ErrGenesisConflict) {
		t.Fatalf("committed genesis conflict: %v", err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if other, err := Open(path1, "pluto-b0-v1", conflicting); !errors.Is(err, ErrGenesisConflict) {
		if err == nil {
			_ = other.Close()
		}
		t.Fatalf("reopen with conflicting genesis: %v", err)
	}
	reopened, err := Open(path1, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer reopened.Close()
	info, err := reopened.Info(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 0 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, hash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("reopened Info: %+v err=%v", info, err)
	}
	retry, err = reopened.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: transport})
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(retry.AppHash, hash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("reopened identical retry: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestInitConflictAndInterruptedDirectory(t *testing.T) {
	binary, transport := fixture(t)
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer a.Close()
	changed := bytes.Clone(binary)
	changed[len(changed)-1]++
	changedTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(changed, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, req := range []*abci.RequestInitChain{
		{InitialHeight: 2, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: transport},
		{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "wrong-chain", AppStateBytes: transport},
		{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: changedTransport},
		{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: binary},
	} {
		if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), req); !errors.Is(err, ErrGenesisConflict) {
			t.Fatalf("conflicting InitChain error %v", err)
		}
	}
	if _, exists, err := a.Committed(); err != nil || exists {
		t.Fatalf("conflict changed state: exists=%v err=%v", exists, err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(path); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Fatalf("conflict created database: %v", err)
	}
	if err := os.Mkdir(path, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", binary); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("interrupted initialization directory was silently reset")
	}
	if _, err := b0store.OpenExisting(path, "pluto-b0-v1"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("incomplete B0 database was accepted")
	}
}

func TestMalformedTransactionDoesNotChangeCommittedState(t *testing.T) {
	a, hash := initialize(t, filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0"))
	defer a.Close()
	resp, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: []byte("arbitrary")})
	if err != nil || resp.Code != 1 || resp.Log != "malformed" || resp.Codespace != resultCodespace {
		t.Fatalf("malformed transaction result: %+v %v", resp, err)
	}
	info, err := a.Info(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 0 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, hash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("transaction changed committed state: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
}

func TestFinalizeBlockHeightAndPendingOrder(t *testing.T) {
	a, genesisHash := initialize(t, filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0"))
	defer a.Close()
	ctx := context.Background()
	for _, req := range []*abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{
		{Height: 0}, {Height: 2},
	} {
		if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, req); err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("accepted invalid height or nonempty block: %+v", req)
		}
	}
	first, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if bytes.Equal(first.AppHash, genesisHash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("height-one pending hash: %+v %v", first, err)
	}
	if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1}); !errors.Is(err, b0store.ErrPending) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate pending finalization: %v", err)
	}
	info, err := a.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 0 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, genesisHash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("pending state became committed before Commit: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	info, err = a.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 1 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, first.AppHash) {
		t.Fatalf("committed height-one state: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
	for _, height := range []int64{0, 1, 3} {
		if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: height}); err == nil {
			t.Fatalf("accepted stale or skipped height %d", height)
		}
	}
	if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 2}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("next height after Commit: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestConcurrentDuplicateFinalizeBlock(t *testing.T) {
	a, genesisHash := initialize(t, filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0"))
	defer a.Close()
	const callers = 8
	start := make(chan struct{})
	results := make(chan error, callers)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < callers; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			<-start
			_, err := a.FinalizeBlock(context.Background(), &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1})
			results <- err
		}()
	}
	close(start)
	wg.Wait()
	close(results)
	success, pending := 0, 0
	for err := range results {
		switch {
		case err == nil:
			success++
		case errors.Is(err, b0store.ErrPending):
			pending++
		default:
			t.Fatalf("unexpected callback result: %v", err)
		}
	}
	if success != 1 || pending != callers-1 {
		t.Fatalf("non-atomic duplicate handling: success=%d pending=%d", success, pending)
	}
	info, err := a.Info(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 0 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, genesisHash[:]) {
		t.Fatalf("concurrent finalization exposed pending state: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
}

func TestFinalizeCommitCallbackInterleaving(t *testing.T) {
	a, _ := initialize(t, filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0"))
	defer a.Close()
	type result struct {
		method string
		err    error
	}
	ready := make(chan struct{}, 2)
	results := make(chan result, 2)
	a.mu.Lock() // Queue both callbacks before allowing either write lock.
	go func() {
		ready <- struct{}{}
		_, err := a.FinalizeBlock(context.Background(), &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1})
		results <- result{method: "FinalizeBlock", err: err}
	}()
	go func() {
		ready <- struct{}{}
		_, err := a.Commit(context.Background(), &abci.RequestCommit{})
		results <- result{method: "Commit", err: err}
	}()
	<-ready
	<-ready
	a.mu.Unlock()
	first, second := <-results, <-results
	var finalizeErr, commitErr error
	for _, got := range []result{first, second} {
		if got.method == "FinalizeBlock" {
			finalizeErr = got.err
		} else {
			commitErr = got.err
		}
	}
	if finalizeErr != nil {
		t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock failed in callback ordering: %v", finalizeErr)
	}
	state, exists, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil || !exists {
		t.Fatalf("committed read: exists=%v err=%v", exists, err)
	}
	_, hasPending, err := a.store.Pending()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if commitErr == nil {
		if state.Height() != 1 || hasPending {
			t.Fatalf("successful Commit did not promote exactly one block: height=%d pending=%v", state.Height(), hasPending)
		}
	} else if errors.Is(commitErr, b0store.ErrNoPending) {
		if state.Height() != 0 || !hasPending {
			t.Fatalf("early Commit changed state: height=%d pending=%v", state.Height(), hasPending)
		}
	} else {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected Commit error: %v", commitErr)
	}
}

func TestEmptyBlockCheckpointRejectsDifferentGenesisAfterHeightZero(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, _ := initialize(t, path)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	binary, _ := fixture(t)
	changed := bytes.Clone(binary)
	changed[len(changed)-1]++ // Same chain ID; different allocation.
	if other, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", changed); !errors.Is(err, ErrGenesisConflict) {
		if err == nil {
			_ = other.Close()
		}
		t.Fatalf("alternate genesis accepted at height one: %v", err)
	}
	reopened, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("original genesis rejected at height one: %v", err)
	}
	defer reopened.Close()
}

func TestGenesisProcessKilledAfterSyncBeforeResponse(t *testing.T) {
	if os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_CHILD") == "1" {
		path := os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_DIR")
		marker := os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_MARKER")
		a, _ := initialize(t, path) // InitChain has returned after Pebble's synced Set.
		if err := os.WriteFile(marker, []byte("genesis sync completed"), 0600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		_ = a // Keep the database open; the parent sends SIGKILL.
		for {
			time.Sleep(time.Hour)
		}
	}

	dir := t.TempDir()
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "b0")
	marker := filepath.Join(dir, "after-sync.marker")
	cmd := exec.Command(os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestGenesisProcessKilledAfterSyncBeforeResponse$")
	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_CHILD=1",
		"PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_DIR="+path, "PLUTO_B0_GENESIS_KILL_MARKER="+marker)
	var childOutput bytes.Buffer
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &childOutput, &childOutput
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			_ = cmd.Wait()
		}
	})
	deadline := time.Now().Add(15 * time.Second)
	for {
		if _, err := os.Stat(marker); err == nil {
			break
		} else if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			_ = cmd.Wait()
			t.Fatalf("child did not complete synced genesis write: %s", childOutput.String())
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("child was not terminated by SIGKILL")
	}
	binary, transport := fixture(t)
	a, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reopen synced height-zero snapshot after SIGKILL: %v", err)
	}
	defer a.Close()
	info, err := a.Info(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 0 || hex.EncodeToString(info.LastBlockAppHash) != "e480c8b300c5ac1d662628f90334aae185ad38ed791c59ab14e3aa42abf083da" {
		t.Fatalf("recovered genesis Info: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
	response, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{
		InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: "pluto-b0-v1", AppStateBytes: transport,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(response.AppHash, info.LastBlockAppHash) {
		t.Fatalf("identical InitChain retry after SIGKILL: %+v %v", response, err)
	}
	t.Log("SIGKILL after synced genesis Set in standalone subprocess: complete height-zero state recovered; identical retry succeeded; no CometBFT call was involved")
}

func TestABCIPathsFailClosedAfterUncertainCommit(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, _ := initialize(t, path)
	if err := a.store.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	store, err := b0store.OpenExistingWithOptions(path, "pluto-b0-v1", b0store.Options{
		AfterSync: func() error { return errors.New("injected response loss after sync") },
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a.store = store // isolated test-only replacement before any concurrent call
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ambiguous Commit succeeded")
	}
	for name, call := range map[string]func() error{
		"Info":            func() error { _, err := a.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{}); return err },
		"Query":           func() error { _, err := a.Query(ctx, &abci.RequestQuery{}); return err },
		"CheckTx":         func() error { _, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: []byte{1}}); return err },
		"PrepareProposal": func() error { _, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1}); return err },
		"ProcessProposal": func() error { _, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1}); return err },
		"FinalizeBlock":   func() error { _, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1}); return err },
		"Commit":          func() error { _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); return err },
	} {
		if err := call(); !errors.Is(err, b0store.ErrUncertain) {
			t.Fatalf("%s exposed uncertain state: %v", name, err)
		}
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	genesis, _ := fixture(t)
	reopened, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer reopened.Close()
	info, err := reopened.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("validated reopen: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
}
