=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0app/transaction_test.go (11996 bytes; sha256 1fe33a67d5dfb142b778672077b0d5235efad154104ee777b649d8176fdce631) ===
package b0app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4/ecdsa"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
)

// Test-only scalar 1 is public and never used as a node or production key.
func signedB0(t testing.TB, chain string, receiver [20]byte, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
	return signedB0Key(t, 1, chain, receiver, amount, nonce)
}

func signedB0Key(t testing.TB, keyByte byte, chain string, receiver [20]byte, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	var secret [32]byte
	secret[31] = keyByte
	key := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secret[:])
	record := tx.Transaction{ChainID: chain, Receiver: receiver, Amount: amount, Nonce: nonce}
	copy(record.Sender[:], key.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	digest, err := tx.SigningDigest(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	signature := ecdsa.Sign(key, digest[:])
	r, s := signature.R(), signature.S()
	r.PutBytes(&record.R)
	s.PutBytes(&record.S)
	wire, err := tx.Encode(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return wire
}

func testAddresses(t testing.TB) (b0state.Address, b0state.Address) {
	t.Helper()
	var secretA, secretB [32]byte
	secretA[31], secretB[31] = 1, 2
	a, b := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secretA[:]), secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secretB[:])
	var ka, kb [33]byte
	copy(ka[:], a.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	copy(kb[:], b.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	addrA, err := tx.SenderAddress(ka)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	addrB, err := tx.SenderAddress(kb)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return addrA, addrB
}

func appWithBalance(t *testing.T, chain string, balance uint64) (*App, string, b0state.Address, b0state.Address) {
	t.Helper()
	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: balance}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	transport, err := b0genesis.Encode(genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: chain, AppStateBytes: transport}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return a, path, sender, receiver
}

func TestB0ABCITransactionLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
	a, path, sender, receiver := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
	ctx := context.Background()
	first := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
	second := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 10, 1)
	bad := bytes.Clone(first)
	bad[0] = 0
	for _, test := range []struct {
		raw  []byte
		code uint32
		log  string
	}{
		{first, 0, "ok"}, {second, 6, "invalid_nonce"}, {bad, 1, "malformed"},
	} {
		resp, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: test.raw})
		if err != nil || resp.Code != test.code || resp.Log != test.log || resp.Codespace != resultCodespace || resp.GasWanted != 0 || resp.GasUsed != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", resp, err)
		}
	}
	proposed, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 1000, Txs: [][]byte{bad, second, first, second}})
	if err != nil || len(proposed.Txs) != 2 || !bytes.Equal(proposed.Txs[0], first) || !bytes.Equal(proposed.Txs[1], second) {
		t.Fatalf("PrepareProposal: %+v %v", proposed, err)
	}
	for _, raws := range [][][]byte{{bad}, {second}, {first, first}} {
		resp, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: raws})
		if err != nil || resp.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
			t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal invalid: %+v %v", resp, err)
		}
	}
	accepted, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: proposed.Txs})
	if err != nil || accepted.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT {
		t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal valid: %+v %v", accepted, err)
	}
	final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{bad, first, first, second}})
	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
	}
	for i, want := range []uint32{1, 0, 6, 0} {
		if final.TxResults[i].Code != want || final.TxResults[i].Codespace != resultCodespace {
			t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock tx %d: %+v", i, final.TxResults[i])
		}
	}
	before, _, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil || before.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("pending became committed: %+v %v", before, err)
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("duplicate Commit")
	}
	for _, check := range []struct {
		addr           b0state.Address
		balance, nonce uint64
	}{
		{sender, 65, 2}, {receiver, 35, 0},
	} {
		q, err := a.Query(ctx, &abci.RequestQuery{Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: check.addr[:]})
		if err != nil || q.Code != 0 || q.Height != 1 || len(q.Value) != 17 || q.Value[0] != 1 || binary.BigEndian.Uint64(q.Value[1:9]) != check.balance || binary.BigEndian.Uint64(q.Value[9:17]) != check.nonce {
			t.Fatalf("Query: %+v %v", q, err)
		}
	}
	info, err := a.Info(ctx, &abci.RequestInfo{})
	if err != nil || info.LastBlockHeight != 1 || !bytes.Equal(info.LastBlockAppHash, final.AppHash) {
		t.Fatalf("Info: %+v %v", info, err)
	}
	replay, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: first, Type: abci.CheckTxType_Recheck})
	if err != nil || replay.Code != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("replay CheckTx: %+v %v", replay, err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	restarted, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("post-transfer restart: %v", err)
	}
	defer restarted.Close()
	state, exists, err := restarted.Committed()
	recoveredHash := state.AppHash()
	if err != nil || !exists || state.Height() != 1 || state.TotalSupply() != 100 || !bytes.Equal(recoveredHash[:], final.AppHash) {
		t.Fatalf("recovered state: %+v %v", state, err)
	}
	third := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 5, 2)
	response, err := restarted.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{first, third}})
	if err != nil || response.TxResults[0].Code != 6 || response.TxResults[1].Code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("post-restart execution: %+v %v", response, err)
	}
}

func TestB0ProposalBudgetAndLimitFallback(t *testing.T) {
	a, _, _, receiver := appWithBalance(t, "c", 6145)
	defer a.Close()
	ctx := context.Background()
	first := signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, 0)
	second := signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, 1)
	if len(first) != 146 {
		t.Fatalf("fixture width %d", len(first))
	}
	resp, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 146, Txs: [][]byte{bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, 195), first, second}})
	if err != nil || len(resp.Txs) != 1 || !bytes.Equal(resp.Txs[0], first) {
		t.Fatalf("skip oversized candidate: %+v %v", resp, err)
	}
	empty, err := a.PrepareProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestPrepareProposal{Height: 1, MaxTxBytes: 0, Txs: [][]byte{first}})
	if err != nil || len(empty.Txs) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("zero budget: %+v %v", empty, err)
	}
	// Every V20 record is signed, funded, and carries a sequential nonce.
	countBatch := make([][]byte, 6145)
	for i := range countBatch {
		countBatch[i] = signedB0(t, "c", receiver, 1, uint64(i))
	}
	if len(countBatch)*len(first) != 897170 {
		t.Fatal("count fixture byte total")
	}
	countInitial, _, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, countCodes, err := b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(countInitial, countBatch)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for i, code := range countCodes {
		if code != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("count fixture tx %d invalid: %d", i, code)
		}
	}
	countDigest := sha256.New()
	for _, wire := range countBatch {
		_, _ = countDigest.Write(wire)
	}
	t.Logf("V20 count chain=c K=1 genesis_supply=6145 tx_count=%d aggregate_bytes=%d sha256_concatenated=%x first_wire=%s last_wire=%s", len(countBatch), len(countBatch)*146, countDigest.Sum(nil), hex.EncodeToString(countBatch[0]), hex.EncodeToString(countBatch[len(countBatch)-1]))
	decision, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: countBatch})
	if err != nil || decision.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
		t.Fatalf("count ProcessProposal: %+v %v", decision, err)
	}
	final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: countBatch})
	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(countBatch) {
		t.Fatalf("count FinalizeBlock: %v", err)
	}
	for _, result := range final.TxResults {
		if result.Code != 10 || result.Log != "block_limit" {
			t.Fatal("count fallback result")
		}
	}
	if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	state, _, err := a.Committed()
	if err != nil || state.Height() != 1 || state.TotalSupply() != 6145 || len(state.Accounts()) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("count fallback state: %+v %v", state, err)
	}
	wantCount, err := countInitial.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil || state.AppHash() != wantCount.AppHash() {
		t.Fatalf("count fallback AppHash: %v", err)
	}
	longChain := strings.Repeat("c", 50)
	b, _, _, longReceiver := appWithBalance(t, longChain, 5378)
	defer b.Close()
	byteBatch := make([][]byte, 5378)
	for i := range byteBatch {
		byteBatch[i] = signedB0(t, longChain, longReceiver, 1, uint64(i))
	}
	if len(byteBatch[0]) != 195 || len(byteBatch)*195 != 1048710 {
		t.Fatal("byte fixture size")
	}
	byteInitial, _, err := b.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, byteCodes, err := b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(byteInitial, byteBatch)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for i, code := range byteCodes {
		if code != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("byte fixture tx %d invalid: %d", i, code)
		}
	}
	byteDigest := sha256.New()
	for _, wire := range byteBatch {
		_, _ = byteDigest.Write(wire)
	}
	t.Logf("V20 bytes chain_length=50 K=1 genesis_supply=5378 tx_count=%d aggregate_bytes=%d sha256_concatenated=%x first_wire=%s last_wire=%s", len(byteBatch), len(byteBatch)*195, byteDigest.Sum(nil), hex.EncodeToString(byteBatch[0]), hex.EncodeToString(byteBatch[len(byteBatch)-1]))
	decision, err = b.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: byteBatch})
	if err != nil || decision.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
		t.Fatalf("byte ProcessProposal: %+v %v", decision, err)
	}
	final, err = b.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: byteBatch})
	if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(byteBatch) {
		t.Fatalf("byte FinalizeBlock: %v", err)
	}
	for _, result := range final.TxResults {
		if result.Code != 10 {
			t.Fatal("byte fallback result")
		}
	}
	wantBytes, err := byteInitial.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pending, exists, err := b.store.Pending()
	if err != nil || !exists || pending.AppHash() != wantBytes.AppHash() {
		t.Fatalf("byte fallback AppHash: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestB0QueryAndNonceBoundary(t *testing.T) {
	a, _, _, receiver := appWithBalance(t, "pluto-b0-v1", 100)

		q, err := a.Query(ctx, req)
		if err != nil || q.Code != 1 || q.Log != "unsupported_query" || q.Codespace != "pluto.query.v1" || len(q.Value) != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("unsupported Query: %+v %v", q, err)
		}
	}
	q, err := a.Query(ctx, &abci.RequestQuery{Path: "b0/account/v1", Data: receiver[:]})
	if err != nil || q.Code != 0 || !bytes.Equal(q.Value, []byte{0}) {
		t.Fatalf("missing account: %+v %v", q, err)
	}
	// Nonce overflow is exercised by the accepted state-engine fixture.
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0app/vector_test.go (13677 bytes; sha256 d3fdf4d2ab418487373990e75b5481be09629d3282fac5bbaf6f4edce775e899) ===
package b0app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"math"
	"math/big"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"sort"
	"testing"

	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble"
	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"

func TestB0AcceptedSingleTransactionVectors(t *testing.T) {
	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
	_, receiver := testAddresses(t)
	valid := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
	clone := func() []byte { return bytes.Clone(valid) }
	badR := clone()
	clear(badR[len(badR)-64 : len(badR)-32])
	highS := clone()
	low := new(big.Int).SetBytes(highS[len(highS)-32:])
	new(big.Int).Sub(secp256k1.Params().N, low).FillBytes(highS[len(highS)-32:])
	modifiedAmount := clone()
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(modifiedAmount[len(modifiedAmount)-80:len(modifiedAmount)-72], 26)
	badKey := clone()
	badKey[12+len(chain)] = 4
	badMagic := clone()
	badMagic[0] = 0
	badVersion := clone()
	badVersion[9] = 2
	short := clone()[:len(valid)-1]
	long := append(clone(), 0)
	badLength0 := clone()
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(badLength0[10:12], 0)
	badLength51 := clone()
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(badLength51[10:12], 51)
	badChainByte := clone()
	badChainByte[12] = 0xff
	uncompressedKey := clone()
	keyEnd := 12 + len(chain) + 33
	uncompressedKey = append(append([]byte(nil), uncompressedKey[:keyEnd]...), append(bytes.Repeat([]byte{0}, 32), uncompressedKey[keyEnd:]...)...)
	zeroAndBadSignature := clone()
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(zeroAndBadSignature[len(zeroAndBadSignature)-80:len(zeroAndBadSignature)-72], 0)
	zeroAndBadSignature[len(zeroAndBadSignature)-33] ^= 1
	badBinding := clone()
	var bSecret [32]byte
	bSecret[31] = 2
	copy(badBinding[12+len(chain):12+len(chain)+33], secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(bSecret[:]).PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	cases := []struct {
		id       string
		raw      []byte
		code     tx.Code
		eligible bool
	}{
		{"V01", valid, 0, true}, {"V02", badR, 4, false}, {"V03", highS, 4, false},
		{"V04", modifiedAmount, 4, true}, {"V05", signedB0(t, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0), 2, true},
		{"V06", badKey, 3, true},
		{"V07-bad-magic", badMagic, 1, false}, {"V07-version", badVersion, 1, false},
		{"V07-short", short, 1, false}, {"V07-trailing", long, 1, false},
		{"V07-chain-length-zero", badLength0, 1, false}, {"V07-chain-length-51", badLength51, 1, false},
		{"V07-chain-byte", badChainByte, 1, false},
		{"V07-65-byte-key", uncompressedKey, 1, false},
		{"V08", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 1), 6, true},
		{"V10", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 101, 0), 7, true},
		{"V11", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 0, 0), 5, true},
		{"V12", signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 100, 0), 0, true},
		{"V16-binding", badBinding, 4, true},
		{"V16-unfunded-B", signedB0Key(t, 2, chain, receiver, 1, 0), 7, true},
		{"V24", zeroAndBadSignature, 5, true},
	}
	for _, vector := range cases {
		t.Run(vector.id, func(t *testing.T) {
			a, _, _, _ := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
			defer a.Close()
			ctx := context.Background()
			id, idErr := tx.TxID(vector.raw)
			if (idErr == nil) != vector.eligible {
				t.Fatalf("TxID eligibility: %v", idErr)
			}
			check, err := a.CheckTx(ctx, &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: vector.raw})
			if err != nil || check.Code != uint32(vector.code) || check.Log != resultLog(vector.code) {
				t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", check, err)
			}
			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{vector.raw}})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			wanted := abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT
			if vector.code == 0 {
				wanted = abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT
			}
			if proposal.Status != wanted {
				t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal: %+v", proposal)
			}
			before, _, err := a.Committed()
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: [][]byte{vector.raw}})
			if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != 1 || final.TxResults[0].Code != uint32(vector.code) || final.TxResults[0].Log != resultLog(vector.code) {
				t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
			}
			pending, exists, err := a.store.Pending()
			if err != nil || !exists {
				t.Fatalf("pending: %v", err)
			}
			expected, code, err := b0state.Evaluate(before, vector.raw)
			if err != nil || code != vector.code {
				t.Fatalf("reference Evaluate code=%d err=%v", code, err)
			}
			expected, err = expected.AdvanceBlock()
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			pendingHash := pending.AppHash()
			if pendingHash != expected.AppHash() || !bytes.Equal(final.AppHash, pendingHash[:]) {
				t.Fatalf("pending AppHash mismatch")
			}
			if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			committed, _, err := a.Committed()
			if err != nil || committed.AppHash() != expected.AppHash() {
				t.Fatalf("committed state: %v", err)
			}
			hash := committed.AppHash()
			t.Logf("%s wire_hex=%s expected_code=%d txid_eligible=%t pluto_txid=%x comet_hash=%x committed_apphash_h1=%x", vector.id, hex.EncodeToString(vector.raw), vector.code, vector.eligible, id, tx.CometHash(vector.raw), hash)
		})
	}
}

// Inject only a complete, integrity-valid synthetic state into a disposable
// D-024 store. This is an ABCI contract fixture, not a real consensus node.
func syntheticApp(t testing.TB, genesisBalance uint64, state b0state.State) *App {
	t.Helper()
	sender, _ := testAddresses(t)
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis("pluto-b0-v1", []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: genesisBalance}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	store, err := b0store.Create(path, genesis, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	logical, err := state.LogicalBytes()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hash := state.AppHash()
	record := append(append([]byte("PLUTOS2!"), logical...), hash[:]...)
	db, err := pebble.Open(path, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Set([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"), record, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a, err := Open(path, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return a
}

func TestB0AcceptedSyntheticBoundaryVectors(t *testing.T) {
	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
	maxNonceState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 100, Nonce: math.MaxUint64}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	zeroBalanceState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 1, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Nonce: math.MaxUint64}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 1}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	accounts := make([]b0state.Account, 65536)
	accounts[0] = b0state.Account{Address: sender, Balance: 1}
	for i := 1; i < len(accounts); i++ {
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(accounts[i].Address[16:], uint32(i-1))
	}
	sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return bytes.Compare(accounts[i].Address[:], accounts[j].Address[:]) < 0 })
	capState, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, 1, accounts)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, item := range []struct {
		id      string
		state   b0state.State
		balance uint64
		nonce   uint64
		amount  uint64
		want    uint32
	}{
		{"V17", maxNonceState, 100, math.MaxUint64, 1, 6},
		{"V19", capState, 1, 0, 1, 9},
		{"V25", zeroBalanceState, 1, math.MaxUint64, 1, 6},
	} {
		t.Run(item.id, func(t *testing.T) {
			a := syntheticApp(t, item.balance, item.state)
			defer a.Close()
			raw := signedB0(t, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, item.amount, item.nonce)
			check, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: raw})
			if err != nil || check.Code != item.want {
				t.Fatalf("CheckTx: %+v %v", check, err)
			}
			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(context.Background(), &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{raw}})
			if err != nil || proposal.Status != abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT {
				t.Fatalf("ProcessProposal: %+v %v", proposal, err)
			}
			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(context.Background(), &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 2, Txs: [][]byte{raw}})
			if err != nil || final.TxResults[0].Code != item.want {
				t.Fatalf("FinalizeBlock: %+v %v", final, err)
			}
			pending, _, err := a.store.Pending()
			beforeAccounts, afterAccounts := item.state.Accounts(), pending.Accounts()
			if err != nil || !slices.Equal(beforeAccounts, afterAccounts) || pending.TotalSupply() != item.state.TotalSupply() || pending.Height() != 2 {
				t.Fatalf("state changed on failed tx: %v", err)
			}
			t.Logf("%s synthetic_integrity_valid_h1=%x wire_hex=%s code=%d pending_apphash_h2=%x", item.id, item.state.AppHash(), hex.EncodeToString(raw), item.want, pending.AppHash())
		})
	}
}

func TestB0AcceptedOrderedVectors(t *testing.T) {
	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
	first := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 25, 0)
	second := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 30, 1)
	insufficient := signedB0(t, chain, receiver, 101, 0)
	self := signedB0(t, chain, sender, 25, 0)
	for _, item := range []struct {
		id                         string
		raws                       [][]byte
		codes                      []uint32
		balanceA, nonceA, balanceB uint64
		accept                     bool
	}{
		{"V13", [][]byte{first, first}, []uint32{0, 6}, 75, 1, 25, false},
		{"V14", [][]byte{first, second}, []uint32{0, 0}, 45, 2, 55, true},
		{"V15", [][]byte{{1}, first}, []uint32{1, 0}, 75, 1, 25, false},
		{"V21", [][]byte{insufficient, first}, []uint32{7, 0}, 75, 1, 25, false},
		{"V22", [][]byte{self}, []uint32{0}, 100, 1, 0, true},
	} {
		t.Run(item.id, func(t *testing.T) {
			a, _, _, _ := appWithBalance(t, chain, 100)
			defer a.Close()
			ctx := context.Background()
			proposal, err := a.ProcessProposal(ctx, &abci.RequestProcessProposal{Height: 1, Txs: item.raws})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			want := abci.ResponseProcessProposal_REJECT
			if item.accept {
				want = abci.ResponseProcessProposal_ACCEPT
			}
			if proposal.Status != want {
				t.Fatalf("proposal status: %+v", proposal)
			}
			final, err := a.FinalizeBlock(ctx, &abci.RequestFinalizeBlock{Height: 1, Txs: item.raws})
			if err != nil || len(final.TxResults) != len(item.codes) {
				t.Fatalf("final: %+v %v", final, err)
			}
			for i, code := range item.codes {
				if final.TxResults[i].Code != code {
					t.Fatalf("tx %d code %d want %d", i, final.TxResults[i].Code, code)
				}
			}
			if _, err := a.Commit(ctx, &abci.RequestCommit{}); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			state, _, err := a.Committed()
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			aAccount, _ := state.Account(sender)
			bAccount, bFound := state.Account(receiver)
			if aAccount.Balance != item.balanceA || aAccount.Nonce != item.nonceA || bAccount.Balance != item.balanceB || state.TotalSupply() != 100 {
				t.Fatalf("accounts: A=%+v B=%+v", aAccount, bAccount)
			}
			if item.id == "V22" && bFound {
				t.Fatal("self transfer created receiver")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestB0V18GenesisSupplyOverflowAndV23CorruptRecord(t *testing.T) {
	sender, receiver := testAddresses(t)
	const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	a, err := Open(path, chain, genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Construct a canonical-looking two-allocation binary whose checked
	// total-supply sum overflows uint64. The accepted encoder must reject it.
	bad := make([]byte, 8+4+2+len(chain)+4+2*(20+8))
	copy(bad[:8], "PLUTOG2!")
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(bad[8:12], 2)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(bad[12:14], uint16(len(chain)))
	copy(bad[14:], chain)
	p := 14 + len(chain)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(bad[p:p+4], 2)
	p += 4
	copy(bad[p:p+20], sender[:])
	p += 20
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(bad[p:p+8], math.MaxUint64)
	p += 8
	copy(bad[p:p+20], receiver[:])
	p += 20
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(bad[p:p+8], 1)
	encoded, err := json.Marshal(base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(bad))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := a.InitChain(context.Background(), &abci.RequestInitChain{InitialHeight: 1, ChainId: chain, AppStateBytes: encoded}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("V18 overflowing genesis accepted")
	}
	if _, exists, err := a.Committed(); err != nil || exists {
		t.Fatalf("V18 created state: exists=%t err=%v", exists, err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	store, err := b0store.Create(path, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	db, err := pebble.Open(path, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	value, closer, err := db.Get([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	corrupt := bytes.Clone(value)
	if err := closer.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	corrupt[len(corrupt)-33] ^= 1 // logical nonce byte; retain old digest
	if err := db.Set([]byte("pluto/committed-state/v2"), corrupt, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if reopened, err := Open(path, chain, genesis); err == nil {
		_ = reopened.Close()
		t.Fatal("V23 corrupt complete record accepted")
	}
	t.Log("V18 InitChain rejected overflowing supply without creating a store; V23 application Open rejected modified logical state with unchanged digest")
}

package b0app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"testing"

	abci "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/types"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
)

func BenchmarkB0CheckTxCost(b *testing.B) {
	sender, receiver := testAddresses(b)
	valid := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, 25, 0)
	self := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", sender, 25, 0)
	wrong := signedB0(b, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0)
	zero := signedB0(b, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, 0, 0)
	badScalar := bytes.Clone(valid)
	clear(badScalar[len(badScalar)-64 : len(badScalar)-32])
	badECDSA := bytes.Clone(valid)
	badECDSA[len(badECDSA)-80] ^= 1
	workloads := []struct {
		name string
		raw  []byte
		code uint32
	}{
		{"Malformed", []byte{1}, 1}, {"WrongChain", wrong, 2}, {"ZeroAmount", zero, 5},
		{"NoncanonicalScalar", badScalar, 4}, {"InvalidECDSA", badECDSA, 4},
		{"SelfTransfer", self, 0}, {"DistinctTransfer", valid, 0},
	}
	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
		accounts := []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 1_000_000}}
		if k > 1 {
			accounts = append(accounts, b0state.Account{Address: receiver, Balance: 1_000_000})
		}
		for i := 0; len(accounts) < k; i++ {
			var addr b0state.Address
			binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(addr[16:], uint32(i))
			if addr == sender || addr == receiver {
				continue
			}
			accounts = append(accounts, b0state.Account{Address: addr, Balance: 1_000_000})
		}
		sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return bytes.Compare(accounts[i].Address[:], accounts[j].Address[:]) < 0 })
		state, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", 1, uint64(k)*1_000_000, accounts)
		if err != nil {
			b.Fatal(err)
		}
		a := syntheticApp(b, uint64(k)*1_000_000, state)
		for _, work := range workloads {
			b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/%s", k, work.name), func(b *testing.B) {
				req := &abci.RequestCheckTx{Tx: work.raw}
				response, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), req)
				if err != nil || response.Code != work.code {
					b.Fatalf("fixture: %+v %v", response, err)
				}
				b.ReportAllocs()
				b.ResetTimer()
				for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
					response, err := a.CheckTx(context.Background(), req)
					if err != nil || response.Code != work.code {
						b.Fatal(response, err)
					}
				}
				b.StopTimer()
				b.ReportMetric(float64(26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k), "state-logical-bytes/op")
			})
		}
		if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
			b.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0state/state.go (6591 bytes; sha256 4068cf7f77e77e16f15776b1c1c3d361588807d17fa115f2b200f34dfb652c32) ===
// Package b0state implements the isolated B0 V2 logical account state.
// It is not connected to the Phase 02 ABCI application.
package b0state

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/binary"
	"errors"
	"math"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
)

const (
	Version      uint32 = 2
	MaxAccounts         = 65536
	stateDomain         = "PLUTO/B0/STATE/V2\x00"
	genesisMagic        = "PLUTOG2!"
	maxLogical          = 4 + 2 + 50 + 8 + 8 + 4 + MaxAccounts*36
)

type Address [20]byte

type Account struct {
	Address Address
	Balance uint64
	Nonce   uint64
}

// State has no writable exported fields. All transitions return fresh account
// slices, so copying a State value cannot mutate an earlier snapshot.
type State struct {
	chainID  string
	height   int64
	supply   uint64
	accounts []Account
	hash     [32]byte
	// trusted is set only by constructors which check every invariant and
	// compute (or verify) the complete commitment. State is immutable after
	// construction; a zero State cannot enter the fast evaluation path.
	trusted bool
}

var ErrState = errors.New("invalid B0 V2 state")
var ErrGenesis = errors.New("invalid B0 V2 genesis")
var ErrHeightOverflow = errors.New("B0 V2 height overflow")

func validChainID(s string) bool {
	if len(s) < 1 || len(s) > tx.MaxChainID {
		return false
	}
	for i := 0; i < len(s); i++ {
		b := s[i]
		if !((b >= 'a' && b <= 'z') || (b >= 'A' && b <= 'Z') ||
			(b >= '0' && b <= '9') || b == '_' || b == '-' || b == '.') {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// NewState validates a canonical, sorted logical snapshot. It is also useful
// for explicit integrity-valid boundary fixtures; genesis uses ParseGenesis.
func NewState(chainID string, height int64, supply uint64, accounts []Account) (State, error) {
	if !validChainID(chainID) || height < 0 || len(accounts) < 1 || len(accounts) > MaxAccounts {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	copyAccounts := append([]Account(nil), accounts...)
	var sum uint64
	for i, account := range copyAccounts {
		if i > 0 && bytes.Compare(copyAccounts[i-1].Address[:], account.Address[:]) >= 0 {
			return State{}, ErrState
		}
		if account.Balance > math.MaxUint64-sum {
			return State{}, ErrState
		}
		sum += account.Balance
	}
	if sum != supply {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	s := State{chainID: chainID, height: height, supply: supply, accounts: copyAccounts, trusted: true}
	s.hash = sha256.Sum256(append([]byte(stateDomain), s.marshalLogical()...))
	return s, nil
}

func (s State) ChainID() string     { return s.chainID }
func (s State) Height() int64       { return s.height }
func (s State) TotalSupply() uint64 { return s.supply }
func (s State) AppHash() [32]byte   { return s.hash }
func (s State) Accounts() []Account { return append([]Account(nil), s.accounts...) }
func (s State) Account(address Address) (Account, bool) {
	i, found := s.lookup(address)
	if !found {
		return Account{Address: address}, false
	}
	return s.accounts[i], true
}
func (s State) lookup(address Address) (int, bool) {
	lo, hi := 0, len(s.accounts)
	for lo < hi {
		mid := int(uint(lo+hi) >> 1)
		if bytes.Compare(s.accounts[mid].Address[:], address[:]) < 0 {
			lo = mid + 1
		} else {
			hi = mid
		}
	}
	return lo, lo < len(s.accounts) && s.accounts[lo].Address == address
}

// LogicalBytes is the exact D-021 state serialization, excluding domain and
// AppHash. A returned slice is independent of State storage.
func (s State) LogicalBytes() ([]byte, error) {
	if err := s.Validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.marshalLogical(), nil
}

func (s State) marshalLogical() []byte {
	n := len(s.chainID)
	out := make([]byte, 4+2+n+8+8+4+36*len(s.accounts))
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(out[:4], Version)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint16(out[4:6], uint16(n))
	copy(out[6:6+n], s.chainID)
	p := 6 + n
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(out[p:p+8], uint64(s.height))
	p += 8
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(out[p:p+8], s.supply)
	p += 8
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(out[p:p+4], uint32(len(s.accounts)))
	p += 4
	for _, a := range s.accounts {
		copy(out[p:p+20], a.Address[:])
		p += 20
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(out[p:p+8], a.Balance)
		p += 8
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(out[p:p+8], a.Nonce)
		p += 8
	}
	return out
}

// Validate checks all logical invariants and the stored application digest.
func (s State) Validate() error {
	if !validChainID(s.chainID) || s.height < 0 || len(s.accounts) < 1 || len(s.accounts) > MaxAccounts {
		return ErrState
	}
	var sum uint64
	for i, a := range s.accounts {
		if i > 0 && bytes.Compare(s.accounts[i-1].Address[:], a.Address[:]) >= 0 {
			return ErrState
		}
		if a.Balance > math.MaxUint64-sum {
			return ErrState
		}
		sum += a.Balance
	}
	if sum != s.supply || sha256.Sum256(append([]byte(stateDomain), s.marshalLogical()...)) != s.hash {
		return ErrState
	}
	return nil
}

// DecodeLogical accepts only exact canonical bytes and an independently
// provided AppHash. The chain context is explicit and must match.
func DecodeLogical(data []byte, hash [32]byte, expectedChainID string) (State, error) {
	const fixed = 4 + 2 + 8 + 8 + 4
	if len(data) < fixed+1+36 || len(data) > maxLogical || binary.BigEndian.Uint32(data[:4]) != Version {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	n := int(binary.BigEndian.Uint16(data[4:6]))
	if n < 1 || n > 50 || len(data) < fixed+n+36 {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	chain := string(data[6 : 6+n])
	if !validChainID(chain) || chain != expectedChainID {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	p := 6 + n
	height := binary.BigEndian.Uint64(data[p : p+8])
	p += 8
	if height > math.MaxInt64 {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	supply := binary.BigEndian.Uint64(data[p : p+8])
	p += 8
	k := uint64(binary.BigEndian.Uint32(data[p : p+4]))
	p += 4
	if k < 1 || k > MaxAccounts || uint64(len(data)) != uint64(p)+36*k {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	accounts := make([]Account, int(k))
	for i := range accounts {
		copy(accounts[i].Address[:], data[p:p+20])
		p += 20
		accounts[i].Balance = binary.BigEndian.Uint64(data[p : p+8])
		p += 8
		accounts[i].Nonce = binary.BigEndian.Uint64(data[p : p+8])
		p += 8
	}
	s, err := NewState(chain, int64(height), supply, accounts)
	if err != nil || s.hash != hash {
		return State{}, ErrState
	}
	return s, nil
}

// AdvanceBlock changes only the height and commitment. MaxInt64 cannot wrap.
func (s State) AdvanceBlock() (State, error) {
	if err := s.Validate(); err != nil {
		return State{}, err
	}
	if s.height == math.MaxInt64 {
		return State{}, ErrHeightOverflow
	}
	next := s
	next.height++
	next.hash = sha256.Sum256(append([]byte(stateDomain), next.marshalLogical()...))
	return next, nil
}
=== END VERBATIM internal/b0state/state.go ===

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0state/transition.go (4144 bytes; sha256 cf52ce4bdf377c1ac25cdefde82b1cf95ad9858747d81fd073434e44a003f7f7) ===
package b0state

import (
	"math"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
)

const (
	CodeInvalidNonce        tx.Code = 6
	CodeInsufficientBalance tx.Code = 7
	CodeBalanceOverflow     tx.Code = 8
	CodeAccountLimit        tx.Code = 9
)

// Evaluate runs the accepted stateless validator first, then the ordered
// state checks. A nonzero code returns the original State without mutation.
// An error indicates an invalid internal state, never an ordinary bad tx.
func Evaluate(s State, raw []byte) (State, tx.Code, error) {
	if err := s.Validate(); err != nil {
		return State{}, 0, err
	}
	return EvaluateTrusted(s, raw)
}

// EvaluateTrusted is for immutable states produced by NewState, ParseGenesis,
// DecodeLogical, or a prior successful transition. The store verifies its
// complete snapshot on load; this path avoids an O(K) hash for cheap-invalid
// wire input. A malformed in-memory State is an internal error, not a tx code.
func EvaluateTrusted(s State, raw []byte) (State, tx.Code, error) {
	if !s.trusted {
		return State{}, 0, ErrState
	}
	if code := tx.Validate(raw, s.chainID); code != tx.CodeOK {
		return s, code, nil
	}
	record, err := tx.Parse(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return State{}, 0, err
	} // unreachable after CodeOK
	sender20, err := tx.SenderAddress(record.Sender)
	if err != nil {
		return State{}, 0, err
	} // unreachable after CodeOK
	var sender, receiver Address
	copy(sender[:], sender20[:])
	copy(receiver[:], record.Receiver[:])
	senderIndex, senderFound := s.lookup(sender)
	senderAccount := Account{Address: sender}
	if senderFound {
		senderAccount = s.accounts[senderIndex]
	}
	if record.Nonce != senderAccount.Nonce || senderAccount.Nonce == math.MaxUint64 {
		return s, CodeInvalidNonce, nil
	}
	if senderAccount.Balance < record.Amount {
		return s, CodeInsufficientBalance, nil
	}
	if sender == receiver {
		accounts := append([]Account(nil), s.accounts...)
		accounts[senderIndex].Nonce++ // sufficient balance implies sender exists
		next, err := NewState(s.chainID, s.height, s.supply, accounts)
		return next, tx.CodeOK, err
	}
	receiverIndex, receiverFound := s.lookup(receiver)
	receiverAccount := Account{Address: receiver}
	if receiverFound {
		receiverAccount = s.accounts[receiverIndex]
	}
	if receiverAccount.Balance > math.MaxUint64-record.Amount {
		return s, CodeBalanceOverflow, nil
	}
	if !receiverFound && len(s.accounts) == MaxAccounts {
		return s, CodeAccountLimit, nil
	}
	accounts := make([]Account, len(s.accounts))
	copy(accounts, s.accounts)
	accounts[senderIndex].Balance -= record.Amount
	accounts[senderIndex].Nonce++
	if receiverFound {
		accounts[receiverIndex].Balance += record.Amount
	} else {
		accounts = append(accounts, Account{})
		copy(accounts[receiverIndex+1:], accounts[receiverIndex:len(accounts)-1])
		accounts[receiverIndex] = Account{Address: receiver, Balance: record.Amount}
	}
	next, err := NewState(s.chainID, s.height, s.supply, accounts)
	return next, tx.CodeOK, err
}

// SimulateOrderedTrusted starts from a verified immutable snapshot and uses
// the same transition logic as Evaluate without repeated full-state checks.
func SimulateOrderedTrusted(s State, raws [][]byte) (State, []tx.Code, error) {
	if !s.trusted {
		return State{}, nil, ErrState
	}
	candidate := s
	codes := make([]tx.Code, 0, len(raws))
	for _, raw := range raws {
		next, code, err := EvaluateTrusted(candidate, raw)
		if err != nil {
			return State{}, nil, err
		}
		candidate = next
		codes = append(codes, code)
	}
	return candidate, codes, nil
}

// SimulateOrdered evaluates every byte string in supplied order against all
// prior successes; failures leave the candidate unchanged and do not stop it.
// It does not enforce an ABCI block envelope or advance height.
func SimulateOrdered(s State, raws [][]byte) (State, []tx.Code, error) {
	if err := s.Validate(); err != nil {
		return State{}, nil, err
	}
	candidate := s
	codes := make([]tx.Code, 0, len(raws))
	for _, raw := range raws {
		next, code, err := Evaluate(candidate, raw)
		if err != nil {
			return State{}, nil, err
		}
		candidate = next
		codes = append(codes, code)
	}
	return candidate, codes, nil
}

package b0state

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"math"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4/ecdsa"
)

const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"

type fixture struct {
	ID       string `json:"id"`
	Wire     string `json:"wire_hex"`
	Sender   string `json:"sender_address_hex"`
	Receiver string `json:"receiver_hex"`
}

func baseFixture(t testing.TB) (Address, Address, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	data, err := os.ReadFile("../../research/evidence/phase_03b/golden_vectors.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var vectors []fixture
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &vectors); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(vectors) < 1 || vectors[0].ID != "V01" {
		t.Fatal("missing V01")
	}
	var a, b Address
	ab, _ := hex.DecodeString(vectors[0].Sender)
	bb, _ := hex.DecodeString(vectors[0].Receiver)
	copy(a[:], ab)
	copy(b[:], bb)
	wire, err := hex.DecodeString(vectors[0].Wire)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return a, b, wire
}

func genesis(t *testing.T) (State, Address, Address, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	a, b, wire := baseFixture(t)
	encoded, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err := ParseGenesis(encoded, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s, a, b, wire
}

func signed(t testing.TB, keyByte byte, receiver Address, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	var secret [32]byte
	secret[31] = keyByte // explicitly public TEST ONLY scalar 1 or 2.
	key := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secret[:])
	var record tx.Transaction
	record.ChainID = chain
	copy(record.Sender[:], key.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	copy(record.Receiver[:], receiver[:])
	record.Amount, record.Nonce = amount, nonce
	digest, err := tx.SigningDigest(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sig := ecdsa.Sign(key, digest[:])
	r, s := sig.R(), sig.S()
	r.PutBytes(&record.R)
	s.PutBytes(&record.S)
	wire, err := tx.Encode(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return wire
}

func equalState(t *testing.T, got, want State) {
	t.Helper()
	a, e1 := got.LogicalBytes()
	b, e2 := want.LogicalBytes()
	if e1 != nil || e2 != nil || !bytes.Equal(a, b) || got.AppHash() != want.AppHash() {
		t.Fatalf("states differ: %v %v", e1, e2)
	}
}

func TestGenesisCanonicalAndBoundaries(t *testing.T) {
	s, a, _, _ := genesis(t)
	if s.Height() != 0 || s.TotalSupply() != 100 || len(s.Accounts()) != 1 || s.Accounts()[0].Nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatal("genesis state")
	}
	for _, id := range []string{"c", string(bytes.Repeat([]byte{'c'}, 50))} {
		wire, err := EncodeGenesis(id, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 1}})
		if err != nil || len(wire) != 18+len(id)+28 {
			t.Fatal("genesis length", err)
		}
		parsed, err := ParseGenesis(wire, id)
		if err != nil || parsed.ChainID() != id || parsed.Height() != 0 {
			t.Fatal("genesis parse", err)
		}
	}
	copyAccounts := s.Accounts()
	copyAccounts[0].Balance = 0
	if s.Accounts()[0].Balance != 100 {
		t.Fatal("account slice escaped")
	}
	logical, err := s.LogicalBytes()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	decoded, err := DecodeLogical(logical, s.AppHash(), chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	equalState(t, decoded, s)
}

func TestIndependentGoldenVectors(t *testing.T) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile("../../research/evidence/phase_03c/state_vectors.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var vectors struct {
		GenesisWire  string `json:"genesis_wire_hex"`
		TransferWire string `json:"transfer_wire_hex"`
		SelfWire     string `json:"self_wire_hex"`
		Genesis      struct {
			Logical string `json:"logical_hex"`
			Hash    string `json:"apphash_hex"`
		} `json:"genesis"`
		PostTransfer struct {
			Logical string `json:"logical_hex"`
			Hash    string `json:"apphash_hex"`
		} `json:"post_transfer"`
		PostSelf struct {
			Logical string `json:"logical_hex"`
			Hash    string `json:"apphash_hex"`
		} `json:"post_self_transfer"`
		PostEmpty struct {
			Logical string `json:"logical_hex"`
			Hash    string `json:"apphash_hex"`
		} `json:"post_empty_block"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &vectors); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, a, _, _ := genesis(t)
	genesisWire, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil || hex.EncodeToString(genesisWire) != vectors.GenesisWire {
		t.Fatal("genesis wire", err)
	}
	check := func(label string, state State, logicalHex, hashHex string) {
		t.Helper()
		logical, err := state.LogicalBytes()
		if err != nil || hex.EncodeToString(logical) != logicalHex || hex.EncodeToString(state.hash[:]) != hashHex {
			t.Fatal(label, err)
		}
	}
	check("genesis", s, vectors.Genesis.Logical, vectors.Genesis.Hash)
	transfer, err := hex.DecodeString(vectors.TransferWire)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, code, err := Evaluate(s, transfer)
	if err != nil || code != 0 {
		t.Fatal(code, err)
	}
	s, err = s.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	check("transfer", s, vectors.PostTransfer.Logical, vectors.PostTransfer.Hash)
	self, err := hex.DecodeString(vectors.SelfWire)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, code, err = Evaluate(s, self)
	if err != nil || code != 0 {
		t.Fatal(code, err)
	}
	s, err = s.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	check("self", s, vectors.PostSelf.Logical, vectors.PostSelf.Hash)
	s, err = s.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	check("empty", s, vectors.PostEmpty.Logical, vectors.PostEmpty.Hash)
}

func TestGenesisRejections(t *testing.T) {
	_, a, b, _ := genesis(t)
	good, _ := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	invalid := map[string]func([]byte) []byte{
		"magic":             func(w []byte) []byte { w[0] = 0; return w },
		"version":           func(w []byte) []byte { w[11] = 3; return w },
		"zero chain length": func(w []byte) []byte { w[13] = 0; return w },
		"long chain length": func(w []byte) []byte { w[13] = 51; return w },
		"bad chain byte":    func(w []byte) []byte { w[14] = 0xff; return w },
		"zero count":        func(w []byte) []byte { binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(w[14+len(chain):], 0); return w },
		"over cap":          func(w []byte) []byte { binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(w[14+len(chain):], 65537); return w },
		"zero balance":      func(w []byte) []byte { clear(w[len(w)-8:]); return w },
		"truncated":         func(w []byte) []byte { return w[:len(w)-1] },
		"trailing":          func(w []byte) []byte { return append(w, 0) },
	}
	for name, change := range invalid {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if _, err := ParseGenesis(change(bytes.Clone(good)), chain); err == nil {
				t.Fatal("accepted")
			}
		})
	}
	if _, err := ParseGenesis(good, "other"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("wrong chain")
	}
	if _, err := ParseGenesis(make([]byte, 2), chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("short")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis("", []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 1}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("empty chain")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, nil); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("empty allocations")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 0}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("zero allocation")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: b, Balance: 1}, {Address: a, Balance: 1}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unsorted")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 1}, {Address: a, Balance: 1}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("duplicate")
	}
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis(chain, []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: math.MaxUint64}, {Address: b, Balance: 1}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("overflow")
	}
	unsorted := append(bytes.Clone(good), make([]byte, 28)...)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(unsorted[14+len(chain):], 2)
	copy(unsorted[len(good):len(good)+20], b[:])
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(unsorted[len(good)+20:], 1)
	copy(unsorted[18+len(chain):18+len(chain)+20], b[:]) // duplicate and unsorted
	if _, err := ParseGenesis(unsorted, chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("noncanonical allocation order")
	}
}

func TestGenesisMaximumAccountCount(t *testing.T) {
	allocations := make([]Allocation, MaxAccounts)
	for i := range allocations {
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(allocations[i].Address[16:], uint32(i))
		allocations[i].Balance = 1
	}
	wire, err := EncodeGenesis("c", allocations)
	if err != nil || len(wire) != 18+1+28*MaxAccounts {
		t.Fatal("maximum genesis encode", err)
	}
	state, err := ParseGenesis(wire, "c")
	if err != nil || len(state.Accounts()) != MaxAccounts || state.TotalSupply() != MaxAccounts {
		t.Fatal("maximum genesis parse", err)
	}
	tooMany := append(allocations, Allocation{Balance: 1})
	if _, err := EncodeGenesis("c", tooMany); err != ErrGenesis {
		t.Fatal("over-cap genesis accepted", err)
	}
}

func TestTransfersSequentialAndReplay(t *testing.T) {
	s0, a, b, v01 := genesis(t)
	s1, code, err := Evaluate(s0, v01)
	if err != nil || code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("V01 %d %v", code, err)
	}
	aa, _ := s1.Account(a)
	bb, _ := s1.Account(b)
	if aa.Balance != 75 || aa.Nonce != 1 || bb.Balance != 25 || bb.Nonce != 0 || s1.TotalSupply() != 100 {
		t.Fatal("V01 state")
	}
	if original, _ := s0.Account(a); original.Balance != 100 || len(s0.Accounts()) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("committed snapshot changed")
	}
	if _, code, err := Evaluate(s1, v01); err != nil || code != CodeInvalidNonce {
		t.Fatal("replay", code, err)
	}
	second := signed(t, 1, b, 30, 1)
	ordered, codes, err := SimulateOrdered(s0, [][]byte{v01, second})
	if err != nil || len(codes) != 2 || codes[0] != 0 || codes[1] != 0 {
		t.Fatal("ordered", codes, err)
	}
	aa, _ = ordered.Account(a)
	bb, _ = ordered.Account(b)
	if aa.Balance != 45 || aa.Nonce != 2 || bb.Balance != 55 {
		t.Fatal("ordered state")
	}
	failed := signed(t, 1, b, 101, 0)
	end, codes, err := SimulateOrdered(s0, [][]byte{failed, v01})
	if err != nil || codes[0] != CodeInsufficientBalance || codes[1] != 0 {
		t.Fatal("failed then valid", codes, err)
	}
	equalState(t, end, s1)
	independent, _, _ := SimulateOrdered(s0, [][]byte{v01, second})
	equalState(t, independent, ordered)
	// A newly funded B spends with its own key and nonce zero.
	spend := signed(t, 2, a, 5, 0)
	end, codes, err = SimulateOrdered(s0, [][]byte{v01, spend})
	if err != nil || codes[0] != 0 || codes[1] != 0 {
		t.Fatal("newly funded sender", codes, err)
	}
	aa, _ = end.Account(a)
	bb, _ = end.Account(b)
	if aa.Balance != 80 || aa.Nonce != 1 || bb.Balance != 20 || bb.Nonce != 1 {
		t.Fatal("funded spend")
	}
}

func TestTransitionBoundaries(t *testing.T) {
	s0, a, b, _ := genesis(t)
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		wire []byte
		code tx.Code
	}{
		{"future nonce", signed(t, 1, b, 1, 1), CodeInvalidNonce},
		{"insufficient", signed(t, 1, b, 101, 0), CodeInsufficientBalance},
		{"zero amount", signed(t, 1, b, 0, 0), tx.CodeZeroAmount},
		{"absent sender", signed(t, 2, a, 1, 0), CodeInsufficientBalance},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			next, code, err := Evaluate(s0, tc.wire)
			if err != nil || code != tc.code {
				t.Fatal(code, err)
			}
			equalState(t, next, s0)
		})
	}
	exact := signed(t, 1, b, 100, 0)
	end, code, err := Evaluate(s0, exact)
	if err != nil || code != 0 {
		t.Fatal(code, err)
	}
	aa, _ := end.Account(a)
	if aa.Balance != 0 || aa.Nonce != 1 {
		t.Fatal("zero-balance sender removed")
	}
	self := signed(t, 1, a, 25, 0)
	end, code, err = Evaluate(s0, self)
	if err != nil || code != 0 || len(end.Accounts()) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("self transfer", code, err)
	}
	aa, _ = end.Account(a)
	if aa.Balance != 100 || aa.Nonce != 1 {
		t.Fatal("self state")
	}
	maxNonce, _ := NewState(chain, 0, 1, []Account{{Address: a, Balance: 0, Nonce: math.MaxUint64}, {Address: b, Balance: 1}})
	end, code, err = Evaluate(maxNonce, signed(t, 1, b, 1, math.MaxUint64))
	if err != nil || code != CodeInvalidNonce {
		t.Fatal("nonce precedence", code, err)
	}
	equalState(t, end, maxNonce)
	maxHeight, _ := NewState(chain, math.MaxInt64, 100, []Account{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	if _, err := maxHeight.AdvanceBlock(); err != ErrHeightOverflow {
		t.Fatal("height overflow", err)
	}
	advanced, err := s0.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil || advanced.Height() != 1 || advanced.AppHash() == s0.AppHash() {
		t.Fatal("empty block")
	}
	if _, err := NewState(chain, 0, 2, []Account{{Address: a, Balance: 1}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("supply mismatch")
	}
	if _, err := NewState(chain, 0, 0, []Account{{Address: b}, {Address: a}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unordered")
	}
}

func TestAccountCapAndCorruptLogical(t *testing.T) {
	_, a, b, _ := genesis(t)
	accounts := make([]Account, MaxAccounts)
	for i := 0; i < MaxAccounts-1; i++ {
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(accounts[i].Address[16:], uint32(i))
	}
	accounts[MaxAccounts-1] = Account{Address: a, Balance: 1}
	s, err := NewState(chain, 0, 1, accounts)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	next, code, err := Evaluate(s, signed(t, 1, b, 1, 0))
	if err != nil || code != CodeAccountLimit {
		t.Fatal(code, err)
	}
	equalState(t, next, s)
	logical, _ := s.LogicalBytes()
	hash := s.AppHash()
	if _, err := DecodeLogical(logical, hash, "wrong"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("wrong chain")
	}
	for _, mutate := range []func([]byte){
		func(b []byte) { b[3] = 3 },
		func(b []byte) { b[5] = 0 },
		func(b []byte) { b[len(b)-1] ^= 1 },
		func(b []byte) { binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(b[6+len(chain)+16:], 0) },
	} {
		bad := bytes.Clone(logical)
		mutate(bad)
		if _, err := DecodeLogical(bad, hash, chain); err == nil {
			t.Fatal("accepted corrupted logical state")
		}
	}
	if _, err := DecodeLogical(logical[:len(logical)-1], hash, chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("truncated")
	}
	badHash := hash
	badHash[0] ^= 1
	if _, err := DecodeLogical(logical, badHash, chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("digest corruption")
	}
}

func FuzzGenesis(f *testing.F) {
	f.Add([]byte{})
	f.Add([]byte("PLUTOG2!"))
	var a Address
	a[19] = 1
	good, _ := EncodeGenesis("c", []Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 1}})
	f.Add(good)
	f.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, raw []byte) {
		s, err := ParseGenesis(raw, "c")
		if err == nil {
			if e := s.Validate(); e != nil {
				t.Fatal(e)
			}
			allocs := make([]Allocation, 0, len(s.Accounts()))
			for _, a := range s.Accounts() {
				allocs = append(allocs, Allocation{Address: a.Address, Balance: a.Balance})
			}
			encoded, e := EncodeGenesis(s.ChainID(), allocs)
			if e != nil || !bytes.Equal(encoded, raw) {
				t.Fatal("noncanonical genesis")
			}
		}
	})
}

func FuzzLogical(f *testing.F) {
	f.Add([]byte{})
	f.Add([]byte{0, 0, 0, 2})
	f.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, raw []byte) {
		var h [32]byte
		_, _ = DecodeLogical(raw, h, "c")
	})
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0state/benchmark_test.go (4800 bytes; sha256 070664944390468ef1c9979ce60eadd575028a4d552d1adf59eb45ff5e5f9a4f) ===
package b0state

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"testing"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
)

func TestTrustedEvaluatorMatchesPhase03CReference(t *testing.T) {
	sender, receiver, valid := baseFixture(t)
	state, err := NewState(chain, 0, 100, []Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wrong := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	wrong[12] = 'x'
	zero := signed(t, 1, receiver, 0, 0)
	noncanonical := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	for i := len(noncanonical) - 64; i < len(noncanonical)-32; i++ {
		noncanonical[i] = 0
	}
	invalidECDSA := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	invalidECDSA[len(invalidECDSA)-80] ^= 1
	for _, raw := range [][]byte{{1}, wrong, zero, noncanonical, invalidECDSA, valid,
		signed(t, 1, sender, 25, 0), signed(t, 1, receiver, 200, 0), signed(t, 1, receiver, 25, 1)} {
		reference, referenceCode, referenceErr := Evaluate(state, raw)
		integrated, integratedCode, integratedErr := EvaluateTrusted(state, raw)
		if referenceErr != nil || integratedErr != nil || referenceCode != integratedCode || reference.AppHash() != integrated.AppHash() {
			t.Fatalf("different result for %x: reference=%d/%v integrated=%d/%v", raw, referenceCode, referenceErr, integratedCode, integratedErr)
		}
		a, _ := reference.LogicalBytes()
		b, _ := integrated.LogicalBytes()
		if !bytes.Equal(a, b) {
			t.Fatal("different logical state")
		}
	}
	if _, _, err := EvaluateTrusted(State{}, []byte{1}); err != ErrState {
		t.Fatalf("untrusted state accepted: %v", err)
	}
}

// The Phase 03C Evaluate method remains the untouched reference path. Each
// subbenchmark uses the same immutable snapshot and test-only signed bytes.
func benchmarkState(b *testing.B, k int, sender, receiver Address) State {
	b.Helper()
	accounts := []Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 1_000_000}}
	if k > 1 {
		accounts = append(accounts, Account{Address: receiver, Balance: 1_000_000})
	}
	for i := 0; len(accounts) < k; i++ {
		var addr Address
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(addr[16:], uint32(i))
		if addr == sender || addr == receiver {
			continue
		}
		accounts = append(accounts, Account{Address: addr, Balance: 1_000_000})
	}
	sort.Slice(accounts, func(i, j int) bool { return string(accounts[i].Address[:]) < string(accounts[j].Address[:]) })
	s, err := NewState(chain, 0, uint64(k)*1_000_000, accounts)
	if err != nil {
		b.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s
}

func BenchmarkB0StateCost(b *testing.B) {
	// baseFixture uses the approved Phase 03B V01 vector; signed uses only
	// explicit public test scalars 1 and 2.
	sender, receiver, valid := baseFixture(b)
	self := signed(b, 1, sender, 25, 0)
	zero := signed(b, 1, receiver, 0, 0)
	wrong := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	wrong[12] = 'x'
	noncanonical := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	for i := len(noncanonical) - 64; i < len(noncanonical)-32; i++ {
		noncanonical[i] = 0
	}
	invalidECDSA := append([]byte(nil), valid...)
	invalidECDSA[len(invalidECDSA)-64-16] ^= 1 // signed amount/nonce region
	workloads := []struct {
		name string
		raw  []byte
		code tx.Code
	}{
		{"Malformed", []byte{1}, 1}, {"WrongChain", wrong, 2},
		{"ZeroAmount", zero, 5}, {"NoncanonicalScalar", noncanonical, 4},
		{"InvalidECDSA", invalidECDSA, 4}, {"SelfTransfer", self, 0},
		{"DistinctTransfer", valid, 0},
	}
	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
		state := benchmarkState(b, k, sender, receiver)
		logicalBytes := 26 + len(chain) + 36*k
		for _, path := range []struct {
			name     string
			evaluate func(State, []byte) (State, tx.Code, error)
		}{
			{"Reference", Evaluate}, {"Trusted", EvaluateTrusted},
		} {
			for _, work := range workloads {
				b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/%s/%s", k, path.name, work.name), func(b *testing.B) {
					_, code, err := path.evaluate(state, work.raw)
					if err != nil || code != work.code {
						b.Fatalf("fixture code=%d want=%d err=%v", code, work.code, err)
					}
					b.ReportAllocs()
					b.ResetTimer()
					for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
						_, code, err := path.evaluate(state, work.raw)
						if err != nil || code != work.code {
							b.Fatal(code, err)
						}
					}
					b.StopTimer()
					b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
				})
			}
		}
		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/EmptyBlock", k), func(b *testing.B) {
			b.ReportAllocs()
			b.ResetTimer()
			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
				if _, err := state.AdvanceBlock(); err != nil {
					b.Fatal(err)
				}
			}
			b.StopTimer()
			b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
		})
		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d/SerializeAndHash", k), func(b *testing.B) {
			b.ReportAllocs()
			b.ResetTimer()
			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
				if _, err := state.LogicalBytes(); err != nil {
					b.Fatal(err)
				}
			}
			b.StopTimer()
			b.ReportMetric(float64(logicalBytes), "logical-bytes/op")
		})
	}
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0store/store.go (9981 bytes; sha256 c81a5eb60341f4bbdc732d41b46b84c89cd4c8add192138a826a4863346a5f6f) ===
// Package b0store persists isolated B0 V2 snapshots in Pebble. It never
// opens or migrates the Phase 02 V1 application store on behalf of the node.
package b0store

import (
	"bytes"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble"
	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs"
)

const (
	recordMagic   = "PLUTOS2!"
	maxRecordSize = 8 + 4 + 2 + 50 + 8 + 8 + 4 + b0state.MaxAccounts*36 + 32
)

var committedKey = []byte("pluto/committed-state/v2")
var v1Key = []byte("pluto/committed-state/v1")

var ErrExisting = errors.New("B0 database path already exists")
var ErrMissing = errors.New("B0 committed state missing")
var ErrV1 = errors.New("Phase 02 V1 record present; no implicit migration")
var ErrPending = errors.New("B0 candidate already pending")
var ErrNoPending = errors.New("no B0 candidate pending")
var ErrUncertain = errors.New("B0 commit outcome uncertain; close and reopen")
var ErrClosed = errors.New("B0 store closed")

// Options supplies deterministic fault hooks for tests. The production
// constructor passes zero Options. An AfterSync error models a response error
// after durable bytes may already have changed; reopening decides recovery.
type Options struct {
	BeforeSync func() error
	AfterSync  func() error
	// FS and the genesis hooks are used by recovery tests. A nil FS uses the
	// production filesystem. No hook changes the persisted record format.
	FS                 vfs.FS
	BeforeGenesisMkdir func() error
	AfterGenesisMkdir  func() error
	BeforeGenesisSync  func() error
	AfterGenesisSync   func() error
}

// Store owns one Pebble lock and serializes candidate and committed access.
type Store struct {
	mu        sync.RWMutex
	db        *pebble.DB
	syncWrite func([]byte, []byte) error // Internal fault-injection seam; production uses pebble.Sync.
	committed b0state.State
	pending   *b0state.State
	options   Options
	uncertain bool
	closed    bool
}

// Create initializes only a previously absent directory. A failed attempt
// leaves its directory for explicit operator inspection; it never resets it.
func Create(dir string, genesis []byte, expectedChainID string) (*Store, error) {
	return createWithOptions(dir, genesis, expectedChainID, Options{})
}

func createWithOptions(dir string, genesis []byte, expectedChainID string, options Options) (*Store, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		return nil, ErrMissing
	}
	state, err := b0state.ParseGenesis(genesis, expectedChainID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(dir); err == nil {
		return nil, ErrExisting
	} else if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(dir), 0700); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if options.BeforeGenesisMkdir != nil {
		if err := options.BeforeGenesisMkdir(); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("before B0 directory creation: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if err := os.Mkdir(dir, 0700); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create B0 database directory: %w", err)
	}
	if options.AfterGenesisMkdir != nil {
		if err := options.AfterGenesisMkdir(); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("after B0 directory creation: %w", err)
		}
	}
	fs := options.FS
	if fs == nil {
		fs = vfs.Default
	}
	db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfExists: true, FS: fs})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open new B0 Pebble database: %w", err)
	}
	record, err := encodeRecord(state)
	if err == nil {
		if options.BeforeGenesisSync != nil {
			err = options.BeforeGenesisSync()
		}
		if err == nil {
			err = db.Set(committedKey, record, pebble.Sync)
		}
		if err == nil && options.AfterGenesisSync != nil {
			err = options.AfterGenesisSync()
		}
	}
	if err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("persist B0 genesis: %w", err)
	}
	return &Store{db: db, syncWrite: func(key, value []byte) error { return db.Set(key, value, pebble.Sync) }, committed: state}, nil
}

func OpenExisting(dir, expectedChainID string) (*Store, error) {
	return OpenExistingWithOptions(dir, expectedChainID, Options{})
}

// OpenExistingWithOptions is intended for fault-injection tests. It requires
// an existing Pebble database and a complete, integrity-valid V2 record.
func OpenExistingWithOptions(dir, expectedChainID string, options Options) (*Store, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		return nil, ErrMissing
	}
	if info, err := os.Stat(dir); errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, ErrMissing
	} else if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	} else if !info.IsDir() {
		return nil, ErrMissing
	}
	fs := options.FS
	if fs == nil {
		fs = vfs.Default
	}
	db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true, FS: fs})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open existing B0 Pebble database: %w", err)
	}
	state, err := load(db, expectedChainID)
	if err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	return &Store{db: db, syncWrite: func(key, value []byte) error { return db.Set(key, value, pebble.Sync) }, committed: state, options: options}, nil
}

func load(db *pebble.DB, expectedChainID string) (b0state.State, error) {
	if _, closer, err := db.Get(v1Key); err == nil {
		if closeErr := closer.Close(); closeErr != nil {
			return b0state.State{}, closeErr
		}
		return b0state.State{}, ErrV1
	} else if !errors.Is(err, pebble.ErrNotFound) {
		return b0state.State{}, err
	}
	value, closer, err := db.Get(committedKey)
	if errors.Is(err, pebble.ErrNotFound) {
		return b0state.State{}, ErrMissing
	}
	if err != nil {
		return b0state.State{}, err
	}
	if len(value) < 8+4+2+1+8+8+4+36+32 || len(value) > maxRecordSize {
		_ = closer.Close()
		return b0state.State{}, b0state.ErrState
	}
	copyValue := bytes.Clone(value)
	if err := closer.Close(); err != nil {
		return b0state.State{}, fmt.Errorf("close B0 value: %w", err)
	}
	return decodeRecord(copyValue, expectedChainID)
}

func encodeRecord(s b0state.State) ([]byte, error) {
	logical, err := s.LogicalBytes()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]byte, 8+len(logical)+32)
	copy(out[:8], recordMagic)
	copy(out[8:8+len(logical)], logical)
	hash := s.AppHash()
	copy(out[8+len(logical):], hash[:])
	return out, nil
}

func decodeRecord(data []byte, expectedChainID string) (b0state.State, error) {
	if len(data) < 8+4+2+1+8+8+4+36+32 || len(data) > maxRecordSize || string(data[:8]) != recordMagic {
		return b0state.State{}, b0state.ErrState
	}
	var hash [32]byte
	copy(hash[:], data[len(data)-32:])
	return b0state.DecodeLogical(data[8:len(data)-32], hash, expectedChainID)
}

func (s *Store) Committed() (b0state.State, error) {
	s.mu.RLock()
	defer s.mu.RUnlock()
	if s.closed {
		return b0state.State{}, ErrClosed
	}
	if s.uncertain {
		return b0state.State{}, ErrUncertain
	}
	return s.committed, nil // State exposes no mutable account slice.
}

func (s *Store) Pending() (b0state.State, bool, error) {
	s.mu.RLock()
	defer s.mu.RUnlock()
	if s.closed {
		return b0state.State{}, false, ErrClosed
	}
	if s.uncertain {
		return b0state.State{}, false, ErrUncertain
	}
	if s.pending == nil {
		return b0state.State{}, false, nil
	}
	return *s.pending, true, nil
}

// Prepare simulates ordered transaction bytes and then advances exactly one
// block. It has no ABCI or block-envelope behavior and performs no disk write.
func (s *Store) Prepare(raws [][]byte) ([]tx.Code, [32]byte, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrClosed
	}
	if s.uncertain {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrUncertain
	}
	if s.pending != nil {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrPending
	}
	candidate, codes, err := b0state.SimulateOrdered(s.committed, raws)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, err
	}
	candidate, err = candidate.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, err
	}
	s.pending = &candidate
	return codes, candidate.AppHash(), nil
}

// PrepareTrusted is the B0 ABCI finalization path. The committed value was
// fully validated on creation/reload and cannot be mutated by callers. It
// preserves the Phase 03C Prepare API as the reference evaluator path.
// An over-limit decided block passes no transactions and advances once.
func (s *Store) PrepareTrusted(raws [][]byte, overLimit bool) ([]tx.Code, [32]byte, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrClosed
	}
	if s.uncertain {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrUncertain
	}
	if s.pending != nil {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, ErrPending
	}
	var candidate b0state.State
	var codes []tx.Code
	var err error
	if overLimit {
		candidate = s.committed
	} else {
		candidate, codes, err = b0state.SimulateOrderedTrusted(s.committed, raws)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, [32]byte{}, err
		}
	}
	candidate, err = candidate.AdvanceBlock()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, [32]byte{}, err
	}
	s.pending = &candidate
	return codes, candidate.AppHash(), nil
}

// Commit performs one synchronous complete-record Pebble write. A failed
// pre-sync hook leaves pending for retry. Any Set or post-sync error leaves
// in-memory committed unchanged and requires reopening to resolve ambiguity.
func (s *Store) Commit() error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return ErrClosed
	}
	if s.uncertain {
		return ErrUncertain
	}
	if s.pending == nil {
		return ErrNoPending
	}
	record, err := encodeRecord(*s.pending)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if s.options.BeforeSync != nil {
		if err := s.options.BeforeSync(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	if err := s.syncWrite(committedKey, record); err != nil {
		s.uncertain = true
		return fmt.Errorf("sync B0 committed snapshot: %w", err)
	}
	if s.options.AfterSync != nil {
		if err := s.options.AfterSync(); err != nil {
			s.uncertain = true
			return fmt.Errorf("post-sync B0 response failure: %w", err)
		}
	}
	s.committed = *s.pending
	s.pending = nil
	return nil
}

func (s *Store) Close() error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.closed {
		return ErrClosed
	}
	s.closed = true
	return s.db.Close()
}
