=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0store/store_test.go (22134 bytes; sha256 ecb57f20b657386b4832b6759ccab89fcde3ec43e43e906d01f7547a9464fa6c) ===
package b0store

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble"
)

const chain = "pluto-b0-v1"

type vector struct {
	Wire     string `json:"wire_hex"`
	Sender   string `json:"sender_address_hex"`
	Receiver string `json:"receiver_hex"`
}

func fixture(t *testing.T) ([]byte, []byte, b0state.Address, b0state.Address) {
	t.Helper()
	data, err := osReadFile("../../research/evidence/phase_03b/golden_vectors.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var v []vector
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var a, b b0state.Address
	aBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString(v[0].Sender)
	bBytes, _ := hex.DecodeString(v[0].Receiver)
	copy(a[:], aBytes)
	copy(b[:], bBytes)
	wire, _ := hex.DecodeString(v[0].Wire)
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis(chain, []b0state.Allocation{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return genesis, wire, a, b
}

// Keep file reads behind one helper so fixtures are never generated from the
// implementation under test in the persistence tests.
func osReadFile(path string) ([]byte, error) { return os.ReadFile(path) }

func TestCreateCommitRestartAndIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, a, b := fixture(t)
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain); err != ErrExisting {
		t.Fatal("repeat init", err)
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Pebble lock not enforced")
	}
	initial, err := s.Committed()
	if err != nil || initial.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	codes, pendingHash, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire})
	if err != nil || len(codes) != 1 || codes[0] != tx.CodeOK {
		t.Fatal(codes, err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrPending {
		t.Fatal("duplicate pending", err)
	}
	committed, _ := s.Committed()
	if committed.Height() != 0 || committed.AppHash() != initial.AppHash() || len(committed.Accounts()) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("pending leaked")
	}
	pending, yes, err := s.Pending()
	if err != nil || !yes || pending.Height() != 1 || pending.AppHash() != pendingHash {
		t.Fatal("pending state")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	committed, _ = s.Committed()
	if committed.Height() != 0 || committed.AppHash() != initial.AppHash() {
		t.Fatal("uncommitted state survived restart")
	}
	if _, yes, _ := s.Pending(); yes {
		t.Fatal("pending survived restart")
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrNoPending {
		t.Fatal("duplicate commit", err)
	}
	committed, _ = s.Committed()
	aa, _ := committed.Account(a)
	bb, _ := committed.Account(b)
	if committed.Height() != 1 || aa.Balance != 75 || aa.Nonce != 1 || bb.Balance != 25 {
		t.Fatal("committed transfer")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	reloaded, _ := s.Committed()
	if reloaded.AppHash() != committed.AppHash() || reloaded.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("restart mismatch")
	}
	codes, _, err = s.Prepare(nil)
	if err != nil || len(codes) != 0 {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	empty, _ := s.Committed()
	if empty.Height() != 2 || empty.AppHash() == reloaded.AppHash() || empty.TotalSupply() != 100 {
		t.Fatal("empty block")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	latest, _ := s.Committed()
	if latest.Height() != 2 || latest.AppHash() != empty.AppHash() {
		t.Fatal("empty restart")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := s.Committed(); err != ErrClosed {
		t.Fatal("closed read", err)
	}
}

func TestFaultBoundaries(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, _, _ := fixture(t)
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	calls := 0
	s, err = OpenExistingWithOptions(dir, chain, Options{BeforeSync: func() error {
		calls++
		if calls == 1 {
			return errors.New("injected before write")
		}
		return nil
	}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected prewrite error")
	}
	old, err := s.Committed()
	if err != nil || old.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("promoted before write")
	}
	prewritePending, yes, err := s.Pending()
	if err != nil || !yes || prewritePending.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("lost pending after prewrite error")
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal("retry before-write failure", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	current, _ := s.Committed()
	if current.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("successful retry not durable")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExistingWithOptions(dir, chain, Options{AfterSync: func() error { return errors.New("injected after sync") }})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected postwrite error")
	}
	if snapshot, err := s.Committed(); err != ErrUncertain || snapshot.ChainID() != "" || snapshot.AppHash() != ([32]byte{}) {
		t.Fatal("committed read did not fail closed", err)
	}
	if snapshot, yes, err := s.Pending(); err != ErrUncertain || yes || snapshot.ChainID() != "" || snapshot.AppHash() != ([32]byte{}) {
		t.Fatal("pending read did not fail closed", err)
	}
	if old.Height() != 0 || current.Height() != 1 || s.committed.Height() != 1 || s.pending == nil || s.pending.Height() != 2 {
		t.Fatal("previous snapshots or internal pending/committed state changed unexpectedly")
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain retry", err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain prepare", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	recovered, _ := s.Committed()
	if recovered.Height() != 2 {
		t.Fatal("post-sync recovery must accept complete new record")
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestConcurrentCommittedReadsDuringCandidateLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, _, _ := fixture(t)
	s, err := Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0"), genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer s.Close()
	initial, _ := s.Committed()
	var group sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 12; i++ {
		group.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer group.Done()
			for j := 0; j < 20; j++ {
				state, readErr := s.Committed()
				if readErr != nil || (state.Height() != 0 && state.Height() != 1) || state.Validate() != nil {
					t.Errorf("invalid concurrent committed snapshot: %v", readErr)
					return
				}
			}
		}()
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, _ := s.Committed()
	if before.AppHash() != initial.AppHash() {
		t.Fatal("pending visible")
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	group.Wait()
	committed, _ := s.Committed()
	if committed.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("commit missing")
	}
}

func TestConcurrentReadersFailClosedOnUncertainCommit(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, _, _ := fixture(t)
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	entered := make(chan struct{})
	release := make(chan struct{})
	s, err = OpenExistingWithOptions(dir, chain, Options{AfterSync: func() error {
		close(entered)
		<-release
		return errors.New("injected post-sync response error")
	}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, err := s.Committed()
	if err != nil || before.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("normal committed read", err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	prepared, yes, err := s.Pending()
	if err != nil || !yes || prepared.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("normal pending read", err)
	}
	commitResult := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { commitResult <- s.Commit() }()
	select {
	case <-entered:
	case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
		close(release)
		t.Fatal("post-sync hook was not reached")
	}
	const readers = 24
	results := make(chan error, readers*2)
	var group sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < readers; i++ {
		group.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer group.Done()
			committed, readErr := s.Committed()
			if readErr != ErrUncertain || committed.ChainID() != "" {
				results <- fmt.Errorf("committed read: %v", readErr)
			}
			pending, available, readErr := s.Pending()
			if readErr != ErrUncertain || available || pending.ChainID() != "" {
				results <- fmt.Errorf("pending read: %v", readErr)
			}
		}()
	}
	close(release)
	if err := <-commitResult; err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected post-sync error")
	}
	group.Wait()
	close(results)
	for err := range results {
		t.Error(err)
	}
	if before.Validate() != nil || prepared.Validate() != nil || before.Height() != 0 || prepared.Height() != 1 ||
		s.committed.Height() != 0 || s.pending == nil || s.pending.Height() != 1 ||
		s.committed.Validate() != nil || s.pending.Validate() != nil {
		t.Fatal("snapshots mutated or pending candidate lost")
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain prepare", err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain commit", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	reopened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer reopened.Close()
	recovered, err := reopened.Committed()
	if err != nil || recovered.Height() != 1 || recovered.AppHash() != prepared.AppHash() {
		t.Fatal("reopen did not validate complete new snapshot", err)
	}
}

func TestProcessKillRecovery(t *testing.T) {
	if stage := os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_KILL_CHILD"); stage != "" {
		genesis, wire, _, _ := fixture(t)
		s, err := Create(os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_KILL_DIR"), genesis, chain)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if stage == "committed" {
			if err := s.Commit(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}
		fmt.Println("B0_READY")
		time.Sleep(time.Hour) // Parent kills this disposable child process.
		return
	}
	for _, stage := range []string{"pending", "committed"} {
		t.Run(stage, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Second)
			defer cancel()
			cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestProcessKillRecovery$")
			cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_B0_KILL_CHILD="+stage, "PLUTO_B0_KILL_DIR="+dir)
			pipe, err := cmd.StdoutPipe()
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var stderr bytes.Buffer
			cmd.Stderr = &stderr
			if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			ready := make(chan string, 1)
			go func() { line, _ := bufio.NewReader(pipe).ReadString('\n'); ready <- strings.TrimSpace(line) }()
			select {
			case line := <-ready:
				if line != "B0_READY" {
					_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
					_ = cmd.Wait()
					t.Fatalf("child not ready: %q %s", line, stderr.String())
				}
			case <-ctx.Done():
				_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
				_ = cmd.Wait()
				t.Fatal("child readiness timed out")
			}
			if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			_ = cmd.Wait() // Signal termination is expected.
			s, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			defer s.Close()
			state, err := s.Committed()
			if err != nil || state.Validate() != nil {
				t.Fatal("invalid recovered state", err)
			}
			want := int64(0)
			if stage == "committed" {
				want = 1
			}
			if state.Height() != want {
				t.Fatalf("recovered height %d, want %d", state.Height(), want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCorruptPhysicalRecordsFailClosed(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, _, a, b := fixture(t)
	cases := map[string]func([]byte) []byte{
		"magic":   func(r []byte) []byte { r[0] = 0; return r },
		"version": func(r []byte) []byte { r[11] = 3; return r },
		"short":   func(r []byte) []byte { return r[:len(r)-1] },
		"long":    func(r []byte) []byte { return append(r, 0) },
		"hash":    func(r []byte) []byte { r[len(r)-1] ^= 1; return r },
		"balance": func(r []byte) []byte { r[len(r)-32-16] ^= 1; return r },
		"nonce":   func(r []byte) []byte { r[len(r)-32-1] ^= 1; return r },
		"supply":  func(r []byte) []byte { r[8+6+len(chain)+15] ^= 1; return r },
		"count":   func(r []byte) []byte { binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(r[8+6+len(chain)+16:], 65537); return r },
		"chain":   func(r []byte) []byte { r[8+6] = 0xff; return r },
	}
	for name, mutate := range cases {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
			s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			raw, closer, err := db.Get(committedKey)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			bad := mutate(bytes.Clone(raw))
			if err := closer.Close(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := db.Set(committedKey, bad, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if opened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); err == nil {
				opened.Close()
				t.Fatal("accepted corrupt record")
			}
		})
	}
	// V1 key is never silently treated as an absent V2 state.
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "v1")
	db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Set(v1Key, []byte("PLUTO02!"), pebble.Sync); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); err != ErrV1 {
		t.Fatal("V1 not rejected", err)
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "absent"), chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("absent DB accepted")
	}
	// A valid record with the wrong explicit chain context also fails.
	dir = filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "wrong-chain")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting(dir, "other"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("wrong chain accepted")
	}
	_ = a
	_ = b
}

func TestPhysicalOrderingOverflowAndHeight(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, _, a, _ := fixture(t)
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	base, _ := b0state.ParseGenesis(genesis, chain)
	makeCorrupt := func(logical []byte) {
		hash := sha256.Sum256(append([]byte("PLUTO/B0/STATE/V2\x00"), logical...))
		record := append([]byte(recordMagic), logical...)
		record = append(record, hash[:]...)
		db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := db.Set(committedKey, record, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if opened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); err == nil {
			opened.Close()
			t.Fatal("corrupt ordered state accepted")
		}
	}
	// Manufacture two accounts with a valid physical length and matching hash
	// but unsorted / duplicate records. Public NewState would reject these.
	baseLogical, _ := base.LogicalBytes()
	accountStart := 6 + len(chain) + 20
	for _, duplicate := range []bool{false, true} {
		logical := append(bytes.Clone(baseLogical), make([]byte, 36)...)
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(logical[6+len(chain)+16:], 2)
		copy(logical[accountStart+36:], logical[accountStart:accountStart+36])
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(logical[accountStart+36+20:], 0)
		if !duplicate {
			clear(logical[accountStart+36 : accountStart+36+20])
		}
		makeCorrupt(logical)
	}
	// Sum overflows even though the stored digest was recomputed to match.
	logical := append(bytes.Clone(baseLogical), make([]byte, 36)...)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(logical[6+len(chain)+16:], 2)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(logical[accountStart+20:], math.MaxUint64)
	copy(logical[accountStart+36:accountStart+36+20], a[:])
	logical[accountStart+36+19]++
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(logical[accountStart+36+20:], 1)
	makeCorrupt(logical)
	// A mismatched supply cannot be rescued by recomputing the digest.
	logical = bytes.Clone(baseLogical)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(logical[6+len(chain)+8:], 99)
	makeCorrupt(logical)
	max, _ := b0state.NewState(chain, math.MaxInt64, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: a, Balance: 100}})
	record, _ := encodeRecord(max)
	db, err := pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Set(committedKey, record, pebble.Sync); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != b0state.ErrHeightOverflow {
		t.Fatal("height overflow", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func FuzzRecord(f *testing.F) {
	f.Add([]byte{})
	f.Add([]byte("PLUTOS2!"))
	f.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, raw []byte) { _, _ = decodeRecord(raw, "c") })
}

func TestLifecycleAndStorageErrorBranches(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, _, _ := fixture(t)
	if _, err := Create("", genesis, chain); err != ErrMissing {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "bad"), []byte("invalid"), chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("bad genesis")
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting("", chain); err != ErrMissing {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	parent := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "regular-file")
	if err := os.WriteFile(parent, []byte("x"), 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Create(filepath.Join(parent, "child"), genesis, chain); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("bad parent accepted")
	}

	emptyDir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "empty-db")
	db, err := pebble.Open(emptyDir, &pebble.Options{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := OpenExisting(emptyDir, chain); err != ErrMissing {
		t.Fatal("missing record", err)
	}

	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
	s, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Pending(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrNoPending {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrPending {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Pending(); err != ErrClosed {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrClosed {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrClosed {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != ErrClosed {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.pending = &b0state.State{} // synthetic invalid internal candidate
	if err := s.Commit(); err != b0state.ErrState {
		t.Fatal("invalid pending", err)
	}
	s.pending = nil
	s.committed = b0state.State{} // synthetic invalid committed state
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != b0state.ErrState {
		t.Fatal("invalid committed", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	s, err = OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Inject a returned write error without closing Pebble behind Store's lock.
	// A real I/O error can have an ambiguous durable outcome; this hook models
	// the old-record outcome while AfterSync tests the new-record outcome.
	s.syncWrite = func([]byte, []byte) error { return errors.New("injected Set error") }
	if err := s.Commit(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("injected Set error accepted")
	}
	if snapshot, err := s.Committed(); err != ErrUncertain || snapshot.ChainID() != "" || snapshot.AppHash() != ([32]byte{}) {
		t.Fatal("Set error committed read did not fail closed", err)
	}
	if snapshot, yes, err := s.Pending(); err != ErrUncertain || yes || snapshot.ChainID() != "" || snapshot.AppHash() != ([32]byte{}) {
		t.Fatal("Set error pending read did not fail closed", err)
	}
	if s.committed.Height() != 0 || s.pending == nil || s.pending.Height() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("Set error promoted or discarded candidate")
	}
	if err := s.Commit(); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain Set retry", err)
	}
	if _, _, err := s.Prepare(nil); err != ErrUncertain {
		t.Fatal("uncertain Set prepare", err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	reopened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	recovered, err := reopened.Committed()
	if err != nil || recovered.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("old state recovery", err)
	}
	if err := reopened.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Oversized values are rejected before copying or allocating account arrays.
	dir = filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "oversized")
	s, err = Create(dir, genesis, chain)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	db, err = pebble.Open(dir, &pebble.Options{ErrorIfNotExists: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Set(committedKey, bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, maxRecordSize+1), pebble.Sync); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if opened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); err == nil {
		opened.Close()
		t.Fatal("oversized record accepted")
	}
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/b0store/recovery_closure_test.go (11882 bytes; sha256 5010411a69133170263ae82a4461d1888c01343de8d98152068cbc53545ca9e2) ===
package b0store

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs"
	"github.com/cockroachdb/pebble/vfs/errorfs"
)

// walFault reaches a specific Pebble WAL filesystem call. It rejects that
// call before it executes. This models a returned I/O error, not a power loss.
func walFault(target errorfs.Op) (vfs.FS, *atomic.Bool, *atomic.Bool) {
	enabled, reached := new(atomic.Bool), new(atomic.Bool)
	fs := errorfs.Wrap(vfs.Default, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(op errorfs.Op, path string) error {
		if enabled.Load() && op == target && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") && reached.CompareAndSwap(false, true) {
			return errorfs.ErrInjected
		}
		return nil
	}))
	return fs, enabled, reached
}

// Pebble v1.1.5 uses File.SyncTo for WAL durability. Its bundled errorfs
// forwards SyncTo without injection, so this wrapper targets that method.
type walSyncFS struct {
	vfs.FS
	enabled *atomic.Bool
	mark    string
	kill    bool
}
type walSyncFile struct {
	vfs.File
	fs   *walSyncFS
	path string
}

func (fs *walSyncFS) wrap(path string, f vfs.File, err error) (vfs.File, error) {
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &walSyncFile{File: f, fs: fs, path: path}, nil
}
func (fs *walSyncFS) Create(path string) (vfs.File, error) {
	f, err := fs.FS.Create(path)
	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
}
func (fs *walSyncFS) Open(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
	f, err := fs.FS.Open(path, opts...)
	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
}
func (fs *walSyncFS) OpenReadWrite(path string, opts ...vfs.OpenOption) (vfs.File, error) {
	f, err := fs.FS.OpenReadWrite(path, opts...)
	return fs.wrap(path, f, err)
}
func (fs *walSyncFS) ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath string) (vfs.File, error) {
	f, err := fs.FS.ReuseForWrite(oldPath, newPath)
	return fs.wrap(newPath, f, err)
}
func (f *walSyncFile) SyncTo(length int64) (bool, error) {
	if f.inject("SyncTo", length) {
		return false, errorfs.ErrInjected
	}
	return f.File.SyncTo(length)
}
func (f *walSyncFile) Sync() error {
	if f.inject("Sync", 0) {
		return errorfs.ErrInjected
	}
	return f.File.Sync()
}
func (f *walSyncFile) SyncData() error {
	if f.inject("SyncData", 0) {
		return errorfs.ErrInjected
	}
	return f.File.SyncData()
}
func (f *walSyncFile) inject(method string, length int64) bool {
	if !f.fs.enabled.Load() || !strings.HasSuffix(f.path, ".log") {
		return false
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(f.fs.mark, []byte(fmt.Sprintf("%s %s length=%d", method, f.path, length)), 0600); err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	if f.fs.kill {
		select {}
	}
	return true
}

func recoveredTransfer(t *testing.T, dir string, oldHash, newHash [32]byte, sender, receiver b0state.Address) string {
	t.Helper()
	s, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Sprintf("FAIL_CLOSED:%v", err)
	}
	defer s.Close()
	state, err := s.Committed()
	if err != nil || state.Validate() != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reopened invalid committed state: %v", err)
	}
	a, _ := state.Account(sender)
	b, found := state.Account(receiver)
	switch state.AppHash() {
	case oldHash:
		if state.Height() != 0 || a.Balance != 100 || a.Nonce != 0 || found {
			t.Fatalf("old hash with incorrect effects: A=%+v B=%+v", a, b)
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("OLD A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent", oldHash)
	case newHash:
		if state.Height() != 1 || a.Balance != 75 || a.Nonce != 1 || !found || b.Balance != 25 || b.Nonce != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("new hash with incorrect effects: A=%+v B=%+v", a, b)
		}
		return fmt.Sprintf("NEW A=1 hash=%x sender=75/1 receiver=25/0", newHash)
	default:
		t.Fatalf("reopened unexpected AppHash=%x height=%d", state.AppHash(), state.Height())
		return ""
	}
}

func TestM4ReturnedWALWriteAndSyncErrors(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, wire, sender, receiver := fixture(t)
	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M4_CHILD"); mode != "" {
		op := errorfs.OpFileWrite
		if mode == "wal-sync" {
			op = errorfs.OpFileSync
		}
		fs, enabled, reached := walFault(op)
		// Pebble treats some WAL write failures as fatal and may exit this
		// process before DB.Set returns. The marker records the reached call.
		fs = errorfs.Wrap(fs, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(current errorfs.Op, path string) error {
			if enabled.Load() && current == op && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") && !reached.Load() {
				if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), []byte(mode+" "+path), 0600); err != nil {
					panic(err)
				}
			}
			return nil
		}))
		var faultFS vfs.FS = fs
		if mode == "wal-sync" {
			faultFS = &walSyncFS{FS: vfs.Default, enabled: enabled, mark: os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK")}
		}
		s, err := OpenExistingWithOptions(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_DIR"), chain, Options{FS: faultFS})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, _, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		enabled.Store(true)
		err = s.Commit()
		enabled.Store(false)
		fmt.Printf("child Commit returned: %v\n", err)
		_ = s.Close()
		return
	}
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		op   errorfs.Op
	}{{"wal-write", errorfs.OpFileWrite}, {"wal-sync", errorfs.OpFileSync}} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
			created, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			old, err := created.Committed()
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := created.Close(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			s, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			_, newHash, err := s.Prepare([][]byte{wire})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			mark := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "reached")
			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Second)
			defer cancel()
			cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestM4ReturnedWALWriteAndSyncErrors$")
			cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M4_CHILD="+tc.name, "PLUTO_FAULT_DIR="+dir, "PLUTO_FAULT_MARK="+mark)
			output, childErr := cmd.CombinedOutput()
			if ctx.Err() != nil {
				t.Fatal(ctx.Err())
			}
			marker, err := os.ReadFile(mark)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("target WAL %s not reached: %v child=%v output=%s", tc.name, err, childErr, output)
			}
			outcome := recoveredTransfer(t, dir, old.AppHash(), newHash, sender, receiver)
			t.Logf("M4 simulated I/O %s reached at %q; child_exit=%v output=%q; reopened %s", tc.name, marker, childErr, output, outcome)
		})
	}
}

func TestM11GenesisCreationFaultBoundaries(t *testing.T) {
	genesis, _, sender, receiver := fixture(t)
	if mode := os.Getenv("PLUTO_M11_CHILD"); mode != "" {
		op := errorfs.OpFileWrite
		if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
			op = errorfs.OpFileSync
		}
		killMode := strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill")
		fs, enabled, _ := walFault(op)
		fs = errorfs.Wrap(fs, errorfs.InjectorFunc(func(current errorfs.Op, path string) error {
			if enabled.Load() && current == op && strings.HasSuffix(path, ".log") {
				if err := os.WriteFile(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), []byte(mode+" "+path), 0600); err != nil {
					panic(err)
				}
				if killMode {
					select {}
				}
			}
			return nil
		}))
		var faultFS vfs.FS = fs
		if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
			faultFS = &walSyncFS{FS: vfs.Default, enabled: enabled, mark: os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_MARK"), kill: killMode}
		}
		_, err := createWithOptions(os.Getenv("PLUTO_FAULT_DIR"), genesis, chain, Options{FS: faultFS, BeforeGenesisSync: func() error { enabled.Store(true); return nil }})
		fmt.Printf("child genesis returned: %v\n", err)
		return
	}
	for _, mode := range []string{"before-mkdir", "after-mkdir", "before-record", "wal-write", "wal-sync", "wal-write-kill", "wal-sync-kill", "after-sync"} {
		t.Run(mode, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0")
			var reached atomic.Bool
			opts := Options{}
			switch mode {
			case "before-mkdir":
				opts.BeforeGenesisMkdir = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
			case "after-mkdir":
				opts.AfterGenesisMkdir = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
			case "before-record":
				opts.BeforeGenesisSync = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
			case "after-sync":
				opts.AfterGenesisSync = func() error { reached.Store(true); return errorfs.ErrInjected }
			}
			var createErr error
			if strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-write") || strings.HasPrefix(mode, "wal-sync") {
				mark := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "reached")
				ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Second)
				defer cancel()
				cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, os.Args[0], "-test.run=^TestM11GenesisCreationFaultBoundaries$")
				cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "PLUTO_M11_CHILD="+mode, "PLUTO_FAULT_DIR="+dir, "PLUTO_FAULT_MARK="+mark)
				var output []byte
				var err error
				if strings.HasSuffix(mode, "-kill") {
					var buf bytes.Buffer
					cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &buf, &buf
					if startErr := cmd.Start(); startErr != nil {
						t.Fatal(startErr)
					}
					deadline := time.Now().Add(15 * time.Second)
					for {
						if _, statErr := os.Stat(mark); statErr == nil {
							break
						}
						if time.Now().After(deadline) {
							_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
							_ = cmd.Wait()
							t.Fatal("genesis WAL kill marker not reached")
						}
						time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
					}
					if killErr := cmd.Process.Kill(); killErr != nil {
						t.Fatal(killErr)
					}
					err = cmd.Wait()
					output = buf.Bytes()
				} else {
					output, err = cmd.CombinedOutput()
				}
				if ctx.Err() != nil {
					t.Fatal(ctx.Err())
				}
				if _, statErr := os.Stat(mark); statErr != nil {
					t.Fatalf("genesis %s not reached: %v exit=%v output=%s", mode, statErr, err, output)
				}
				createErr = fmt.Errorf("child exit=%v output=%q", err, output)
				reached.Store(true)
			} else {
				_, createErr = createWithOptions(dir, genesis, chain, opts)
			}
			if !reached.Load() || createErr == nil {
				t.Fatalf("genesis %s target not reached: %v", mode, createErr)
			}
			if mode == "before-mkdir" {
				if _, err := os.Stat(dir); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
					t.Fatalf("directory unexpectedly created: %v", err)
				}
				if _, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain); !errors.Is(err, ErrMissing) {
					t.Fatalf("absent store opened: %v", err)
				}
				if _, err := os.Stat(dir); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
					t.Fatalf("OpenExisting created missing path: %v", err)
				}
				retry, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain)
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("fresh retry failed: %v", err)
				}
				state, err := retry.Committed()
				if err != nil || state.Height() != 0 {
					t.Fatalf("fresh retry state: %v", err)
				}
				t.Logf("M11 before-mkdir reached; no B0 directory; fresh retry A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0", state.AppHash())
				_ = retry.Close()
				return
			}
			if _, err := Create(dir, genesis, chain); !errors.Is(err, ErrExisting) {
				t.Fatalf("incomplete path reset: %v", err)
			}
			opened, err := OpenExisting(dir, chain)
			if mode == "after-sync" && err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("post-sync genesis not recoverable: %v", err)
			}
			if err == nil {
				state, stateErr := opened.Committed()
				if stateErr != nil || state.Height() != 0 || state.Validate() != nil {
					t.Fatalf("post-sync genesis invalid: %v", stateErr)
				}
				a, _ := state.Account(sender)
				_, receiverFound := state.Account(receiver)
				if a.Balance != 100 || a.Nonce != 0 || receiverFound {
					t.Fatal("genesis effects incorrect")
				}
				t.Logf("M11 %s reached; A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0 receiver=absent; identical record recoverable", mode, state.AppHash())
				_ = opened.Close()
				return
			}
			t.Logf("M11 simulated %s reached; create=%v; incomplete store fails closed: %v", mode, createErr, err)
		})
	}
}

package b0store

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
)

func benchmarkGenesis(k int) []byte {
	allocations := make([]b0state.Allocation, k)
	for i := range allocations {
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint32(allocations[i].Address[16:], uint32(i))
		allocations[i].Balance = 1_000_000
	}
	sort.Slice(allocations, func(i, j int) bool { return string(allocations[i].Address[:]) < string(allocations[j].Address[:]) })
	genesis, err := b0state.EncodeGenesis("pluto-b0-v1", allocations)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return genesis
}

func BenchmarkB0SyncSnapshot(b *testing.B) {
	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
		b.Run(fmt.Sprintf("K=%d", k), func(b *testing.B) {
			dir := filepath.Join(b.TempDir(), "store")
			store, err := Create(dir, benchmarkGenesis(k), "pluto-b0-v1")
			if err != nil {
				b.Fatal(err)
			}
			defer store.Close()
			b.ReportAllocs()
			b.ResetTimer()
			for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
				b.StopTimer()
				if _, _, err := store.PrepareTrusted(nil, false); err != nil {
					b.Fatal(err)
				}
				b.StartTimer()
				if err := store.Commit(); err != nil {
					b.Fatal(err)
				}
			}
			b.StopTimer()
			b.ReportMetric(float64(8+26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k+32), "record-bytes/op")
		})
	}
}

// Enable explicitly for raw, independent sync latency samples. The test
// prints every observation; no percentile is inferred from a tiny sample.
func TestB0SyncLatencySamples(t *testing.T) {
	if os.Getenv("PLUTO_B0_SYNC_SAMPLES") != "1" {
		t.Skip("set PLUTO_B0_SYNC_SAMPLES=1 for diagnostic samples")
	}
	for _, k := range []int{1, 64, 4096, 65536} {
		store, err := Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "store"), benchmarkGenesis(k), "pluto-b0-v1")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for i := 0; i < 30; i++ {
			if _, _, err := store.PrepareTrusted(nil, false); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			start := time.Now()
			if err := store.Commit(); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			t.Logf("sync_sample k=%d trial=%d elapsed_ns=%d record_bytes=%d", k, i, time.Since(start).Nanoseconds(), 8+26+len("pluto-b0-v1")+36*k+32)
		}
		if err := store.Close(); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/node/b0.go (3080 bytes; sha256 48036a541c14efb86bb480251ffe8540e4a0dba9a81c1a0fda90c25b963d1d16) ===
package node

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/server"
	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/types"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0app"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
)

// runB0 is an explicit genesis-only profile. It keeps the Phase 02 runtime
// and database path untouched while retaining the same child lifecycle.
func runB0(ctx context.Context, home, cometBinary string, config Config, output io.Writer) error {
	genesis, err := types.GenesisDocFromFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "genesis.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read CometBFT genesis: %w", err)
	}
	if genesis.ChainID != config.ChainID || genesis.InitialHeight != 1 {
		return errors.New("B0 genesis conflicts with Pluto node metadata")
	}
	binary, _, err := b0genesis.Decode(genesis.AppState, config.ChainID)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("decode B0 genesis transport: %w", err)
	}
	digest := sha256.Sum256(binary)
	if hex.EncodeToString(digest[:]) != config.GenesisSHA256 {
		return errors.New("B0 genesis digest conflicts with Pluto node metadata")
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto")); err == nil {
		return errors.New("Phase 02 application database is present; refusing B0 profile")
	} else if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return fmt.Errorf("inspect Phase 02 database: %w", err)
	}
	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
	if _, err := os.Stat(dbDir); errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		for _, name := range []string{"state.db", "blockstore.db", "cs.wal"} {
			if _, cometErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, "data", name)); cometErr == nil {
				return errors.New("B0 database is absent while CometBFT state exists; refusing implicit reset")
			} else if !errors.Is(cometErr, os.ErrNotExist) {
				return fmt.Errorf("inspect CometBFT data %s: %w", name, cometErr)
			}
		}
	} else if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("inspect B0 database: %w", err)
	}
	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, config.ChainID, binary)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer a.Close()
	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", a)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("start B0 ABCI server: %w", err)
	}
	defer srv.Stop()
	cmd := exec.Command(cometBinary, "node", "--home", home)
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = output, output
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("start CometBFT: %w", err)
	}
	result := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { result <- cmd.Wait() }()
	select {
	case err := <-result:
		return fmt.Errorf("CometBFT exited: %w", err)
	case <-ctx.Done():
		if err := cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("signal CometBFT: %w", err)
		}
		select {
		case err := <-result:
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("stop CometBFT: %w", err)
			}
			return nil
		case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			<-result
			return errors.New("CometBFT did not stop within 10 seconds")
		}
	}
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM internal/node/node.go (8222 bytes; sha256 ed22008ab69805c0199a8b0869b0d746f2cefe7a7cafc7e810981939fed5e150) ===
// Package node manages a local CometBFT process and Pluto ABCI socket server.
package node

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/abci/server"
	cfg "github.com/cometbft/cometbft/config"
	"github.com/cometbft/cometbft/types"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/app"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0genesis"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/store"
)

const ProfileB0 = "b0-v2"

type Config struct {
	ChainID       string `json:"chain_id"`
	ABCIAddress   string `json:"abci_address"`
	RPCAddress    string `json:"rpc_address"`
	P2PAddress    string `json:"p2p_address"`
	Profile       string `json:"profile,omitempty"`
	GenesisSHA256 string `json:"genesis_sha256,omitempty"`
}

func address(port int) (string, error) {
	if port < 1 || port > 65535 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid port %d", port)
	}
	return "tcp://127.0.0.1:" + strconv.Itoa(port), nil
}

func Init(home, cometBinary, chainID string, rpcPort, p2pPort, abciPort int, output io.Writer) error {
	return initNode(home, cometBinary, chainID, rpcPort, p2pPort, abciPort, nil, "", "", output)
}

// InitB0 explicitly creates a fresh B0 profile. The source binary is parsed
// before touching the home; CometBFT receives only D-025 JSON/Base64 bytes.
func InitB0(home, cometBinary, chainID string, binaryGenesis []byte, rpcPort, p2pPort, abciPort int, output io.Writer) error {
	transport, err := b0genesis.Encode(binaryGenesis, chainID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	digest := sha256.Sum256(binaryGenesis)
	return initNode(home, cometBinary, chainID, rpcPort, p2pPort, abciPort, transport, ProfileB0, hex.EncodeToString(digest[:]), output)
}

func initNode(home, cometBinary, chainID string, rpcPort, p2pPort, abciPort int, transport []byte, profile, genesisSHA256 string, output io.Writer) error {
	if home == "" || cometBinary == "" {
		return errors.New("home and bundled CometBFT binary are required")
	}
	if _, err := store.Genesis(chainID); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid chain ID: %w", err)
	}
	if rpcPort == p2pPort || rpcPort == abciPort || p2pPort == abciPort {
		return errors.New("RPC, P2P, and ABCI ports must differ")
	}
	rpcAddr, err := address(rpcPort)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	p2pAddr, err := address(p2pPort)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	abciAddr, err := address(abciPort)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(home); err == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("node home already exists: %s", home)
	} else if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return fmt.Errorf("inspect node home: %w", err)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(home, 0700); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("create node home: %w", err)
	}
	cmd := exec.Command(cometBinary, "init", "--home", home)
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = output, output
	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize CometBFT files: %w", err)
	}
	genesisFile := filepath.Join(home, "config", "genesis.json")
	genesis, err := types.GenesisDocFromFile(genesisFile)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read generated genesis: %w", err)
	}
	genesis.ChainID = chainID
	genesis.InitialHeight = 1
	genesis.AppState = transport
	if err := genesis.ValidateAndComplete(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("validate generated genesis: %w", err)
	}
	if err := genesis.SaveAs(genesisFile); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("write generated genesis: %w", err)
	}
	config := cfg.DefaultConfig()
	config.SetRoot(home)
	config.ProxyApp = abciAddr
	config.RPC.ListenAddress = rpcAddr
	config.P2P.ListenAddress = p2pAddr
	config.Consensus.CreateEmptyBlocks = true
	config.Consensus.CreateEmptyBlocksInterval = time.Second
	cfg.WriteConfigFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "config.toml"), config)
	metadata := Config{ChainID: chainID, ABCIAddress: abciAddr, RPCAddress: rpcAddr, P2PAddress: p2pAddr, Profile: profile, GenesisSHA256: genesisSHA256}
	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(metadata, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "pluto.json"), append(data, '\n'), 0600); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("write Pluto node metadata: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func ReadConfig(home string) (Config, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "pluto.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("read Pluto node metadata: %w", err)
	}
	var config Config
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("decode Pluto node metadata: %w", err)
	}
	if _, err := store.Genesis(config.ChainID); err != nil {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid Pluto node metadata: %w", err)
	}
	if config.Profile != "" && config.Profile != ProfileB0 {
		return Config{}, errors.New("unknown Pluto runtime profile")
	}
	if config.Profile == ProfileB0 {
		digest, err := hex.DecodeString(config.GenesisSHA256)
		if err != nil || len(digest) != sha256.Size {
			return Config{}, errors.New("invalid B0 genesis digest in Pluto metadata")
		}
	}
	return config, nil
}

func State(home string) (*store.State, error) {
	config, err := ReadConfig(home)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if config.Profile == ProfileB0 {
		return nil, errors.New("Phase 02 state command is unavailable for B0 profile")
	}
	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto")
	if _, err := os.Stat(dbDir); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("application database is absent: %w", err)
	}
	db, err := store.Open(dbDir)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer db.Close()
	state, err := db.Load()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if state == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("application state has not been initialized")
	}
	return state, nil
}

// Run starts the Pluto socket server before launching the pinned CometBFT
// executable. Cancellation terminates only the child process started here.
func Run(ctx context.Context, home, cometBinary string, output io.Writer) error {
	config, err := ReadConfig(home)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if config.Profile == ProfileB0 {
		return runB0(ctx, home, cometBinary, config, output)
	}
	genesis, err := types.GenesisDocFromFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "genesis.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read CometBFT genesis: %w", err)
	}
	if genesis.ChainID != config.ChainID || genesis.InitialHeight != 1 || len(genesis.AppState) != 0 {
		return errors.New("CometBFT genesis conflicts with Pluto node metadata")
	}
	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto")
	if _, err := os.Stat(dbDir); errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		if _, cometErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, "data", "state.db")); cometErr == nil {
			return errors.New("Pluto database is absent while CometBFT state exists; refusing implicit reset")
		}
	} else if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("inspect Pluto database: %w", err)
	}
	a, err := app.Open(dbDir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer a.Close()
	if a.Committed() == nil {
		if _, cometErr := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, "data", "state.db")); cometErr == nil {
			return errors.New("Pluto committed state is absent while CometBFT state exists")
		}
	}
	if state := a.Committed(); state != nil && state.ChainID != config.ChainID {
		return errors.New("persisted application chain ID conflicts with genesis")
	}
	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", a)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("start ABCI server: %w", err)
	}
	defer srv.Stop()
	cmd := exec.Command(cometBinary, "node", "--home", home)
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = output, output
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("start CometBFT: %w", err)
	}
	result := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { result <- cmd.Wait() }()
	select {
	case err := <-result:
		return fmt.Errorf("CometBFT exited: %w", err)
	case <-ctx.Done():
		if err := cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("signal CometBFT: %w", err)
		}
		select {
		case err := <-result:
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("stop CometBFT: %w", err)
			}
			return nil
		case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			<-result
			return errors.New("CometBFT did not stop within 10 seconds")
		}
	}
}

//go:build integration

package integration

import (
	"bytes"

	"encoding/hex"

const b0GoldenGenesis = "504c55544f47322100000002000b706c75746f2d62302d763100000001b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a0000000000000064"

type capturedB0App struct {
	*b0app.App
	observed chan []byte
}

func (c *capturedB0App) InitChain(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestInitChain) (*abci.ResponseInitChain, error) {
	select {
	case c.observed <- bytes.Clone(req.AppStateBytes):
	default:
	}
	return c.App.InitChain(ctx, req)
}

func b0WaitHeight(t *testing.T, port int, minimum int64) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(35 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		var status statusResponse
		if err := rpcGet(t, port, "/status", &status); err == nil {
			h, _ := strconv.ParseInt(status.Result.SyncInfo.LatestBlockHeight, 10, 64)
			if h >= minimum && status.Result.NodeInfo.Network == "pluto-b0-v1" {
				return
			}
		}
		time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	t.Fatal("B0 node did not reach expected height")
}

func TestPinnedCometGenesisTransportAndB0Restart(t *testing.T) {
	plutod, err := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	comet, err := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0-home")
	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis-v2.bin")
	binary, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, binary, 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
	}
	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("init-b0: %v: %s", err, out)
	}
	genesis, err := types.GenesisDocFromFile(filepath.Join(home, "config", "genesis.json"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	expectedTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(binary, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(genesis.AppState, expectedTransport) {
		t.Fatalf("Comet genesis app_state mismatch: %v", err)
	}
	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
	if err != nil || config.Profile != node.ProfileB0 {
		t.Fatalf("B0 profile: %+v %v", config, err)
	}
	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	capture := &capturedB0App{App: a, observed: make(chan []byte, 1)}
	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", capture)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	logFile, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "comet.log"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = logFile, logFile
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	stop := func() {
		if cmd == nil {
			return
		}
		_ = cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM)
		done := make(chan error, 1)
		go func() { done <- cmd.Wait() }()
		select {
		case err := <-done:
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("stop CometBFT: %v", err)
			}
		case <-time.After(15 * time.Second):
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			<-done
			t.Error("CometBFT stop timeout")
		}
		cmd = nil
	}
	t.Cleanup(stop)
	select {
	case received := <-capture.observed:
		if !bytes.Equal(received, expectedTransport) || bytes.Equal(received, binary) {
			t.Fatalf("actual CometBFT InitChain bytes differ from D-025 JSON transport")
		}
		t.Logf("pinned CometBFT delivered exact JSON/Base64 InitChain bytes: length=%d", len(received))
	case <-time.After(35 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("real CometBFT did not call InitChain")
	}
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
	stop()
	_ = logFile.Close()
	if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	committed, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", binary)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	state, exists, err := committed.Committed()
	if err != nil || !exists || state.Height() < 2 || state.TotalSupply() != 100 || len(state.Accounts()) != 1 || state.Accounts()[0].Balance != 100 {
		t.Fatalf("recovered B0 state: exists=%v height=%d err=%v", exists, state.Height(), err)
	}
	if err := committed.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// At height > 0 the node metadata still detects a changed genesis file,
	// even though the durable state record has no independent genesis digest.
	genesisPath := filepath.Join(home, "config", "genesis.json")
	originalGenesisJSON, err := os.ReadFile(genesisPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	changedBinary := bytes.Clone(binary)
	changedBinary[len(changedBinary)-1]++
	changedTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(changedBinary, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	genesis.AppState = changedTransport
	if err := genesis.SaveAs(genesisPath); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	genesisProbe, genesisProbeErr := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home).CombinedOutput()
	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisPath, originalGenesisJSON, 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if genesisProbeErr == nil || !bytes.Contains(genesisProbe, []byte("genesis digest conflicts")) {
		t.Fatalf("changed genesis was accepted after height zero: err=%v output=%s", genesisProbeErr, genesisProbe)
	}
	// The ordinary CLI start path must recognize the B0 profile on restart.
	logRestart, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "restart.log"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	restart := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = logRestart, logRestart
	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer logRestart.Close()
	restartRunning := true
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if restartRunning {
			_ = restart.Process.Kill()
			_ = restart.Wait()
		}
	})
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, state.Height()+1)
	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	restartRunning = false
	// A missing B0 store beside persisted CometBFT data must fail closed.
	backup := filepath.Join(home, "data", "b0-test-backup")
	if err := os.Rename(dbDir, backup); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	probeOut, probeErr := exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home).CombinedOutput()
	if err := os.Rename(backup, dbDir); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if probeErr == nil || !bytes.Contains(probeOut, []byte("refusing implicit reset")) {
		t.Fatalf("missing B0 store accepted: err=%v output=%s", probeErr, probeOut)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto")); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Fatalf("Phase 02 database was created: %v", err)
	}
	t.Logf("B0 restart preserved genesis balance and refused implicit reset at height >= %d", state.Height()+1)

	// This disposable-home test probes the remaining ambiguity: the B0
	// missing-store guard cannot distinguish total data loss from first startup.
	// Pinned CometBFT may still reject the loss for its own reasons.
	oldData := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "previous-data")
	if err := os.Rename(filepath.Join(home, "data"), oldData); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(home, "data"), 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	probeContext, cancelProbe := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
	defer cancelProbe()
	allLossOutput, allLossErr := exec.CommandContext(probeContext, plutod, "start", "--home", home).CombinedOutput()
	if probeContext.Err() != nil || allLossErr == nil || !bytes.Contains(allLossOutput, []byte("priv_validator_state.json")) {
		t.Fatalf("all-data-loss outcome: context=%v err=%v output=%s", probeContext.Err(), allLossErr, allLossOutput)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(dbDir); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Fatalf("all-data-loss startup created B0 database: %v", err)
	}
	t.Log("all local data lost under retained config: CometBFT refused startup because priv_validator_state.json was missing; no B0 snapshot was created")
}

package integration

import (

type genesisResponseGate struct {
	*b0app.App
	synced  chan struct{}
	release chan struct{}
}

func (g *genesisResponseGate) InitChain(ctx context.Context, req *abci.RequestInitChain) (*abci.ResponseInitChain, error) {
	resp, err := g.App.InitChain(ctx, req)
	if err == nil {
		close(g.synced)
		<-g.release
	}
	return resp, err
}

func TestB0M11RealInitChainResponseLoss(t *testing.T) {
	plutod, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
	comet, _ := filepath.Abs("../../build/cometbft")
	root := t.TempDir()
	home := filepath.Join(root, "home")
	genesisFile := filepath.Join(root, "genesis.bin")
	genesis, _ := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
	}
	args := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, args...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("init: %v %s", err, out)
	}
	config, err := node.ReadConfig(home)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	dbDir := filepath.Join(home, "data", "pluto-b0-v2")
	a, err := b0app.Open(dbDir, "pluto-b0-v1", genesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	gate := &genesisResponseGate{App: a, synced: make(chan struct{}), release: make(chan struct{})}
	srv, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", gate)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	firstLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-first.log"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = firstLog, firstLog
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		if cmd.ProcessState == nil {
			_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
			_ = cmd.Wait()
		}
		_ = srv.Stop()
		_ = a.Close()
		_ = firstLog.Close()
	})
	select {
	case <-gate.synced:
	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("real InitChain did not reach post-sync gate")
	}
	if err := cmd.Process.Kill(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := cmd.Wait(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Comet was not killed")
	}
	close(gate.release)
	if err := srv.Stop(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := a.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_ = firstLog.Close()
	beforeB, beforeC := cometHeights(t, home)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err != nil || !exists || before.Height() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("genesis reopened: %v exists=%v A=%d", err, exists, before.Height())
	}
	accounts := before.Accounts()
	if len(accounts) != 1 || accounts[0].Balance != 100 || accounts[0].Nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("genesis effects=%+v", accounts)
	}
	if beforeB != 0 || beforeC != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("genesis response loss B=%d C=%d A=0", beforeB, beforeC)
	}
	expectedTransport, err := b0genesis.Encode(genesis, "pluto-b0-v1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	capture := &capturedB0App{App: reopened, observed: make(chan []byte, 1)}
	second, err := server.NewServer(config.ABCIAddress, "socket", capture)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := second.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer second.Stop()
	defer reopened.Close()
	restartLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(root, "comet-restart.log"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	restart := exec.Command(comet, "node", "--home", home)
	restart.Stdout, restart.Stderr = restartLog, restartLog
	if err := restart.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)

			_ = restart.Wait()
		}
		_ = restartLog.Close()
	})
	var retried []byte
	select {
	case retried = <-capture.observed:
	case <-time.After(30 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Comet did not retry InitChain")
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(retried, expectedTransport) {
		t.Fatal("retry transport differs from initial genesis")
	}
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
	if err := restart.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := restart.Wait(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	after, _, err := reopened.Committed()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	finalB, finalC := cometHeights(t, home)
	if finalB != finalC || finalC != after.Height() || after.Accounts()[0].Balance != 100 || after.Accounts()[0].Nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("post-retry B=%d C=%d A=%d accounts=%+v", finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.Accounts())
	}
	if artifactDir := os.Getenv("PLUTO_RECOVERY_ARTIFACTS"); artifactDir != "" {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(artifactDir, 0700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for _, name := range []string{"comet-first.log", "comet-restart.log"} {
			raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(root, name))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(artifactDir, "m11-"+name), raw, 0600); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}
	}
	t.Logf("M11 real InitChain response withheld after synced A=0; SIGKILL Comet; before B=%d C=%d A=0 hash=%x sender=100/0; identical JSON/Base64 retry observed; after B=%d C=%d A=%d hash=%x sender=100/0", beforeB, beforeC, before.AppHash(), finalB, finalC, after.Height(), after.AppHash())
}

=== BEGIN VERBATIM test/integration/b0_transaction_test.go (8153 bytes; sha256 79448efdc4966cb24a1f787ee9eec1bd4d0df7dd5d75c3f2489a7243fee1bbc2) ===
//go:build integration

package integration

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"example.com/pluto/internal/b0state"
	"example.com/pluto/internal/tx"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4"
	"github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4/ecdsa"
)

func realNodeSigned(t *testing.T, receiver b0state.Address, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
	return realNodeSignedChain(t, "pluto-b0-v1", receiver, amount, nonce)
}

func realNodeSignedChain(t *testing.T, chain string, receiver b0state.Address, amount, nonce uint64) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	var secret [32]byte
	secret[31] = 1 // Public TEST ONLY scalar; never a validator key.
	key := secp256k1.PrivKeyFromBytes(secret[:])
	record := tx.Transaction{ChainID: chain, Receiver: receiver, Amount: amount, Nonce: nonce}
	copy(record.Sender[:], key.PubKey().SerializeCompressed())
	digest, err := tx.SigningDigest(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sig := ecdsa.Sign(key, digest[:])
	r, s := sig.R(), sig.S()
	r.PutBytes(&record.R)
	s.PutBytes(&record.S)
	wire, err := tx.Encode(record)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return wire
}

type broadcastResult struct {
	Result struct {
		CheckTx struct {
			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
			Log  string `json:"log"`
		} `json:"check_tx"`
		TxResult struct {
			Code uint32 `json:"code"`
			Log  string `json:"log"`
		} `json:"tx_result"`
		Hash   string `json:"hash"`
		Height string `json:"height"`
	} `json:"result"`
	Error any `json:"error"`
}

func rpcGetLong(t *testing.T, port int, path string, target any) error {
	t.Helper()
	client := http.Client{Timeout: 15 * time.Second}
	response, err := client.Get(fmt.Sprintf("http://127.0.0.1:%d%s", port, path))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer response.Body.Close()
	if response.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return fmt.Errorf("RPC HTTP %d", response.StatusCode)
	}
	return json.NewDecoder(response.Body).Decode(target)
}

func TestB0RealTransactionCommitAndRestart(t *testing.T) {
	plutod, err := filepath.Abs("../../build/plutod")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	home := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "b0-home")
	genesisFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "genesis-v2.bin")
	genesis, err := hex.DecodeString(b0GoldenGenesis)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(genesisFile, genesis, 0600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rpc, p2p, abciPort := freePort(t), freePort(t), freePort(t)
	if rpc == p2p || rpc == abciPort || p2p == abciPort {
		t.Skip("ephemeral port collision")
	}
	initArgs := []string{"init-b0", "--home", home, "--chain-id", "pluto-b0-v1", "--genesis-file", genesisFile,
		"--rpc-port", strconv.Itoa(rpc), "--p2p-port", strconv.Itoa(p2p), "--abci-port", strconv.Itoa(abciPort)}
	if out, err := exec.Command(plutod, initArgs...).CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("init B0: %v: %s", err, out)
	}
	var process *exec.Cmd
	start := func(label string) {
		file, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), label+".log"))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		process = exec.Command(plutod, "start", "--home", home)
		process.Stdout, process.Stderr = file, file
		if err := process.Start(); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = file.Close() })
	}
	stop := func() {
		if process == nil {
			return
		}
		_ = process.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGTERM)
		done := make(chan error, 1)
		go func() { done <- process.Wait() }()
		select {
		case err := <-done:
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("stop B0: %v", err)
			}
		case <-time.After(15 * time.Second):
			_ = process.Process.Kill()
			<-done
			t.Error("B0 stop timeout")
		}
		process = nil
	}
	t.Cleanup(stop)
	start("first")
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, 2)
	var sender, receiver b0state.Address
	addrA, _ := hex.DecodeString("b9631c0f8d9d2d9d56bd6151559f3339e14eb36a")
	addrB, _ := hex.DecodeString("e4affb78013f3a929c0e667d632e6fc9fc09732c")
	copy(sender[:], addrA)
	copy(receiver[:], addrB)
	broadcast := func(raw []byte) broadcastResult {
		t.Helper()
		var r broadcastResult
		if err := rpcGetLong(t, rpc, "/broadcast_tx_commit?tx=0x"+hex.EncodeToString(raw), &r); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if r.Error != nil {
			t.Fatalf("broadcast RPC error: %+v", r.Error)
		}
		return r
	}
	query := func(address b0state.Address) (uint64, uint64) {
		t.Helper()
		var response struct {
			Result struct {
				Response struct {
					Code  uint32 `json:"code"`
					Value string `json:"value"`
				} `json:"response"`
			} `json:"result"`
		}
		path := "/abci_query?path=%22b0%2Faccount%2Fv1%22&data=0x" + hex.EncodeToString(address[:])
		if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, path, &response); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		value, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(response.Result.Response.Value)
		if err != nil || response.Result.Response.Code != 0 || len(value) != 17 || value[0] != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("account query: %+v %v", response, err)
		}
		return binary.BigEndian.Uint64(value[1:9]), binary.BigEndian.Uint64(value[9:17])
	}
	first := realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 0)
	bad := []byte{1, 2, 3}
	invalid := broadcast(bad)
	if invalid.Result.CheckTx.Code != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("malformed CheckTx: %+v", invalid)
	}
	badSignature := append([]byte(nil), first...)
	badSignature[len(badSignature)-80] ^= 1
	for _, item := range []struct {
		raw  []byte
		code uint32
	}{
		{realNodeSignedChain(t, "pluto-other-1", receiver, 25, 0), 2},
		{badSignature, 4},
		{realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 25, 1), 6},
		{realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 101, 0), 7},
	} {
		response := broadcast(item.raw)
		if response.Result.CheckTx.Code != item.code {
			t.Fatalf("negative CheckTx code=%d want=%d", response.Result.CheckTx.Code, item.code)
		}
	}
	valid := broadcast(first)
	cometHash := tx.CometHash(first)
	if valid.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || valid.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 || !strings.EqualFold(valid.Result.Hash, hex.EncodeToString(cometHash[:])) {
		t.Fatalf("valid broadcast: %+v", valid)
	}
	height, err := strconv.ParseInt(valid.Result.Height, 10, 64)
	if err != nil || height < 1 {
		t.Fatalf("broadcast height: %+v %v", valid, err)
	}
	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 75 || nonce != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("sender after transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
	}
	if balance, nonce := query(receiver); balance != 25 || nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("receiver after transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
	}
	var block blockResponse
	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, fmt.Sprintf("/block?height=%d", height), &block); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(block.Result.Block.Data.Txs) != 1 || block.Result.Block.Data.Txs[0] != base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(first) {
		t.Fatalf("included transaction bytes: %+v", block)
	}
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, height+1)
	if err := rpcGet(t, rpc, fmt.Sprintf("/block?height=%d", height+1), &block); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	expected, err := b0state.NewState("pluto-b0-v1", height, 100, []b0state.Account{{Address: sender, Balance: 75, Nonce: 1}, {Address: receiver, Balance: 25}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hash := expected.AppHash()
	if !strings.EqualFold(block.Result.Block.Header.AppHash, hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])) {
		t.Fatalf("header H+1 does not contain state hash after H: %s expected %X", block.Result.Block.Header.AppHash, hash)
	}
	t.Logf("transaction H=%d CometHash=%s; header H+1 AppHash=%X; Pluto TxID is separate", height, valid.Result.Hash, hash)
	stop()
	start("restart")
	b0WaitHeight(t, rpc, height+2)
	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 75 || nonce != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("sender after restart: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
	}
	replay := broadcast(first)
	if replay.Result.CheckTx.Code != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("replay accepted: %+v", replay)
	}
	second := broadcast(realNodeSigned(t, receiver, 10, 1))
	if second.Result.CheckTx.Code != 0 || second.Result.TxResult.Code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("post-restart tx: %+v", second)
	}
	if balance, nonce := query(sender); balance != 65 || nonce != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("sender after second transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
	}
	if balance, nonce := query(receiver); balance != 35 || nonce != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("receiver after second transfer: %d/%d", balance, nonce)
	}
}
